# Changelog

All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/).

## [Unreleased]

### Added
- **cp231 — pure composition form + translation scope + the brand's first seo entry (Ian's late-night sweep, 2026-07-18).** (1) BOTH homepages (playground + `../omegajs.dev`) converted from the `data: resolved.*` frontmatter bridge to the spec §5 NATIVE form — frontmatter carries meta only (layout/permalink/composition), every band's words live inside its own `{% section %}` call as block YAML (hero keeps its `demo_html` slot). The A/B proof (live dev-server captures, comment-stripped normalized diff) surfaced exactly ONE rendered delta — and it's a fix: the classy index layout's frontmatter default `cta.primary_button` ("Start free today" → /signup) had been leaking through the bridge into pages that never asked for it; the pure form renders only the page's own words, so the inert generic /signup button dies on both brands (the real brand's terminal demo also now shows its own :4200 instead of forked :4000). The bridge remains the sanctioned mechanism for layout-composed pages; body compositions carry their YAML in the calls. about.md conversion queued on the same proof lane. (2) `translation.exclude` brand policy: 16 app-shell/auth/transactional routes (account, app, dashboard, signin/signup/login/register, forgot/recover/reset/reset-password/change-password, token, join, cancel, refund) excluded in BOTH brands' configs — the skip MECHANISM was already ported from UJM (system routes/folders auto-excluded; `translation.exclude` files+folders); this lands the scope policy: the SEO surface translates, app chrome doesn't. The real brand's 4 now-excluded warm caches pruned; es cold work drops ~34 → ~20 pages. (3) The real brand's first `seo.json5` entry (`omega-stack-guide`, developer-tool template) — service DRY-RUN green (1 repo planned); the REAL run is session-classifier-gated → Ian's one command. (4) D5 fact base for Ian's font review: classy = Inter (UI grotesk) + Newsreader (marketing serif); newsflash = Fraunces (display serif) + Schibsted Grotesk (grotesk).
- **cp230 — every production build emits `dist/CNAME` (UJM's auto-CNAME, ported): both deploy lanes keep the Pages custom domain.** Ian's catch ("we're never creating the CNAME manually right?"): `--direct` wrote the file at deploy time and the manage github service reconciles the Pages SETTING, but the scaffolded CI workflow passes no `cname:` to its gh-pages action and the build emitted nothing — a CI deploy's branch push without the file CLEARS the GitHub Pages custom domain (the classic UJM-era failure; setup.js even listed CNAME generation as a pending UJM port). Now `omega build` writes brand.url's bare host to `dist/CNAME` via the shared `pagesHost()` (SSOT — the `--direct` plan's cname derives from the same helper; its deploy-time write stays as idempotent overlap). No host configured → no file. web 249/0 (pagesHost pins) · corpus-shapes ✓ · cross-stack e2e PASSED (the corpus BACKEND leg is deferred tonight only because the three live dev-fleet emulator suites hold its ports — it was green at cp229 and nothing backend-side changed).
- **cp229 — THE REAL BRAND IS BORN (Ian 2026-07-18: "build the real omegajs site but still use local"): `../omegajs.dev`, local era.** The fork landed EARLY and LOCAL-FIRST as a sibling repo with its own git history (birth `7fb5e95`): the playground's polished content forked 1:1 (git-archive of tracked files; desktop/extension apps, stale es caches, and the vestigial sample hero excluded), identity authored real — id `omega`, name "OMEGA", url omegajs.dev (the apex the test brands' subdomains kept clean), classy/system, website :4200 + backend targets only (desktop/extension are launch-era additions), github org Omega-JS-Stack `shared: false` (the real brand owns the org profile), offline `demo-omega` Firebase until the production project exists. Every `@omega.js/*` dep is a COMMITTED relative `file:` spec into this monorepo (`file:../../../omega/packages/<name>`; brand root `file:../omega/packages/manager`) — versions pre-reset to 1.0.0 (cp228) so first publish flips every spec to `^1.0.0` in one sweep; docs/local-dev.md records the pattern. Real-resource ids scrubbed to launch-gate nulls (analytics/Sentry/Stripe/SendGrid/minted operator ids; gcp + adsense sections absent); template shape-donor ids kept. Birth verification: workspace install → both setups — the forked tree self-healed exactly like any consumer (rules blocks restamped v1.0.0, build.yml regenerated with imagemin cache-restore + conditional Cloudflare purge, still dispatch-only/D13; new .gitattributes / targets-only config stub / SW seed / lockfile scaffolds) → offline asset mint from brandmark.svg (20 logo files + icns + ico + 4 socials + 7 favicons; MrLogo skipped offline) → production build 70 pages / 2.93s, zero playground strings, all-omegajs.dev sitemap. Fresh es translation hit a Claude usage-window stall at 12/34 — the per-string cache is resumable by design; the first 11 pages are committed (`e72fa76`) and `omega translate` + the next deploy ship the rest. Boot probe GREEN: `omega dev` at the brand root — https://localhost:4200 serves `<title>OMEGA</title>` on classy, emulator suite up with 55 seeded personas. The live-publish lane (repo → push → `omega deploy --direct` → github/cloudflare manage services → verify) is prepared for Ian's own terminal — the session's permission layer auto-denies outward writes; PROGRESS Blocked tracks the six steps.
- **cp226 — content pass B (spec §13 step 4): classy speaks generic SaaS; the omega voice belongs to brands.** The sweep of the whole theme layer found exactly four literal framework accents and killed each: the hero's `command: 'npx omega setup'` default is REMOVED outright (no command default — the mono $ button renders only when a brand sets one), the classy index layout's cta `command`/`command_href` lines are removed, the bento code tile's hardcoded `// omega.json5 — one file, every target` comment became the `config_demo.label` arg (generic default `// one config — every target`), and the bento terminal mock's hardcoded `$ omega deploy` session became per-item args (`item.terminal.{command,out,ok}`, generic default `$ npm run deploy`). The playground homepage passes its own omega voice through the new args (`omega deploy`, `// omega.json5 — one file, every target`) — the division proven end-to-end: theme generic, brand promotes. Dev-only /test/* surfaces keep their framework references (they demo the real machinery; purged from production). Two test pins flipped from asserting the omega default falls through to asserting NO command button renders unset. Goldens: zero unexplained on both themes with four declared lanes (8 classy / 2 nf command-button removals, code-label + deploy-cmd swaps, and the showcase's raw-defaults display tracking its own schema). web 247/247 · corpus 1242 · e2e + journey PASSED · playground rebuild proves the omega voice survives via args with zero generic leakage.
- **cp225 — content pass A (spec §13 step 3, freeze lifted by Ian): the playground carries the real OMEGA pitch.** The homepage is now a full composition (`composition: true`): hero with the terminal session as the product shot (a `demo_html` slot — the cp224 mechanism's flagship consumer; the generic SaaS dashboard mock is killed via `frame: enabled: false`), six true-capability bento tiles (config/theming/deploys/brand-ramps/auth/sections), honest stats (4 targets · 1 config · 2 themes · 100% static), and the `npx omega setup` command CTA. Trusted-by, product-demo, showcase, and testimonials are deliberately ABSENT until real logos/videos/quotes exist — no fiction. The about page tells the real story: era-labeled consolidation timeline (five managers → one stack), the project's actual working principles as the values band, facts rail, `gallery: false`. Fork-portable by construction: copy carries `{{ site.brand.name }}` refs so the real brand's name lands at fork time. Proof: `omega build --cached-only` + 11 rendered-content assertions (terminal present, mock absent, single item sets, no fiction bands).
- **cp224 — HTML slots for sections/components (Ian's ruling 2026-07-18): `{% slot name %}` blocks pass finished markup.** When an arg needs real HTML beyond strings/numbers, the call's block body may carry named slot blocks — composing with inline args OR the YAML body (the both-forms error now applies to the YAML remainder only). Slot content renders in the CALLER's scope (site vars, page captures, `uj_*` tags) and reaches the section as a finished-HTML string arg (new schema type `html`), merged OUTERMOST (over defaults ← data ← named) and never re-rendered — it merges after call-site liquification, so literal braces survive (`{% raw %}` code samples intact). Whitespace-only slot = `''` = explicit-empty (kills an html default). Duplicate names/unclosed blocks throw; slot names ride the same schema validation + did-you-mean lane; `{% slot %}` is body-text grammar (not a registered tag) so stray use fails loudly; components speak slots too. Shipped consumers: `marketing/hero` gains `demo_html` (finished markup replacing the typed demo lanes) and `marketing/stats` gains `after` (markup inside the band container after the grid — classy and the newsflash override both serve it). The cp223 audit's parked composite converted as the real-page proof: alternative.html's stats+CTA band now composes the section with its trailing CTA in the after slot. Goldens: every classy production page BYTE-ZERO (the only classy diffs are the showcase's arg tables documenting the new args); newsflash adds the predicted per-alternative band flip to the theme's stats override with the after-CTA verified surviving inside it. Docs: sections.md Slots section + spec §5 amendment. web 246/246 (10 new slot tests) · corpus 1242 · e2e + journey PASSED.
- **cp223 — the extraction close (spec §13 step 2 COMPLETE): the last duplicated band becomes a section call.** A full-page audit of every classy layout for bands still written inline found exactly ONE true duplicate left: alternatives/index's stats band, a byte-level copy of the extracted `marketing/stats` — now `{% section "marketing/stats", items: resolved.stats %}` (the same call the homepage makes). Classy goldens: BYTE-ZERO across all 128 pages (call-site whitespace tuned so the section file's own newlines replace the removed tag lines). Newsflash goldens: exactly one signature-verified explained diff — `/alternatives`' stats band flips from the inline classy grid to the theme's stats override (rule-head "By the numbers" + stroked numerals), the §7/FLIP doctrine reaching the last band newsflash couldn't reskin on that page; 133/134 pages byte-zero. Everything else that looks repeated was audited and STAYS with recorded reasons (divergent per-page composites: alternative.html's stats+CTA, pricing's star-row social proof and FAQ+guarantee panel; distinct idioms: status subscribe; SCSS-pattern-only repeats: the `classy-person` card, rowlist/hairline). The audit settled the boundary doctrine now in docs/sections.md: sections/components take data-only args; context-bound partials (post-card, nav/footer, account-section-header) stay includes; plumbing pages (auth, blog taxonomy, payment, portal) stay layouts.
- **cp222 — auto-generated sample content (spec §8): rolling dates + the gitignored materialized tree.** The shared corpus (11 posts / 4 teammates / 4 updates in `defaults/sample-*`) now generates instead of copying: each file's authored date is a rhythm relative to the corpus epoch (2026-07-18), re-anchored to the build day on every non-production build — post FILENAMES shift (Eleventy `page.date` rides the prefix), `update.date` frontmatter lines rewrite inside the frontmatter block only, dateless team files pass through — so a virgin blog always looks alive, never "6 months stale". URLs never change (slugs are date-free). Anchor precedence: `options.sampleAnchor` > `OMEGA_SAMPLE_ANCHOR` env > today (UTC); the web test harness pins the epoch, making generation an identity transform — the cp222 goldens are BYTE-ZERO against the cp221 baselines on both themes. `omega dev` additionally materializes the generated set under the app's `.omega/sample-content/` with a self-`.gitignore` (`*` — structurally uncommittable regardless of the app's ignore rules), regenerated every boot, removed per collection the moment the consumer owns that collection (and the whole tree when they own all three) — never committed, never mixed with real brand content. Every generated file carries an inert YAML header comment naming itself generated. Mechanism: new `src/sample-content.js` (corpus epoch, anchor resolution, `generateSampleSet`, `reconcileSampleContent`; `hasOwnContent` moved here from engine.js — SSOT), engine injection now generates through it, `commands/dev.js` reconciles at boot with honest log lines. The wizard journey gained a standing step proving LIVE rolling: a wizard-born brand's dev boot materializes 11 posts whose newest lands ~10 days before today, unpinned (it caught the date actually moving: authored 2026-07-08 → rolled 2026-07-09). Tests: 5 new in `sample-content.test.js` (anchor precedence/validation, epoch identity byte-for-byte, +30d uniform shift with order/slug/body preservation, reconcile lifecycle incl. idempotency + step-aside + full removal, and both date lanes rendered through the real engine); the bare-fixture builder was factored into the shared test lib (`buildSite`) instead of a third copy. Proofs: web 236/0, goldens byte-zero (classy 128 / newsflash 134), corpus 1242, cross-stack e2e PASSED, journey PASSED with the new step.
- **cp221 — `[slug].js` wildcard page modules (spec §7): `asset_path` is dead, page assets bind to URLs alone.** The resolver (`resolvePageAsset`, assets.js — the engine's `pageAssets` computed consumes it): exact key first, then the per-page-dir `<key>/index` spelling, then `[name]` wildcard filenames (Next.js convention, Windows-safe) where a wildcard segment matches exactly ONE URL segment, exact beats wildcard, the most-literal wildcard wins (ties lexicographic), and a trailing `/index` on a wildcard key never consumes a URL segment. Internal uses converted in place: `blog/post` → `blog/[slug]` (core js+css, classy css, newsflash js+css — same filename across layers so the union override keeps working), `updates/[update]`, `alternatives/[alternative]`, and the flat legal URLs (`/terms`/`/cookies`/`/privacy` — unreachable by wildcard by design) split into two-line exact entries over new underscore partials (`legal/_document.js`/`_document.scss`; underscore basenames under `pages/` are now shared partials in BOTH languages, never entries). Every `asset_path` frontmatter line died (theme post/taxonomy/legal/app layouts, update + alternative blueprints, the 5 hero-demo pages, the ports fixture), and the write-only `data-asset-path` html attribute died with the mechanism. The §7 dividend: the homepage video-tab logic moved from the dying core `index` page module into `product-demo/section.js` behind DOM-presence init — the band's behavior now works on every page composing it (the showcase pages never had it before); the emptied core index module pair was deleted (the demo pages provably render no tab markup, so nothing regressed). Proofs: web 231/0 (resolver semantics + real-manifest family pins + render-side wildcard emission in the slice suite + dev-mode bracket names surviving esbuild/sass verbatim); goldens **zero unexplained on both themes** (128+134 pages — two explained lanes: the dead attribute leaving every html tag, the product-demo presence attribute arriving on its two showcase pages); corpus 1242 + shapes; cross-stack e2e + wizard journey (real `omega dev` boot) green.
- **cp220 — `omega customize <url>` (spec §8): materialize a default page, own only what you change.** The settled verb lands: no URL lists every customizable default URL with its lane; a URL materializes the page into `src/pages/`, idempotently (existing pages never overwritten; delete the file to return to the default). Two lanes, detected from the resolved theme layout chain — never hardcoded: **composition** (the layout wraps a pure section composition in the new `{% composition %}…{% endcomposition %}` tag — classy's home today, more as extraction continues) prefills the default composition as clean one-liners with `composition: true` — NO copy inlined: the layout chain stays intact, so shared-band words keep flowing from the theme's frontmatter through `resolved.*` (the built page renders "Sarah Johnson" though the consumer file never contains it), and the file lands `.html` so section output never passes through markdown; **shell** (everything else, incl. pages a theme shadows with its own hand-crafted layout — newsflash's home) copies the thin default verbatim for frontmatter-args customization. The wrap is tri-state with byte-parity to the `{{ content | uj_content_format }}` line it replaces: empty page → default composition; body content → composition with content appended BELOW (the legacy contract, preserved verbatim — the slice-suite pin caught the first over-eager replace-always version); body + `composition: true` → the body REPLACES the composition. Proofs: bare-consumer probe content-neutral on 130 pages (same-code control byte-identical); goldens **zero diffs both themes** (classy 128/128, newsflash 134/134); materialize-then-build identity + divergence pins (an added frontmatter arg lands; a deleted one-liner drops exactly that band; everything else untouched) in the new `test/customize.test.js`; web 228/0; corpus 1242 + shapes 7/7; cross-stack e2e + wizard journey green; CLI smoke against the sandbox consumer.
- **cp219 — the auto-generated showcase + schema docs (spec §9): the library documents itself.** `/test/sections` now renders the whole RESOLVED section/component library — an index grouped by kind + category folder, plus one page per entry carrying the args reference generated from its json5 schema, the pretty-printed defaults (raw `{{ site.brand.name }}` tokens shown, never liquified — display strings arrive HTML-escaped from the collector, which is also what keeps the data-cascade walkers off them), and every `demo` variant rendered LIVE through the real tag over the data bridge. New folder → new pages, zero authoring. Development builds only, riding the sample-content gate — production omitting the surface is what keeps PurgeCSS §7 self-trimming meaningful — and collection-excluded, so sitemap.xml/pages.json never carry it. Enablers: the tags accept expression names (`{% section entry.id, data: variant.args %}` — one tag, data-driven composition), and `buildSectionLibrary` exposes the resolved library as the `sectionLibrary` global. The demo sweep landed variants across all 17 demoable entries (shared bands carry generic copy — neutral defaults stay neutral; faq variants namespace their own `dom_id`s; `stage_class` gives components their natural shell); the two lookup-driven news components document args only by design. Entry pages chip the owning layer — overrides read `newsflash`, fallthroughs read `classy`: the cp218 doctrine, visible. Loose-args audit: nothing new warranted (testimonials `superheadline` + story-card `id` remain the two deliberate typeless args). Proofs: goldens **115/115 byte-identical** on BOTH themes with exactly the declared additions (+13 classy, +19 newsflash); web 225/0 (the cross-theme page-count pin evolved to the one sanctioned delta, derived from the collector); corpus 1242 + shapes 7/7; cross-stack e2e + wizard journey green.
- **cp218 — the fallthrough flip: `marketing/cta` goes native + the newsletter rail variant kills the THIRD dead form.** Newsflash override #3: the dark big-read band (`cta-panel` + rings) that repeated inline across the index/about/pricing layouts is now `themes/newsflash/_sections/marketing/cta/` serving classy's exact contract — those three bands compose it, and every fallthrough page (`/download`, `/extension`, `/alternatives` ×2) flips from classy markup to the panel, with the icon keys classy's markup ignores finally rendering and `command` served as the mono $ button. The newsletter-cta override gained `variant: "rail"` — the compact sidebar signup card with no section wrapper (+ `anchor`/`disclaimer` knobs), composed by the index aside — so the rail card that posted to the dead `/email-subscription` route since birth now speaks the managed FormManager dialect through its own §7 root (dead form 3/3 dead). Frontmatter normalized to the shared keys (`cta.description`→`subheadline`, `button`→`primary_button`; pricing's inline `| default:` copy moved into its frontmatter block); no brand page passed the old keys. The standing doctrine settled and documented: a theme overrides a shared id only when the band exists in its own design vocabulary — hero/bento/product-demo/showcase/testimonials/trusted-by/faq deliberately fall through to classy under newsflash (lede/splash serve the hero role); team's charter/join bands stay inline (per-page uniques). Goldens: newsflash exactly the 12 declared pages (6 index-layout: band conversion + rail fix; about-blueprint + pricing conversions; 4 flips), 12/12 containment-proven region-only, 20 more whitespace-run-identical (the override's branch wrapper); classy **115/115 byte-identical**. Web 217/0 (flip + doctrine + rail pins), corpus + sandbox backend 1242, cross-stack e2e + wizard journey PASSED.
- **cp217 — §7 override inheritance + newsflash newsletter override #2: two dead forms die.** An override folder's json5 may now declare `inherit: ['js']` (and/or `'scss'`) — §7 asset lanes it deliberately leaves to the chain, filled from the first lower full entry that has the file (the chain continues past layers lacking it; declaring a lane the folder also ships throws; an unfillable declaration warns and inherits nothing; html/json5 are never inheritable). First consumer: `themes/newsflash/_sections/marketing/newsletter-cta/` — the vermilion slab as a theme-layer override (adds `headline_accent` + the `narrow` post-column knob), with classy's FormManager `section.js` inherited into the bundle and binding through the override's `data-omega-section` root. Landing it fixed TWO live dead forms: newsflash posts carried the plain `action="/email-subscription"` form (the cp209 bug, still live on every Daily Build post page), and the blog index's inline slab spoke the managed dialect but had NO presence-init root — nothing ever bound it (frozen at `initializing`, no-op submit). A third family member found and parked: the homepage rail signup card posts to the same dead route (different markup family — rides the index-band step). Also: story-card's `id` arg schema loosened to typeless (posts carry numeric ids by convention — kills the ~135-line warn flood per newsflash corpus build) and classy newsletter-cta's stale pre-§7 json5 description refreshed. Goldens: newsflash exactly the 20 declared pages (3 blog-index + 17 posts), containment-proven slab-only; classy **115/115 byte-identical** (the collector change provably inert). Web 215/0 (new pins: inherit units incl. chain-skip + contradiction/malformed/unfulfilled, the real-tree inherit pin, the both-forms-fixed build pin), corpus + cross-stack e2e + wizard journey pass.
- **cp216 — the rule-head/lede sweep: every newsflash band head renders through the components.** All 20 remaining `section-head` clusters (about ×4, contact ×2, pricing ×3, team ×2, member ×1, blog index ×2, post ×1, category ×1, tag ×1, plus the three index heads left inline at cp213) and 6 more lede clusters (about ×4, contact + pricing faq — pricing keeps its `| default:` filter args inline per the engine-composed-copy rule) flip to `heading/rule-head` / `heading/lede` calls — composite heads join via `{% capture %}` (pricing one-time, post related), member appends via filter, and the component's LINK slot goes live (index top-stories + the-latest + post related pass link args; the slot was dormant-by-design since cp213). One real find: team/member's whitespace-trimmed captures glued the old head tight to its parent — the component's doc comment now trims its trailing newline (`{% endcomment -%}`) so trimmed-context call sites keep their glued joins (pinned byte-exact). team/index's masthead lede variant (mb-3/mb-4) stays inline — single site, class knobs unearned. Goldens: newsflash **zero content differences** (115 pages); classy **115/115 byte-identical**. Web 210/0, corpus + cross-stack e2e pass.
- **cp215 — `news/story-card` + `news/byline`: the posts-driven tile family, component-in-component proven.** The newsflash story tile (framed `uj_post` art + kicker + h5 + byline) converges its 6 identical instances (index top-stories + more-to-chew-on, blog index grid, category + tag grids, related posts) into one component that NESTS `news/byline` — the first component-inside-component composition; the byline alone also serves the lead-story splash (`p_class` knob), the-latest feed items, and the hero cover story (9 byline sites total). The context-freeness problem posts posed is solved WITHOUT new engine surface: `uj_post`/`uj_member` look up via the injected site adapter (scope-independent — the component owns image + author-name lookups from plain id args), readtime pre-captures at the call site (`{% capture rt %}{% uj_readtime post.content %}{% endcapture %}` — post content never rides args: liquifyDeep has no circularity guard and content strings can carry literal braces), and the kicker passes display-ready with `| default: "" | uj_title_case` (title-casing undefined would render "Undefined" — the default makes absence flow through as suppression). Goldens on the enriched corpus: newsflash **zero content differences** (85 byte-identical + 30 ws-only = exactly every blog surface + index-family page); classy **115/115 byte-identical**. Web 209/0 (+1 lane pin incl. the no-"Undefined" guard), corpus + cross-stack e2e pass.
- **cp214 — posts-rich fixture: 17 posts light every posts-driven band.** The mini fixture grows 2 → 17 posts (15 new, dated 2023 so the existing two stay newest and every content pin naming them survives; only the two feed COUNT pins moved 2 → 17). Corpus 98 → 115 pages both themes (15 post pages + two pagination pages at size 6); zero new taxonomy surfaces. Under newsflash the strict slot dedup is corpus-proven end to end (hero cover = Second post, top = First+17+16, feed = 15..11, most-read = 10..06, more = 05..03) — before this, the feed and more-stories bands rendered NOWHERE (the pricing-catalog lesson applied proactively). Double-capture determinism proven (115/115).
- **cp213 — the newsflash lane opens: FIRST theme-layer section override.** The newsflash theme joins the section library on its own golden lane (theme-override build of the same 98-page corpus — the classy-corpus tests never render newsflash layouts): `themes/newsflash/_sections/marketing/stats/` OVERRIDES the classy base — same `items` contract (number/label/sublabel), newsflash markup, whole-folder resolution so its json5 carries the theme's own head default ("By the numbers" — theme defaults are part of the theme's identity) — plus newsflash-specific sections `marketing/rundown` (numbered newsroom playbook) and `marketing/desks` (coverage cards with category links) and the theme's head components `heading/rule-head` (micro h2 + rule + optional view-all link, arrow built in; 23 sites across 10 layouts eventually) and `heading/lede` (h1-sized h2 + accent + lead; 9 sites). The index composes all three bands (~100 layout lines → 3 calls, nested composition throughout). Deliberately DEFERRED: `marketing/cta`/`marketing/hero` overrides — a same-id override flips every fallthrough page (download, extension, alternatives) to newsflash markup, so that "goes native" step is its own declared change; posts-driven bands (top stories, the latest + rail, more stories) stay inline — the fixture has 1 post, they render nowhere, and their conversion needs a posts-rich lane first. Goldens: newsflash lane **zero content differences** (92 byte-identical + 6 ws-only = exactly the band-rendering pages); classy corpus re-proven **98/98 byte-identical** (the nf layer is invisible to classy builds). New `test/sections-newsflash.test.js` pins the bands AND the two not-flipped contracts (fallthrough /download keeps `classy-cta`; body-called hero resolves to the classy base). Web 209/0, corpus + cross-stack e2e pass.
- **cp212 — `heading/section-head` + NESTED COMPOSITION: 22 h2 clusters converge, including inside sections.** The h2-band head (classy-micro eyebrow + `classy-display--section` h2 with optional em accent + plain sub) becomes the second component — 18 layout sites (about ×2, pricing ×3, download ×3, extension ×3, team, blog/post, alternatives/index ×2, alternative ×3) and, the milestone, 4 SECTION-INTERNAL sites: bento, product-demo, showcase, and faq now compose the component from their own markup — sections render on the same engine, so `{% component %}` inside section markup just works, args evaluating against the section's `{ args }` scope (faq's captured head collapses to one nested call). Fully neutral schema (no defaults at all); the h2 suppresses whole when headline is blank (blog/post's guard semantics win the union). Pricing's `| default:` copy stays INLINE as filter args — `resolved.pricing` is engine-composed from payment.products (C2), so frontmatter injection was the wrong home; alternative's three `| uj_liquify` chains drop. Golden (98 pages): **zero content differences** (81 byte-identical + 17 ws-only) — with sections-demo now body-calling showcase + product-demo `enabled: true` so their default-suppressed heads are actually covered. The catalog-gated pricing bands (one-time, comparison) render nowhere in the corpus — the wave-7 pin builds once with a payment catalog and proves both `| default:` call lines live. Web 207/0, corpus 7/7.
- **cp211 — the FIRST real component: `heading/masthead` converges 17 interior-page heads.** The repeated masthead cluster (classy-micro eyebrow + `classy-display--page` h1 with optional `<em>` accent + sub line) becomes the component tier's first production citizen — pages keep their own band shells (dotgrid/duo/split/masthead wrappers, reveal staggers, breadcrumbs, trailing pickers) and compose the head inside. One LOOSE h1 form absorbs all three legacy accent shapes with no differ change; `sub_class`/`h1_class` knobs carry the per-page class variants (centered `classy-hero__sub mx-auto`, blog's `mb-0` pair, legal's quiet sub); breadcrumb pages (blog taxonomy ×4, alternative) pass no eyebrow and keep their link rows; about's iftruthy/iffalsy eyebrow fallback compresses to a `| default:` filter arg (parseInlineArgs scans values to the next unquoted comma, so filter colons ride through — now covered by a new `/about-blueprint` fixture page since the mini /about is a custom page); pricing's inline `| default:` hero copy moves to frontmatter (cp206 precedent); alternative's explicit `| uj_liquify` drops (call-site liquification, golden-proven on the competitor-name tokens). Golden (98 pages): **zero content differences** — 77 byte-identical, 21 ws-only under one new declared tier (the union imposes one tight sub-`<p>` form; trim scoped to `__sub`-class paragraphs only). Web suite 206/0 (+wave-6 pin), corpus 7/7.
- **cp210 — wave 5 opens: the FAQ band converges (4 of 5 pages).** `marketing/faq`: the Bootstrap-accordion band as one union section — asymmetric duo layout (aside head + accordion) by default, `variant: "center"` for the stacked col-lg-8 shell (alternatives), cta-style conditional accent h2, and a `dom_id` knob (default `faq`) because Bootstrap collapse wires by DOM id — per-page prefixed ids (`contactFaq…`, `downloadFaqAccordion`, …) converge to the default namespace, and a second instance on one page passes its own. contact / download / extension / alternatives/alternative convert to one-line calls (guards stay at callers; alternative's explicit `| uj_liquify` drops — call-site liquification subsumes it, golden-proven on brand tokens). **Pricing's FAQ deliberately stays inline**: its aside embeds the bespoke billing-guarantee object (same ruling as alternative's stats+cta hybrid). Golden 97 pages: 93 byte-identical, 1 ws-only (the center variant), 3 pages differing ONLY in the declared id convergence (proven by targeted rename-then-compare). Fixture: acme-growth gains faqs data so the center variant + liquify drop are actually covered; sections-demo body-calls the band with a custom `dom_id`. Web suite 205 (+wave-5 pin), corpus 7/7.
- **cp209 — §7 PROVEN LIVE: the newsletter section owns its behavior, and blog/post's dead form dies.** The lane's first real consumer: `marketing/newsletter-cta` gains its own `section.js` — the FormManager binding MOVED from `core/js/pages/blog/index.js` (which owned it when only the blog index rendered the form) into the section, element-scoped (`el.querySelector('form')`, so future multi-instance pages bind correctly), with the section root carrying `data-omega-section="marketing/newsletter-cta"`. blog/post CONVERTS to the shared band: its column-narrowed variant — whose plain `action="/email-subscription"` form posted to a page that never existed — is deleted; posts now render the same working band as the index (full-width; flagged in-layout for the skin review if the narrowed look mattered), with post-owned copy riding the data bridge (+ success/error message keys added to its frontmatter block). **Live proof on the playground dev server** (linked monorepo code, both pages): the §7 presence init found the band and bound it — `data-form-state` reached `ready` without any page JS, and a real submit ran the FULL managed lifecycle on the blog index (`Newsletter subscription: proof@…` → tracking interceptors → success toast → `submitted`) AND on a post page — where every submit had been a silent 404 until today. En route: my own layout comment leaked "/email-subscription" into rendered HTML — the new no-dead-dialect pin caught it (comments are output; trimmed). New parked find from the live pass: the band's static success/error alert slots are DEAD markup (FormManager toasts via showNotification; pre-existing on blog/index) — content pass B culls or wires them. Golden: exactly the declared diff set — /blog gains the presence attribute, both post pages swap the band, the cp206 ws-only set unchanged, nothing else moved. Suites: web 204/0 (+pin: posts speak the form-manager dialect, dead dialect gone, attribute present), corpus 7/7.
- **cp208 — the §7 ASSET LANES land: sections own their styles and behavior.** The spec's remaining anatomy promise is real: (1) **CSS lane** — every layer-resolved `section.scss`/`component.scss` compiles into the MAIN sheet through a synthesized `omega:sections` sass module (a custom importer generates the `@use` list in deterministic kind+id order; core main.scss pulls it LAST so section rules sit on top of the theme vocabulary; consumers configuring via `omega:main` inherit the library free; empty library = empty module). PurgeCSS self-trims sections a site never renders — zero config, exactly as specced. (2) **JS lane** — every `section.js` bundles into the main bundle via the generated boot stub: it registers `id → init` and the new `bootSections` runtime (runtime/boot.js) initializes after the main boot, once per present element — `init(el, { manager, options })` for each `[data-omega-section="<id>"]`/`[data-omega-component="<id>"]`; absent sections cost one query; one init's failure never blocks another. Page bundles stay clean (main-stub-only registry — pinned). (3) **Resolution SSOT**: `collectSectionAssets` (sections.js) walks the same layer chain as the tags with whole-folder first-wins — a consumer overriding a section owns its styles/behavior too (pinned: consumer assets beat the theme's for the same id; markup-only entries carry null assets). (4) **Wired everywhere**: static build (`buildSite`) + dev server pass `sectionRoots` (consumer dir + theme layers); dev watch adds consumer `src/_sections`/`_components` (theme layers were already covered); the two direct main.scss test compiles gain the importer. Docs: **docs/sections.md opens** as the landed contract (tags, resolution, bridge, schemas, shared-band rule, asset lanes, update semantics) with CLAUDE.md pointing at it. Suites: web 203/0 (+2: lane test — scss in sheet, registry + presence-init in bundle, page-stub cleanliness; collector precedence/ordering unit), corpus 7/7 (full-site builds through the new module — the empty-library production case). Next: cp209 makes newsletter-cta the proving consumer (its section.js takes the form binding, blog/post's dead form dies).
- **cp207 — extraction wave 4: the newsletter band becomes a section; the dead-form dialect question ANSWERED.** cp206 deliberately skipped blog's two newsletter CTAs because they speak different form dialects — investigation settled it: blog/index's band speaks the CLIENT FORM-MANAGER contract (`data-form-state`, novalidate, alert slots — the same machinery behind auth/contact/feedback/checkout, bound by `core/js/pages/blog/index.js`), while blog/post's plain `action="/email-subscription"` form posts to a page that DOES NOT EXIST — a dead UJM-era relic (silent 404 on submit, today, in production themes). The canonical dialect extracts as `marketing/newsletter-cta` (neutral defaults per the shared-band ruling; `section_class` arg carries blog's `pt-0`), blog/index converts — **byte-identical** (not even whitespace drift; golden re-proven: same 12 ws-only pages as cp206, zero content diffs). blog/post's conversion is PARKED with the finding, deliberately: the section can't own its form binding until the spec §7 section-JS lane exists (DOM-presence init) — converting now would swap a 404-form for an unbound one. §7 is the named next rock, with newsletter-on-post as its proving consumer. Web 201/0 (+1 pin: newsletter copy + JS-contract markup through the section), corpus intact.
- **cp206 — extraction wave 3: the CTA family CONVERGES — eight pages, one section, zero content drift.** The nine near-copies of the ink CTA band flushed out by cp205's survey collapse into `marketing/cta` v2: a UNION contract (optional superheadline micro label, headline with optional `<em>` accent rendered tight when absent, subheadline, primary button, `secondary_button.nudge: true` for the arrow form vs plain, index's mono `command`, `section_class` for `pt-0`-style variants) with **neutral defaults per the §6 shared-band ruling** — the index's copy moves back to its layout frontmatter (as testimonials already did), because with eight callers ANY json5 copy would leak between pages (about's CTA would have grown index's `npx omega setup` command). Converted callers: about (`button`→`primary_button`, dead nested `icon` dropped), pricing (its inline `| default:` markup fallbacks become a real frontmatter `cta:` block), download + extension (secondary gains `nudge: true`), team (the `_team_count == 0` conditional class rides a capture into `section_class`), alternatives index + per-competitor (`description`→`subheadline` harmonized to the classy triad; the `| uj_liquify` filters die — call-site liquification covers competitor-name interpolation natively). ~170 lines of duplicated band markup deleted. **Two catches en route**: the `section_class` guard first emitted `class=""` (empty capture is truthy in Liquid — `!= blank` fixes it; the golden caught it on /team), and the h2 union form initially introduced render-neutral boundary whitespace the differ rightly refused to bless (fixed by the tight no-accent form). **Golden: zero content differences across 97 pages** — 85 byte-identical, the 12 ws-only pages are EXACTLY the converged set (declared whitespace-collapse tier: presence-preserving, h2-boundary-trimmed; inner runs like `</span>npx omega setup` still bind byte-exact). NOT converged, deliberately: blog's two newsletter CTAs (different form dialects — next wave's design question) and alternative's stats+CTA hybrid band (embeds a captured block). Web 200/0 (+1 union-variance build pin), corpus 7/7.
- **cp205 — extraction wave 2: the ENTIRE classy homepage is now composition, proven output-identical.** The seven remaining index bands extract to `themes/classy/_sections/marketing/*` — trusted-by (logo marquee), bento (feature grid), product-demo (video tabs, off-default), showcase (alternating rows, off-default), stats, testimonials, cta — and the index layout body collapses to eight one-line section calls (+ the `{{ content }}` tail). Three shapes beyond the hero precedent: (1) **the bare-array bridge** — `stats:` is a top-level frontmatter ARRAY, so it rides a named arg (`{% section "marketing/stats", items: resolved.stats %}`); a named arg evaluating `undefined` deep-merges as absent (keeps defaults), so absence semantics survive — unit-pinned. (2) **The first SHARED section**: `marketing/testimonials` absorbs the `testimonial-scroll` include (deleted — SSOT) and serves FOUR callers (classy index/pricing/alternatives + neobrutalism index, which resolves it through the layer chain's classy base — smoke-built under the neobrutalism theme to prove it). This produced the **shared-band defaults ruling → spec §6**: multi-page sections keep json5 defaults NEUTRAL (mechanical knobs only — here the lazy-avatar placeholder frame, previously a hardcoded `site.uj.placeholder.src` context read) and every caller passes its own copy over the data bridge, so no page can inherit another page's words; single-caller sections lift their page copy into json5 (hero precedent, applied to the other six). (3) **Context-freeness flushed two more hardcoded reads**: bento's fake-config panel showed `site.brand.name`/`.color` — now `config_demo` args, brand-liquified at the call site. Validation earned its keep AGAIN: the alternatives + neobrutalism layouts carried dead `testimonials.headline`/`headline_accent`/`subheadline` keys (an older band design the include never rendered) — flagged on first build, removed, provably output-neutral. **Proof: 97-page golden diff byte-identical pre/post, zero warnings**; web 199/0 (+2: named-arg-undefined unit + a full-composition build pin asserting every band's defaults render and disabled bands stay suppressed); shape corpus 7/7; neobrutalism smoke green.
- **cp204 — the section machinery is born + the first extraction (classy hero), proven output-identical.** The `{% section %}` and `{% component %}` Liquid tags land in `packages/web/src/sections.js` exactly as the ratified spec draws them: **dual-form** (inline named args with quote-aware parsing and real Liquid value expressions, OR a YAML block body — same dialect as frontmatter; both on one call is an error; discrimination via token-stream peek, sound because YAML bodies never contain section tags), **layer-resolved** (consumer `_sections`/`_components` → active theme → classy base, first match wins — underscore naming amendment: machinery folders match `_layouts`/`_includes`), **schema-validated** (`section.json5` carries description/args/defaults; unknown args warn with did-you-mean via Levenshtein, type mismatches warn, nothing throws), and **context-free** (markup renders against `{ args }` ONLY — proven by a leak test; interpolation happens at the CALL site instead, so defaults like `"Introducing {{ site.brand.name }}"` liquify against the calling page's scope before the section sees them). The reserved `data:` arg deep-spreads between defaults and named args — the bridge that keeps every existing frontmatter `hero.*` override working unchanged (layouts pass `data: resolved.hero`); `deepMerge` moved to `src/merge.js` so the resolved cascade and the section merge share ONE semantics. First extraction: the classy homepage hero (156 lines incl. the demo capture) now lives in `themes/classy/_sections/marketing/hero/` with its full arg contract + defaults in `section.json5` (including the previously-hardcoded frame title, now `frame.title`), and the index layout's hero region is ONE line. Dead `tagline` keys cleaned from the five hero-demo pages + the mini fixture (never rendered — the new validation flagged them immediately, working as designed). **Proof: a 96-page full-site golden diff — every page byte-identical pre/post** (normalized only for per-process artifacts: cache-breaker stamps, buildTime, svg instance counters, RFC dates — each validated by a same-tree control rebuild), including the five hero-demo pages exercising the partial-override bridge deep-merge end-to-end. New fixture page pins the body-call authoring lane (`{% section %}` + YAML in a real build; frame suppressed, defaults riding). Parked from the control run: sitemap/pages.json entry ORDER is nondeterministic across builds (pre-existing Eleventy render-concurrency artifact). Suites: web 197/0 (+14 section pins), shape corpus 7/7.
- **cp203 — spec RATIFIED, all open items ruled (Ian, 2026-07-18).** Same-night ratification: extraction refactor is GO. The three §14 rulings: (1) the customize verb is **`omega customize <url>`**; (2) sample filler (posts/team/updates) stays **SHARED across themes** — and gets a design upgrade: auto-generated with rolling current dates (virgin blogs never look stale) into a gitignored test path, never committed or mixed with real brand content; (3) playground deploys after the fork = **both lanes** — simple direct push as the normal lane (CI runs cost money), full CI-dispatch exercised deliberately once in a while so it stays proven. Spec status flipped to RATIFIED; PROGRESS Now/Next updated.
- **cp202 — the sections/components architecture spec (design night with Ian, 2026-07-18).** A four-hour design conversation consolidated into [plans/omega-sections-spec.md](plans/omega-sections-spec.md) (DRAFT — Ian's ratification pending). The rulings it captures: (1) **Brand topology locked**: the real omegajs.dev site = the FIRST SUB-BRAND of Ian's eventual company umbrella, born at arc close by FORKING the playground's polished content onto fresh infrastructure (fresh repo/Firebase/zone, CI deploys) — never a promotion of the test project; the playground stays in-repo forever as the dedicated live test brand (real Firebase, zero customer risk); CLAUDE.md brand topology amended. (2) **Theme content model**: theme defaults speak their VERTICAL (classy = generic problem→solution SaaS, newsflash = news — the latter already true), promoting OMEGA is a brand's job; recon showed classy is ~90% there already (the gap = omega dev-accents like the `npx omega setup` hero command and omega.json5 code frames, which move to playground overrides in the content passes). (3) **The section/component library replaces frontmatter monoliths**: two tiers (sections = page bands, components = smaller pieces), folder anatomy owning markup+scss+js+json5 schema, nested category ids, consumer-local layer (consumers author their own sections; theme sections stay hidden in the package), YAML block args for structured data (N-item testimonials etc.), frontmatter shrinks to page meta forever, build-time arg validation with did-you-mean warnings, showcase + docs AUTO-GENERATED from schemas. (4) **`asset_path` dies** → `[id].js`/`[id].scss` wildcard page modules (Next.js convention, Windows-safe; exact beats wildcard). (5) **Blueprints/absence model stays** + `omega customize <url>` materializes prefilled compositions; update-semantics truth table written (untouched pages update fully; customized pages keep words, get internals; only schema renames bite, deprecation-governed). (6) **i18n verdict**: the existing post-build translation system (static /{lang}/ copies, committed per-string cache, Claude provider) STAYS the mechanism — raw-liquid and data-level translation rejected; sections add `translate="no"` on code-y components + a future per-locale arg lane. (7) **Cross-framework standardization (Ian: design once, never again)**: @omega.js/web's engine becomes the single HTML factory for every framework's HTML surfaces (desktop/extension pages built by it, target-scoped), one token sheet, one translator in devkit — enabled by the context-free rule (args in → HTML out, no page globals). (8) **Liquid stays this generation** (the layered file-override model depends on interpreted templates; consumers never write Liquid; schemas deliver the typed-props benefit) — sections are the migration unit if omega v2 ever switches engines. Sequencing locked: ratify → extraction (corpus-pinned, freeze-safe) → omega-ify playground → genericize classy → showcase → THE FORK.
- **cp201 — Ian's rulings on the cp199/200 flags: warming kept-but-disabled, preload breadth closed.** (1) The SW cache-warming comes back in **disabled-behind-a-flag** form (Ian: keep it in case we want it later): `CACHE_WARMING_ENABLED = false` at the top of `sw/manager.js` gates `updateCache` (early return) — the machinery (method + `update-cache` message command + build-meta `assets` URLs) stays wired so a single flag flip re-enables it; runtime behavior is identical to the cp200 removal (nothing writes caches, NO fetch handler, fastest SW config). Bundle-proven: flag present and off, no fetch listener, eviction/push/takeover intact, meta assets restored; web 183/0, e2e PASSED. (2) **Preload breadth CLOSED** (Ian: keep whole-site preloads as-is — dashboard-first visits "will never happen"). Extension's write-only background cache stays parked with the same flag-disable treatment planned.
- **cp200 — dead SW cache-warming REMOVED (Ian's page-speed call) + font-preload fall-through.** Ian ruled on the cp199 review flags: page loading speed wins over offline/caching. (1) **The service worker no longer warms any cache**: `updateCache` + the `update-cache` message command are gone — the cache was write-only (no fetch handler ever read it), so every visitor was downloading `/` + the main bundles a second time into dead storage. What remains is exactly what the SW is for: FCM push notifications, the cross-project takeover (skipWaiting/claim + stale-cache eviction — eviction still clears older builds' and legacy workers' leftovers). A header warning now pins the design: NO fetch handler on purpose — browsers skip SW startup entirely for navigations when no fetch listener exists, so this is the fastest possible SW configuration; offline support would tax every request and is deliberately not wanted. The now-orphaned `assets` block left the build meta too (`/build.js`+`/build.json` keep brand/version/timestamp/cacheBreaker/firebase — the client version check and takeover are untouched). Proven by bundling the real SW: no `updateCache`/`addAll`/fetch listener, eviction+push+takeover intact — plus corpus 7/7 and the cross-stack e2e PASSED against the rebuilt worker. (2) **Font preloads fall through the theme layer chain**: the first theme layer WITH a `fonts/` dir wins (was: active layer or nothing), so a consumer-local theme that vendors no faces rides the base theme's preloads; pinned in the tier-2 toy-theme test. (3) Parked: the extension framework's background cache has the same write-only pattern — same treatment on its next pass. Suites: web 183/0, corpus 7/7, backend 1242, e2e PASSED.
- **cp199 — Fable review of cp198: two hardenings, all lanes re-proven.** Fresh-model review of the Opus-built cp198 confirmed the SW closure (no fetch handler; install can't even be bricked — the cache-warming is fire-and-forget with its own catch), the preload mechanics (`crossorigin` correct, hrefs byte-match the `@font-face` URLs so preloads are consumed not double-fetched, `-latin-ext` correctly excluded, `toSiteGlobal` clone keeps the site mutation internal), and the e2e promotion (no corpus double-run; skip knob verified). Two defects fixed: (1) **`permalinkOf` now scans ONLY the leading frontmatter block** — it previously regexed the whole file, so a body line like a fenced code sample `permalink: /terms/` claimed the URL and silently suppressed the framework default at `/terms`; the cp198 `.+?` widening had grown that false-positive surface. Quoted values now win verbatim, unquoted values shed trailing YAML comments, BOM tolerated — +5 pins (15 total). (2) **Font-preload order sorted** — `readdirSync` order is filesystem-dependent (APFS ≠ ext4); emitted HTML is now deterministic across machines. Bookkeeping: cp198's "web 176/0" figure was actually 178/0. Flagged for Ian, no action: whole-site preload breadth (classy fetches Inter 73KB + Newsreader 132KB every page — right for marketing first-paint, wasted on app-shell pages), write-only SW cache-warming, sibling-theme builds shipping classy's unused woff2s. Re-proven: web 183/0, shape corpus 7/7, sandbox backend 1242 passing, cross-stack e2e PASSED, `OMEGA_SKIP_E2E=1` verified.
- **cp198 — parked-findings sweep + FOUC fix + test:e2e promotion.** Three parked findings resolved, the FOUT killed, and the sandbox e2e joins root `npm test`. (1) **FOUC/FOUT eliminated**: font preloads are now theme-aware and automatic — the engine scans the active theme's `fonts/` dir for `*-normal-latin.woff2` faces (the first-paint subset) and emits `<link rel="preload" as="font" crossorigin>` in the head BEFORE the stylesheet, so the browser fetches fonts in parallel with CSS parsing instead of waiting for the cascade to trigger them; both themes pinned (Inter+Newsreader for classy, Fraunces+Schibsted for newsflash), corpus invariant added (all 7 cells assert preloads present). (2) **permalinkOf regex fixed (parked cp155)**: `\S+` couldn't match spaced paths or Liquid-templated permalinks (`/{{ page.lang }}/about/`) — now `.+?` with trailing trim; 10-test suite (spaced, Liquid, paginated, quoted, bare, root, null) — web 176/0. (3) **SW cached-404 (parked cp182) CLOSED**: investigation found NO fetch handler in the service worker at all — it only does cache-warming (`cache.addAll`, which rejects non-ok by spec) and push notifications; the feared cache-serve-404 pattern cannot manifest. Finding removed from PROGRESS. (4) **Sandbox e2e promoted to root `npm test`**: the sequence is now packages → corpus → sandbox e2e → wizard journey (all four tiers); `OMEGA_SKIP_E2E=1` skip knob added (consistent with `OMEGA_SKIP_JOURNEY=1`). Suites: web 176/0 (+12), shape corpus 7/7, sandbox 1242/0, all workspaces green.
- **cp197 — the brand-SHAPE corpus (Ian 2026-07-17: "sure do corpus" — offline, no mocks, no live calls).** `scripts/corpus-shapes.js` opens the `test:corpus` lane (and root `npm test` through it): 7 cells born through the REAL onboard in temp dirs — target combos (web-only · default web+backend derivation · all-four · backend-only · desktop+extension), the theme axis (a real NEWSFLASH build asserting `data-theme-id="newsflash"` alongside classy), and a content cell (a real `_posts` entry the blog page must list). Every cell proves config validity + git birth + exact apps/ scaffold; web cells build through the monorepo's own Eleventy engine (the zero-page-pin lane — no installs) with invariants: branded homepage, active theme, `/blog`, sitemap + robots. Failing cells keep their temp brand for autopsy; new shapes are a `CELLS` row, never a new harness. First run caught its own theme-flip assumption (scaffold seeds `id: "classy"` double-quoted) — 7/7 after. Root scripts rewired: `npm test` = workspaces → corpus (shapes + sandbox backend) → journey — **and the lane rejoining the sequence immediately caught a LATENT RED: the sandbox backend corpus had been silently failing since cp157** (`signup-with-invalid-affiliate-code` 400 "already processed", 16 suite-mates skipped — no full corpus run was recorded between cp157 and now). Root cause: cp157's STATIC/JOURNEY persona split filed EVERY signup-suite actor as STATIC, so the seeder stamped `flags.signupProcessed` + consent on the very accounts those suites exist to sign up — deterministic 400 at the route guard (and the consent-shape tests added later in cp17x were BORN into the aborted suite, never once green). Fixed by the full sweep: all eight signup actors (`referred`/`referred-invalid`/`referred-disposable`, `consent-granted`/`-declined`/`-missing`/`-preserve`, `signup-merge`) moved to JOURNEY_ACCOUNTS — `referrer` stays static (the pre-existing affiliate owner), and the `_test.allow_*` consent sentinels keep their extended-mode provider round-trip role (that test establishes its own state). Corpus green after with the formerly-skipped suite fully running. Bonus catch while revalidating: the monorepo watch had silently died sessions ago — a stale backend dist masked the first fix attempt; watch restarted (root `npm start`), propagation verified live. Docs: testing.md (tier 2 + the corpus section).
- **cp196 — Ian's three calls landed: onboard `git init`s, deploys RECORD themselves, and the testing service learns never-deployed vs deployed-but-down.** (a) **Git birth**: onboard now initializes a repo + makes the first commit when the brand root isn't already inside a work tree (the scaffolded .gitignore is live first, so `.env`/`.omega` can never be captured — pinned; neutral fallback identity when the machine has none; clean skip inside existing repos; the extension package task's source-zip notice era ends). (b) **The deploy record**: new `@omega.js/devkit/deploy-record` (`recordDeploy`/`readDeployRecord`, brand-root-resolved, `deploy.<target>` in `.omega/state.json`) written by every deploy verb's success path — web dispatch AND `--direct`, extension dispatch, desktop release dispatch, backend `firebase deploy`. (c) **The truth table** (Ian's design question answered): live-URL checks (homepage, API health) consult the record — no record + miss → ⚠ "not deployed yet — run `omega deploy` when ready" (a nudge, never an error); no record + HIT → pass + ADOPT the record (fresh clones of deployed brands self-heal); record + miss → the honest failure, retries intact. The journey lane's allowed-error set tightened to EMPTY and proved it live: run green end-to-end with `errors only in {}` — git repo born in-lane (`✓ git repository initialized (first commit made)`) and both nudges in the manage log; web took :4000 straight now that the rehearsal stack is gone (Ian: delete — done, dir + stack). Suites: manager 690/0 (2 git pins + 3 truth-table tests + 2 seeded-record updates), devkit 201/0 + 8/0 (deploy-record units), web 166/0, extension 102/0, desktop 771/0, backend parse-audit 424. Docs: deploys.md (the record), testing.md.
- **cp195 — the wizard journey becomes a STANDING LANE (Ian 2026-07-17: "add that as an easy thing we can run, part of the greater omega test sequence") — and its very first runs caught three more framework bugs, one of them destructive.** The cp194 hand rehearsal is now `npm run test:journey` and the tail of root `npm test` (testing.md tier 2.5): `@omega.js/devkit/test/journey-harness` (spec-driven `{ id, url, targets, expect }` — the corpus hook) births a brand OUTSIDE the monorepo through the real flags-mode onboard in a realpath'd temp dir, links it with one `i local`, runs every framework setup headless, boots `omega dev` (web + backend emulator) and probes the announced URL for a 200 + brand name + theme id, then finishes with a headless creds-scrubbed manage judged from the brand's own `.omega/runs/*.json` (update must succeed; only the allowed `{testing}` error — the live probe of a never-deployed `.invalid` brand — may fail). Preconditions (registry, java) SKIP the lane cleanly offline (`OMEGA_JOURNEY_STRICT=1` to harden); `OMEGA_SKIP_JOURNEY=1` / `OMEGA_JOURNEY_KEEP=1` knobs; failure keeps the temp brand + stage logs in `.temp/journey/`. The catches, each fixed at its layer with pins: (8) **brand-root verbs died outside the monorepo** — omega-bin resolves `@omega.js/manager` FROM the brand root, but nothing declared it (the scaffolded `start: omega-manager` script was equally broken; workspace hoisting masked it in-repo, and the cp194 rehearsal masked it by driving the monorepo's manager bin directly); onboard now scaffolds `@omega.js/manager` into the brand root's devDependencies and the cp194 tree-wide linker carries it with zero new code (`discoverApps` already includes the brand root). (9) **backend setup stamped `engines.node` from the AMBIENT process** (`process.versions.node`) while the framework doctrine pins 22 (its own engines + the `v22/*` .nvmrc lockstep) — my rehearsal shell just happened to sit on 22 via auto-nvm; the harness's clean spawn ran setup under 24 and `omega dev` died on the designed `Manager.init` runtime-mismatch. Setup now derives the stamp from the FRAMEWORK's engines pin (consumer-authored engines still win), 3-test pin group, and the lane itself re-proved it: engines 22 + `.nvmrc v22/*` under an ambient-24 spawn. (10) **manifest-writing setup fixes clobbered npm's work — destructively**: fixes ran `npm i` (disk manifest moves on) while later fixes wrote the BOOT-TIME in-memory snapshot back (the project-scripts fix erased the just-installed firebase-admin/functions); the next setup re-installed, npm's reconcile re-ran the file:-LINKED framework's `prepare` INSIDE THE MONOREPO, raced the watch/running stacks, and rmdir-killed `@omega.js/backend`'s own dist mid-run (repaired by re-prepare). The outside topology is the point — every prior brand lived inside the omega monorepo where root hoisting silently papered over consumer-truth gaps. What the rehearsal caught: (1) **web shipped no `mgr` bin, no `omega-web` self-bin, and NO install command** — the only framework where the scaffold README's own "npx mgr i local inside each app" was impossible; web now mirrors its siblings (bins + `install.js` on devkit local + `i` alias, dispatch-table pin extended). (2) **`linkLocalPackages` died on ANY fresh outside brand**: per-dep `npm install <path>` makes npm resolve the WHOLE workspace tree, so a sibling app's unpublished `@omega.js/backend: *` 404'd the registry before anything linked — rewritten tree-wide: every app's `@omega.js/*` specs flip to `file:` first (placement preserved), then ONE `npm install` materializes the tree; reruns all-skip. (3) **Relative `file:` specs computed through an aliased path DANGLE** (macOS `/var` → `/private/var`: 8 ups through the alias is 8 ups into `/private/Users/...` physically) — caught by the new regression test itself (both standalone repros false-passed by never realpath-ing); specs now compute from REAL paths on both ends. (4) **A wizard-born site 404'd at `/`** — the ~60-page default set had every universal page EXCEPT a homepage; new 4-line `defaults/pages/index.md` rides `blueprint/index` (the playground's own homepage is literally the same 4 lines), so a virgin brand opens on the full branded classy hero; same-URL consumer suppression pinned free by the mini fixture, zero-page-consumer build pinned in slice. (6) **Desktop's synced projectScripts run `npx cross-env` but never DECLARED it** — in-monorepo brands were saved by root hoisting; outside, the manage update-service build died exit 127. cross-env joins desktop's peerDependencies + DEPENDENCY_MAP (setup declares + installs it like electron/gulp), with a new build-suite pin extracting every npx tool from projectScripts and asserting it's a declared peer. Proof chain on the born brand: `i local` links all four frameworks in one pass → all four setups scaffold → brand-root `omega dev` boots web+backend with the FULL N7 story firing live (web 4000→4001; the emulator suite's whole port map bumped via `firebase.resolved.json` beside a surviving foreign emulator; 55 personas seeded) → the homepage renders branded → headless creds-scrubbed manage: 19 services skip cleanly with the 🔑 aggregation (cloud skips on demo-* + no creds by construction), and the update service builds ALL FOUR apps green (extension packaged chromium/firefox/opera). Honest residuals flagged for Ian, not silently changed: the testing service probes the LIVE urls of a never-deployed brand (ENOTFOUND ✗ — should it skip pre-first-deploy?); onboard doesn't `git init` (the extension package task's source-zip step skips without a repo); the emulator inherits machine ADC (Firebase's own "be careful" warning — demo-* keeps everything emulated); backend setup's stats ping is the brand's OWN api (fails soft offline, correctly).
