- **cp190 — NEWSFLASH modernization IV: the CSS fall-through direction call, answered with working code (parked finding RESOLVED).** The audit's asymmetry — layouts fall through per-file but a theme's `_theme.scss` never did, so non-classy themes rendered the shared `classy-*` vocabulary UNSTYLED on fall-through pages (auth, dashboard, admin) — now has a two-lane doctrine, both lanes proven: (1) **Partial/consumer themes** (no Bootstrap of their own) put `@forward 'omega:theme';` atop their `_theme.scss` — the layered importer SELF-SKIPS the requesting file, so the forward lands on classy's `_theme.scss` (the always-present base layer) and emits its whole chain, with the partial theme's own rules landing after to win the cascade; the previously-untested hatch is now PINNED in themes.test.js (new fixture `partial-theme`: classy auth + app vocabulary + token bridge inherited, cascade order asserted — web 165/0). (2) **Full sibling themes** (own Bootstrap config — newsflash) must NOT inherit wholesale (two Bootstraps); instead they import classy's app/auth partials directly as the vocabulary FLOOR — those partials are deliberately TOKEN-PURE (audited: `pages/auth`, `app/panels` = table/statgrid/iconbtn/count, `components/receipt`, `components/badges` = chips/dot-status carry ZERO Sass config references; `layout/shell` only self-contained local constants), so the classes paint through the importing theme's token re-values — which is exactly what cp187's re-value bought: newsflash's bundle now serves the classy floor and /signin renders `.classy-auth` at its 26rem contract wearing EDITORIAL values (cream-ink borders on dark paper, Fraunces headings, Schibsted UI — live-probed). Floor imports land EARLY on purpose — the floor sits UNDER the theme's voice, so later newsflash rules win collisions (classy's `.badge` base restyle being the concrete one). docs/theming.md gains the two-lane section + the standing rule that classy's app/auth vocabulary partials MUST stay token-pure (a Sass config dep there breaks every sibling theme's floor); the PROGRESS parked finding collapses to RESOLVED (Ian can re-litigate direction at reconvene).
- **cp189 — NEWSFLASH modernization III: the masthead is born, and every baseline catch closes.** The cramped-nav mystery resolved honestly: there never WAS a lost masthead include — cp71's "masthead" was newsflash css styling the OLD shared UJM nav markup (`.navbar-wrapper > .navbar.navbar-floating`), which classy-v2's `classy-nav` replaced, leaving the theme's nav css dead against markup it never matched (and the theme README describing chrome that never existed as files). So cp189 BUILDS it: newsflash's first `_includes/frontend/sections/nav.html` — the editorial MASTHEAD (sticky blurred-paper bar closed by a hairline ink rule, serif Fraunces brand, centered uppercase pill links, ink hover fill / accent active pill) on classy's exact nav.json data contract (links + dropdowns, account include reuse, Sign-up action with auth bindings, mobile framed-pill toggler + collapse drawer), scroll state via `data-omega-scroll-watch` (live-proven: attribute flips, shadow paints, settles back); `_navigation.scss` reworked against the markup the theme actually ships (the dead `.navbar-floating` block and the `.text-body` !important arms-race both die — the include keeps its markup utility-free). **Framework catch — the uj_post media contract**: classy's includes honored `post.image` frontmatter (remote hero → lazy-load, `false` → designed no-media panel) while template-kit's `postImagePath` ignored it and always emitted the local blog-assets convention — so every `uj_post image-tag` consumer (all newsflash layouts) 404'd on the remote-hero sample posts; THAT was the "empty homepage bands" baseline catch (broken cover story, story tiles, feed art). The contract now lives in the tag itself, mirroring `memberImagePath`: explicit `post.image` wins (image-tag rides buildImageHtml's external lazy branch), `image: false` renders nothing, convention otherwise — template-kit 43/43 with a 3-way regression pin; the Daily Build home now lazy-loads 9 real heroes with ZERO convention-path 404s, on classy too (its `uj_post` fallback branch gets the same honesty). **Offline mint proven**: the manager assets service ran headless for the Daily Build (`npm start -- --service=assets`) and, because `assets/logo/brandmark.svg` exists (the hand-drawn front-page glyph), skipped the MrLogo API entirely and derived the FULL identity set locally — 20 logo files, macOS/Windows icons, 4 socials, 7 favicons into `.omega/assets` — which the web build's mint bridge served immediately (brandmark.png/webmanifest/favicons all 200; the broken-image masthead brand is now the real minted glyph). Plus: the blog index sheds its hardcoded "The" (`headline: "The {{ brand.name }}"` → brand name alone — "The **The** Daily Build wire" dies, and "OMEGA Playground wire" still reads right). All four cp186 baseline catches are now closed (brandmark ✓, nav ✓, The-The ✓, empty bands ✓). Suites: template-kit 43/0, web 164/0; masthead + images + mint live-verified at :4100 (structural probes; 9/9 images loaded).
- **cp188 — NEWSFLASH modernization II: mechanics onto the shared engine.** The signature ticker stops hand-rolling its loop: base.html authors ONE set of headlines and stamps `data-omega-marquee="50"` — the shared motion engine clones the set for the seamless -50% loop (proven live: 5 authored + 5 engine clones, 26s over a 1314px half-track = the constant ~50px/s contract, hover pause running), and `_ticker.scss` sheds its bespoke keyframes/duplication mechanics while keeping the skin (ink slab, volt hovers, live-dot pulse) plus a reduced-motion escape that turns the static row hand-scrollable and drops the engine's edge mask so no headline fades out. ENGINE improvement while porting: marquee clones now DETAB their focusables (`tabindex=-1` on the clone root + descendants, alongside the existing aria-hidden) — the classy logo wall never carried links, but the ticker's headlines do, and focusable-but-aria-hidden clones are an a11y violation; deliberately NOT `inert`, which would break mouse-clicking the clones (they occupy most of the viewport as the track scrolls) — the exact tabindex pattern the old hand-duplicated markup used, now automated for every interactive marquee. Theme JS shrinks: `masthead-scroll.js` was DEAD CODE (it targeted `.navbar-floating`, which the fall-through classy nav never renders — more evidence for the cp189 chrome recovery) and is deleted with its `_theme.js` wiring; the navigation css's `.scrolled` hook modernizes to the engine's `[data-omega-scrolled='true']` stamp ahead of the cp189 masthead rebuild. Suites: client 110/0, web 164/0. docs/theming.md's marquee row now documents the clone-a11y contract.
- **cp187 — NEWSFLASH modernization I: the token rebase (the parallel `--nf-*` economy retires).** The theme now speaks the classy-v2 contract. `css/base/_root.scss` re-VALUES the shared `--omega-*` sheet with the editorial skin — paper/ink neutrals onto ground/surface/ink/line (line-strong = FULL ink, because hairline frames ARE the identity), the vermilion family as the accent placeholder, a TRUE status amber for `--omega-warn` (volt is a highlight, never a warning — Bootstrap's `$warning: volt` stays for the badge look, two vocabularies both correct in context), editorial radii/motion/pop-shadows onto the shape+motion slots — using the core sheet's exact three-stamp plumbing, computed from the `_config.scss` Sass knobs so consumer overrides keep flowing into runtime tokens. The Bootstrap bridge adopts classy's `:root, :root[data-bs-theme]` pattern (the (0,1,1) arm outranks Bootstrap's compiled dark block by SPECIFICITY, retiring the cp71-era doubled-selector hack) plus newsflash extensions: `--bs-primary`/link colors ride the accent token (the old hand-rolled dark-block primary hack dies too) and computed RGB-triplet mixins keep the translucency utilities honest. Components swept onto the contract (~120 refs across 15 scss files): paper→ground, paper-2→surface-2, ink→ink, line→line, vermilion→accent, accent-interactive(-ink)→accent(-ink), border-color→line-strong, shadow-pop→shadow-2, every `var(--bs-primary)` read→`var(--omega-accent)`; the `--nf-*` namespace survives ONLY for genuinely-editorial concepts (volt, ink panels, hard-offset shadow recipes, grain, electric blue, masthead translucency). Fraunces + Schibsted Grotesk are VENDORED (D5 — 8 variable woff2, latin+latin-ext, both OFL, classy's `_fonts.scss` pattern exactly) re-pointing `--omega-font-ui`/`-serif` (the default "mix" pairing = serif marketing display over grotesk UI is newsflash's voice for free); the Google Fonts CDN link in base.html is dead, and core head.html's fonts.googleapis/gstatic preconnect hints (cp71 relics — no first-party theme CDN-loads anymore) are removed with a pointer at `theme.head.content` for themes that do. Live proof at :4100: light + dark render the full editorial look through the new plumbing (dark = cream ink/frames on deep warm paper, volt vibrant), the computed-style probe shows the DAILY BUILD BRAND RAMP winning the accent slots over the theme placeholder (`--omega-accent: #f35f42`, the dark-lifted ramp of brand `#F03612` — the contract working end-to-end), all four faces load, and the page makes ZERO external requests. Suites: web 164/0 twice (after the theme rewrite and again after the core head change). Found for cp189: the cp71 theme's own serif masthead nav + ink-slab footer includes NEVER survived the monorepo port (the README documented them; the tree ships no `_includes` at all) — the real cause of the cramped-nav baseline catch; the legacy UJM repo still holds them to recover from. Theme README rewritten to current truth (omega.json5 selection, `omega:main` override syntax, the token-contract story, vendored fonts, the missing-chrome note). Ops note: layout/include edits do NOT hot-rebuild the running dev server — bounce it (CSS/Sass edits DO hot-flow).
- **cp186 — The Daily Build is born: the standing second-skin brand (NEWSFLASH arc opens).** Ian's calls (2026-07-17): the playground stays default/classy and untouched — the newsflash testbed is a NEW brand, born by COPY of the playground ("you could also just COPY+PASTE the entire omega-playground and rename it"), with the wizard rehearsal deferred to its own exercise. `apps/newsflash-brand` = **"The Daily Build"** (id `daily-build`, dailybuild.omegajs.dev — the playground's subdomain rule), a fictional dev-news publication wearing the newsflash theme PERMANENTLY so both first-party skins stay alive in real consumers (classy = playground, newsflash = here) instead of newsflash rotting unconsumed like it has since cp71. Birth mechanics: the 61 TRACKED playground files copied (desktop/extension excluded — website+backend only; gitignored real secrets never touched), then the identity fully scrubbed OFFLINE on the sandbox pattern: demo-* Firebase (`demo-daily-build`), every real ITW resource dropped (github/download/domain/gcp/adsense/slapform-chatsy-replyify/certificates sections gone; analytics/monitoring/processor ids nulled), an EDITORIAL placeholder catalog (Reader/Insider/Archive Pass — newspaper-shaped test content for the pricing/checkout surfaces), newsflash vermilion `#F03612` as brand.color, a hand-drawn front-page brandmark glyph, stale playground es-translations deleted, and the backend's fake-credential trio committed sandbox-style (`.firebaserc` + fake `.env` + gitignore re-includes). Side-by-side is real: website pinned `ports.website: 4100` beside classy's 4000; the backend emulator keeps the setup-enforced CLASSIC firebase.json ports and rides N7 bumps. TWO framework catches from the birth itself: (1) **N7's port probe false-positived across brands** — `isPortFree` bind-probed 127.0.0.1 only, but on macOS/BSD wildcard and specific-address listeners COEXIST per port, so the playground https proxy's IPv6 `*:5002` was invisible, the allocator handed 5002 to the second brand's functions emulator, and firebase's own connect-probe refused to boot. `isPortFree` is now a TRIPLE bind-probe (127.0.0.1 + ::1 when present + the wildcard — each surface catches listeners the others can't; measured empirically before writing); config 112/0 with a coexistence regression test; the fixed allocator live-booted the Daily Build stack clean (functions correctly skipped 5002 → 5003, https proxy → 5004). (2) **npm's workspace-root walk-up**: installing from the brand ROOT resolves to the MONOREPO (root workspaces glob `apps/*`) — brand installs must run from inside an app dir (nearest ancestor whose workspaces glob matches), matching the playground's brand-root-hoist footprint. Vendor propagation (cp184) proved itself live: the ports.js fix flowed into backend's vendored dist automatically. Both stacks verified side-by-side (4000 classy ✓ / 4100 `data-theme-id="newsflash"` ✓, all emulators ready on demo-daily-build) and cp71-era baselines captured for the modernization pass (editorial soul intact: ticker, Fraunces hero, volt badges, the config→composer→pricing chain rendering the editorial catalog; catches logged: unminted brandmark, nav spacing, "The The" headline bug, empty homepage bands). CLAUDE.md brand topology gains the third entry; the wizard rehearsal is queued on the board.
- **cp185 — Ian's round-10d: the account rail and content were "different fucking heights".** Exactly right, and his inspector proved it: `nav.col-lg-3` measured 726px on `#security` and 346px on `#connections` — the rail/content row only grew to whichever column happened to be tallest, so on short sections the rail (Sign out + its hairline) dangled below the content card into naked panel, and on long ones the content dwarfed a floating rail; every section had a different skeleton. Fix: ONE skeleton — the grow chain now reaches the row (`container-fluid` → `row flex-grow-1` inside the already-flex `page-content`), so the row fills the panel on every section, and the rail column draws a full-height lane divider (new `hairline-end` utility, joining the hairline family with a logical `border-inline-end` for RTL). Result: rail height == content height == page-content height on EVERY section (563/703/885 proven live at one viewport — always equal), short sections fill the panel bottom instead of stranding widgets, and the lane divider forms a clean T with each section's page-header hairline. Mobile untouched (rail hidden below lg; select row intact). Rides along (Ian's call, same round): the page-owned Sign out leaves the account page entirely — the rail item + its hairline AND the mobile icon button are gone (the topbar avatar menu is the ONE home for signing out; verified live in the dropdown), the mobile row simplifies to the bare select, and the orphaned rail-button css selector is cleaned up. The `.auth-signout-btn` mechanism is client-side event delegation, so removal is contract-safe by construction. Live-proven dark+light+mobile as the admin persona across connections/security/profile; web 164/0 with two new skeleton pins (row fills the panel; rail draws the lane).
- **cp184 — Ian's round-10c: `http://localhost:5002` didn't redirect while 4000 did — three stacked causes, one structural fix.** The surface: plain http on the emulator port got a dropped connection (a bare TLS server's answer), not cp178's 307. The chain underneath: (1) the running emulator predated cp178 (started 19:37, the redirect landed 20:31) — but a restart alone did NOT fix it because (2) backend's CLI runs from dist with a VENDORED devkit copy (`dist/vendor/devkit/local-https.js`, frozen at its last prepare with zero 307s) that only refreshes on a FULL prepare, and (3) nothing ever re-prepares it: each package's `prepare:watch` watches only its OWN src — devkit edits are invisible — and the monorepo watch wasn't even running (the website session ran plain `omega dev`, not `--local`). Web dodged the whole class by serving src directly + frequent restarts; backend/client/desktop/extension were all silently stale. Immediate fix: backend re-prepared + emulator restarted → `http://localhost:5002/…` → 307 to https with the path preserved (IPv4 + IPv6), https still 200. Structural fix: NEW `startVendorPropagation` in `@omega.js/devkit/local` — watch-all now also watches the vendorable shared srcs (devkit/config/account; the list is the vendor tool's own `VENDORABLE_PACKAGES` export, SSOT) and re-runs `npm run prepare` in every watchable package on change, debounced and coalescing (a burst folds into one pass; edits landing mid-pass queue exactly ONE follow-up; a failing prepare is logged and never stops the rest; shutdown closes it with the lock). Deliberately a full prepare, not a vendor-hook re-run: vendor.js derives its module list by scanning dist for `@omega.js/` refs, and an already-rewritten dist has none — a bare re-run would clear `dist/vendor` and break the dist. Testing honest about macOS: FSEvents streams activate asynchronously (a write right after `fs.watch()` can be LOST), so the logic tests drive the documented `poke()` seam deterministically (devkit precedent: `setPromptStreams`) and ONE integration test pokes a nested file until the stream reacts, asserting pass structure. Proof: devkit 195/0 + 8/0 isolated (7 new tests, local.test.js 3× consecutive green), live `npm start` → "Watching 3 shared packages", an identical-content rewrite of a devkit src file → `[shared] devkit changed — re-preparing backend, client, desktop, extension` → all four dists refreshed (vendored mtime bump proven) with the 5002 redirect still live. Docs: local-dev.md (vendor-propagation contract + API row), devkit CLAUDE.md.
- **cp183 — the account page joins the backend dialect (Ian: "it's completely not the same vibe as the rest of the backend").** cp182 moved account INTO the shell but left its interior speaking marketing: giant `classy-display--section` serif section titles, a card-boxed blue-pills rail with oversized icons and an underlined active label, in-body `h5.card-title` cards. Now every admin detail is the account detail: (1) **sections open with the admin page-header anatomy** — icon chip + `page-title` + hairline, stamped INSIDE each hash-driven section via new parameterized include `frontend/sections/account-section-header.html`, so the visible title tracks the active section with zero JS (My profile / Billing and usage / … each wearing its own section icon); (2) **the rail speaks the shell sidebar dialect** — card box gone, `classy-side__*` recipe reused verbatim (SETTINGS micro-label, fa-sm icon column, compact 34px items, active = fill + accent icon, hairline + quiet Sign out item), with `.nav-link` kept purely as the JS hook and the on-surface active/hover stepping one token down (`--omega-surface-2`) since the sidebar's surface-fill is invisible on the surface itself — enabled by scoping the shell's rail-collapse rules under `.omega-shell__sidebar` so in-page rails never squish when the sidebar collapses (collapse-proven live: icon-only sidebar, full-width rail); (3) **every card wears the admin cap** — ~20 cards converted from in-body h5 titles to `card-header` + h6 + fa-md icon, with in-cap actions/status exactly like the admin tables (Active sessions' signout-all, Team's Invite, API key's Reset, push's status chip, and the billing plan cap carrying the LIVE plan-name binding + dot-status chip); (4) **referral stats become a real `classy-statgrid`** (Total referrals / This month — the admin App DNA); (5) **the mobile select row sheds its card** (+ gains an aria-label on the icon-only signout); (6) **the UJM-era `.nav-link.active { text-decoration: underline !important }` hack is DEAD at its home** (`themes/bootstrap/overrides/_links.scss`) — it compiled after every theme (the overrides layer imports last), which is why classy's nav needed a specificity counter-rule (now removed as redundant); every nav component owns its active look (pills fill, tabs border, classy fills), live-proven on the marketing nav, the MCP provider tabs, and the admin firebase collection rail (all have their own active styling). Every JS contract intact (`#account-nav .nav-link`, `data-section`, `.active` toggling, `#mobile-nav-select`, `{id}-nav-item` reveals, `auth-signout-btn`, all wm-bind expressions + section module ids). Live-proven dark+light+mobile as the admin persona: profile/security/billing/referrals/API sections, section switching, statgrid, cap bindings ("Basic plan" + Free chip), collapse behavior. Web 164/0 (scheme test now pins the dialect: classy-side rail, page-title headers, card caps, and NO marketing display voice in the app).
- **cp182 — the signed-in URL scheme: `user : /dashboard :: staff : /admin` (Ian's harmony call, converged 2026-07-16).** One rule, zero exceptions: each audience gets a tree, the root is the app's home, pages live beneath it, and no page is named "dashboard" anymore — the word means the user app. (1) **Account becomes a page IN the user app**: `/account` → `/dashboard/account`, swapping its bespoke cover treatment (fixed brand header + close-✕, the pt-8 spacer dance) for the shared omega-shell (`backend/core/minimal`) — app sidebar (Account active; the default Dashboard nav entry is GONE since no default homepage ships; brands re-add it with their homepage via the sidebar.json override), topbar with breadcrumb + search + avatar, section rail living inside the content card with every contract intact (section ids/hashes, `#account-nav`/`#mobile-nav-select`, all page-JS modules, badges, bindings). (2) **The staff overview serves AT its root**: `/admin` now renders the dashboard (was a redirect to `/admin/dashboard`); every other `/admin/<tool>` unchanged. (3) **Permanent redirects keep every old link alive**: `/account` → `/dashboard/account`, `/admin/dashboard` → `/admin`, and the default `/dashboard` → account (suppressed automatically the day a brand scaffolds its own homepage — cp173's no-default-dashboard spirit preserved). (4) **The redirect machinery got REAL while wiring this**: the new engine never emitted `/assets/js/modules/*.bundle.js` — every redirect page since the UJM port (login/join/cancel shortlinks included) rendered its shell but never actually forwarded (nobody noticed: tests asserted the HTML attribute, not behavior). New module lane in buildAssets (layered, IIFE, fixed URLs, client-importing modules like vert.js excluded to protect the ESM client singleton — parked for the ads lane) + fragment forwarding in redirect.js so `/account#billing` emails land on the billing SECTION, not the profile default. (5) **Repoint sweep**: dropdown (Account/Admin panel), footer, backend+admin sidebar data, auth fallbacks (oauth, session-params, custom-token return), client `redirects.authenticated` default, dev palette, admin users signin-as return, hero-demo fallback, cancel-billing shortlink, backend `auth:token` CLI default, and 14 transactional-email links (`${brandUrl}/account…` → the canonical URL — emails must not depend on a suppressible redirect). Live-proven end-to-end as the admin persona: shell account dark+light+mobile (drawer topbar), section switch, `/account#billing` → redirect → billing active, `/admin` overview + `/admin/dashboard` bounce, dropdown/footer hrefs baked right; suites web 164/0 (+1 module-lane pin, scheme e2e in slice, static/contract/assets pins moved), client 110/0, backend email 11/0.
- **cp181 — Ian's round-10 pair: the halfway-down account page (a REAL engine bug) + Dashboard leaves the default dropdown.** (1) **Why /account floated mid-page**: the cover shell vertically centers by default and the account/checkout layouts ask for top alignment via `theme.main.align: "start"` — but the frontmatter resolver only honored the per-page scope for values containing `page.` refs, so the cover's `align-items-{{ resolved.theme.main.align | default: 'center' }}` template rendered against a scope with NO `resolved` at all (default 'center' always won) and the wrong string was CACHED by raw source for every page. Fix at the root (`frontmatter-liquid.js`): `resolved.`-ref values join `page.`-refs in the per-page branch — rendered uncached against `{ resolved, page }`, left RAW when no scope exists yet (the preprocessor defers them to the engine's resolved pass). TDD'd red→green: new 5-test `frontmatter-liquid.test.js` (site-ref caching, per-page rendering, no cross-page cache poisoning, deferral, copy-on-write renderData) + 2 slice e2e assertions (account main `align-items-start` / signin stays centered; the alternative page's hero accent — `{{ resolved.alternative.competitor.name }}` in layout frontmatter — now renders the ACTUAL competitor instead of empty, which every alternative page had silently lost). The fix un-hides authored-but-never-rendered layout intent: /account now tops out under its fixed header (the existing `pt-8` spacer finally does its job), checkout keeps its one-screen promise (re-proven `overflow: 0` at 390×844, pay button 560/844) and desktop checkout starts where the eye starts. (2) **Dashboard out of the default user dropdown** (Ian: "maybe dashboard should not be in the user dropdown by default?" — right: `/dashboard` 404s by design since cp173): the item is gone from the core account.json defaults — dropdown reads user header → Account → Admin panel (admin-bound) → Sign out; brands that scaffold their own dashboard restore it via the data-file override. Live-proven dark+light, desktop+mobile as the admin persona. Web 162/0 (+6).
- **cp180 — skin-pass finisher: /account joins the language + the last admin corners.** Survey verdict first: every blueprint page now has a classy story — account (cp152/155) and the user dashboard (cp153) were already remade, `/dashboard` 404s BY DESIGN (cp173's no-default-dashboard call → `/account` IS the user home), studio is deliberately its own tool aesthetic, stackblitz is an iframe in the themed shell. What actually remained: (1) **/account de-bootstrapped** — the last user-facing surface wearing stock chrome: the blue nav-pills rail goes quiet (surface-2 active, token hover — new theme page css that also retints list-group surfaces and removes the core css's hover elevation), solid blue/red role badges → classy chips (frontmatter `bg_class`/`bg_color` hexes → `chip_class`; Admin ink, Premium/VIP accent, the rest neutral), the billing status becomes a REAL dot-status pill (STATUS_CONFIG emits classy-status + new `dotClass` through the page object; the layout binds dot + label separately), 2FA/push-permission/session-Current/referral-rank/team-role badges across five section modules → classy chips and dot-status pills (`classy-dot--muted` joins the dot set), both sign-out buttons stop screaming danger-red (quiet outline; the destructive signout-all keeps its outline-danger), Generate-link + keep-account modal buttons → ink adaptive, and the fixed header sheds its shadow. Badge show/hide contract (ids) untouched. (2) **admin/firebase explorer onto the language**: collection-rail counts + JS boolean cells → classy chips, the docs table → `classy-table`, and a new theme page css retints the rail + JSON viewer off bootstrap's unthemed tertiary-bg (the exact "table too dark" disease from round-8). (3) **admin/users/new + calendar touches**: card headers onto the h6+icon idiom, the campaign-time badge → chip, section icons drop their text-info/success tints. Live-proven dark+light as the admin persona: explorer (53-user list + doc view + back), create-user, calendar, account profile/billing. Web 156/0.
- **cp179 — checkout: one screen, nothing hidden (Ian's round-9).** The cp178 fold reversed the right way: the price rows are ALWAYS visible now (no collapse behind the total — Ian: "don't hide the pricing details") and the page still fits one phone screen with the pay button above the fold — proven `overflow: 0` at 390×844, 360×740, AND iPhone-SE 375×667. Where the height came from: the cover shell's `py-5` desktop air reclaimed on phones, the summary panel drops its header row + brandmark on mobile (the page header already carries the brand), receipt rows/discount input/trial note tighten, wallet buttons pair up two-across in a `classy-checkout__paybuttons` grid (card leads full-width), the mobile "Payment" heading goes (the buttons speak), trust foot + help link merge into one quiet page foot shared by both breakpoints (help leaves the sticky column — still exactly ONE `#checkout-help-button`), and an SE-class `max-height: 700px` tier tightens the rhythm again without hiding a single row. Desktop unchanged (the rows were already open; unwrapping the dead collapse leaves identical visuals). Engine hooks verbatim. Web 156/0.
- **cp178 — Ian's round-8 punch list: checkout density, admin polish, a dashboard that works, and http→https.** (1) **Checkout, Shopify-density (Ian's ALFRED ref)**: slim header ("Checkout" left, small brand mark right — the "Almost in." block retired), and on MOBILE everything now collapses by default except the summary + pay button — Plan and Account become one-line rows showing the LIVE bound selection ("Premium · $9.99 monthly" re-binds as you switch), the price breakdown folds behind the total's chevron, and the whole page fits one phone screen; the cp177 regressions restored (trust icons are GREEN again, discount field has rounded ends). (2) **Admin table language**: new `classy-table` (micro-caps header on surface-2, hairline rows on the panel surface — no more black-on-black) + `classy-iconbtn` ghost ⋮ (the white-circle button is dead) + `classy-count`, applied to admin/users, admin/posts, and both dashboard tables; plan/provider/tag badges → classy chips (paid plans accent), verification → dot-status pills (Verified ●ok / Disabled ●danger), user/post cells get icon-chip avatars. (3) **Dashboard, made useful**: Orders (30d) stat joins users/MRR/push; NEW "Needs attention" triage — abandoned carts, subs expiring ≤7d, suspended subs, each a live Firestore count with warn tint + review link; the billing-frequency chart (low signal) replaced by **Signups — last 14 days** (14 aggregation counts); NEW Content card reads the live feed (latest 3 → editor links, post count, New post/All posts); charts retinted from the `--omega-*` tokens (bootstrap-palette hexes gone); quick actions gain Write a post + View live site; cron/backup merge into one Maintenance card. (4) **`http://` now lands somewhere (devkit)**: the local-https proxy front is polyglot — first byte routes TLS vs plain http, and plain http 307s to https on the same host+path, so typing `http://localhost:4000` works (new devkit test; live-proven `curl http://…` → 307 → 200; backend/web get it on their next serve/emulator/dev boot). (5) **Client GET audit (Ian's ask)**: every HTTP GET in the web app reviewed — the only ones left are those a client Firestore read CANNOT replace (admin/users/list = Firebase AUTH join, content/post = GitHub w/ server token, oauth2 authorize = provider secrets, user/sessions + data-request = Admin-SDK/signed URLs, trial-eligibility = payment history the client can't read, status pings = the point); everything else (dashboard stats/charts/attention/trend, recent signups/orders, user docs, user edits) already reads Firestore directly via aggregation counts + queries. Suites: web 156/0, devkit 188/0 + 8/0; browser-proven dark+light, desktop+mobile.
- **cp177 — checkout: the commitment moment (classy-v2).** The deferred hate-list page — the last payment surface still speaking mega-card Bootstrap (card-in-card accordions, blue outline tiles, alert boxes) — recomposed into the classy language: quiet hairline sections on the ground (billing tiles with a real radio glyph, serif tabular prices and the pricing page's accent save-chip · account · payment), a brand row + secure-checkout status up top (cover pages carry no nav — checkout is no longer a dead end), the restored "Change plan" escape (present-but-hidden in the old markup), trial/guarantee as quiet icon-chip notes, and the order summary recomposed as the SAME receipt panel the confirmation page hands back — "Almost <em>in</em>." → "You're in — thank you!" is now a designed pair. Receipt vocabulary promoted to `themes/classy/css/components/_receipt.scss` (checkout is its second consumer; the confirmation page css keeps only the ok-glyph) and the core checkout page css retired — classy page css is the fall-through floor for every theme. EVERY payment-engine hook preserved verbatim (`#checkout-form`, `name="frequency"` radios, `data-payment-method`/`data-action` buttons, `#discount-code`, `#switch-account`, `#checkout-help-button`, dev-panel ids, all `data-wm-bind` expressions) and live-proven end-to-end: frequency switch re-binds cadence/recurring/terms, discount error round-trips the live API, dev panel populates + applies, the one-time layout drops the billing section without an orphan hairline, the error panel renders classy, and a REAL submit rode the test processor through intent → webhook → subscription-active → the https confirmation redirect. TWO real catches ride along: (1) **one-time checkouts always showed a $0.00 summary** — the client money-math read legacy `prices.amount`/`monthly` but the catalog key is `prices.once` (launch-kit proved it live); fixed with a new 4-test `checkout-pricing.test.js` pinning once/legacy/trial/discount math; (2) **backend `getWebsiteUrl` still said `http://localhost:4000` after cp176 made local dev https** — payment redirects (and anything else aiming at the website in dev) died at the TLS proxy with a browser connection error; the scheme now follows `OMEGA_HTTPS_PORT` (the same signal `getApiUrl` uses, one mkcert install drives both sides), pinned in `helpers/environment` (14/14) + documented in environment-detection.md. Suites: web 156/0 (+4), backend boot 11/11, browser-proven light+dark, desktop+mobile.
- **cp175 — the admin CMS seed: /admin/posts + the post editor.** The other half of the admin mandate ("the cms will eventually go here too"): NEW `/admin/posts` — the CMS list view reads the site's OWN JSON feed (`/feeds/posts.json`), so it lists exactly what's LIVE (stat cards, instant client-side filter, per-post Edit / View live / Copy URL) — and NEW `/admin/posts/editor`, one editor with two modes: create (title with slug-follow, description, .jpg hero URL, author, categories/tags, markdown body) → `POST /admin/post` (the existing image download/resize/GitHub-commit pipeline), edit (`?post=<url>`) → `GET /content/post` fills title/body (frontmatter shown read-only, preserved server-side) → `PUT /admin/post`. Admin sidebar gains Content → Posts (All posts / New post). THREE backend catches while wiring it: (1) **the D13 `deploy: false` opt-out was dead on arrival** — neither admin/post schema declared `deploy` and validation strips unknown keys, so the flag never reached dispatch-deploy; both schemas now carry it (default true), a 4-test schema regression suite pins the contract, and the editor's "Dispatch a site build" switch is its first real consumer; (2) **the framework defaults aimed a real GH_TOKEN at a placeholder repo** — `templates/config/omega.json5` shipped `github.repo_website: 'username/omega'` as a RUNTIME default, silently defeating the routes' "not configured" guard for every unconfigured brand; the default is now deliberately empty (the guard fires honestly) and the playground config carries the TRUE repo (`Omega-JS-Stack/omega-playground`); (3) **the MCP count tests had been stale through two checkpoints** — `test/mcp` asserted 25 tools (test names still said 19) so cp173/cp174's tool additions would have failed the suite had it run; `roles.js` now derives expectations from the tools.js SSOT and `utils.js` pins the true role distribution with a bucket-sum guard. Plus a new `get_post` MCP tool (public, read-only) → **28 tools (24 admin / 2 user / 2 public)** — Claude can now do the whole CMS story: feed → `get_post` → `update_post`. Live-proven over HTTPS as the admin persona: list renders 11 sample posts with stats + filter ("design" → 3 of 11), create submit rides the REAL pipeline (schema→auth→config→GitHub) to the honest branch-404 — the playground source repo's `main` is born at arc close (Ian's queued task); deploy switch OFF so the gated Actions dispatch stays impossible — and edit mode loads-and-fails clean on a feed post that isn't in the repo yet. Backend 55/55 (mcp + deploy-flag + users routes) + boot 11/11, web 152/0.
- **cp176 — HTTPS everywhere (`@omega.js/devkit/local-https`).** Ian: "website needs https too! everything needs https. recycle the logic as usual in the right place." The legacy BEM mkcert flow is now a shared devkit module — `ensureLocalHttpsCerts` (reuses valid pairs in `.temp/certs/`, wipes + regenerates expired/foreign-CA ones, null when mkcert is absent) + `startLocalHttpsProxy` (TLS terminator forwarding to an internal plain-http port with `x-forwarded-proto/host`, PLUS WebSocket-upgrade tunneling so dev-server live-reload survives — the one addition over legacy). Three consumers: backend `omega serve` (~170 private cert/proxy lines deleted — now delegates), backend `omega emulator` (NEW — interactive runs default https ON with `--no-https` opt-out: hosting moves internal, the proxy owns the public port, `firebase.resolved.json` always materializes, the orphan sweep excludes the in-process proxy, the ports file publishes `https` + `hosting`; the `omega test` auto-start stays plain http), and web `omega dev` (NEW — `https://localhost:4000` by default, Eleventy on an internal port behind the proxy; `applyDevSiteUrl` grew the https param so site.url + every canonical/og/absolute surface follows the scheme). This is what makes `@omega.js/client`'s `getApiUrl` dev contract (`https` ports-key → `https://localhost:5002`) cohere — the admin directory's authorizedFetch was the forcing function. devkit: new 6-test suite (cert reuse, live proxy round-trip, WS tunnel; mkcert-gated tests skip cleanly) + 187/0, web 152/0 incl. the applyDevSiteUrl scheme assertion; live-proven emulator `https://localhost:5002` → 200 and website `https://localhost:4000` → 200 with the https canonical.
- **cp174 — /admin/users becomes a real directory (admin arc pt 2 opens).** Per Ian's "go CRAZY on admin… make it SUPER USEFUL": the admin users page stops being a Firestore-only shell — it now loads the live `GET /admin/users/list` directory immediately (newest-first) via authorizedFetch, wearing the auth-JOIN columns client SDKs can never read (provider badges, email verification, disabled flag, last sign-in) with email/uid prefix search, cursor pagination (25/page + "Load more" riding `nextCursor`), and View/Edit lazy-fetching the full Firestore doc on demand; sign-in-as / edit-save / delete preserved. NEW backend surface: `POST /admin/users/disable` — auth-level disable/enable via `admin.auth().updateUser` PLUS refresh-token revocation on disable (live sessions actually end), self-disable guard (400), unknown uid → 404; schema twin + `set_user_disabled` MCP tool (admin, idempotent-annotated). Docs true-up: `docs/mcp.md` was still advertising 25 tools — cp173's `list_users` had never made the table — counts + rows now reflect all 27 (24 admin / 2 user / 1 public). Proven live in Chrome over HTTPS as the admin persona: 53 users render, pagination 25→50 (no cursor repeats), search `_test.refunded` → 1 match, disable → Disabled badge + Enable-user menu flip → re-enable clean (persona re-verified `disabled:false` via the API). Backend routes 6/6 (users-list + users-disable), web 152/0.
- **cp173 — the dashboard decision + the admin data engine.** Per Ian's lean ("part of me thinks we should just not ship a default user dashboard"): the DEFAULT user dashboard is GONE — no brand would ever launch the fake-revenue placeholder; `/dashboard` 404s, authenticated users land on `/account` (already the client default), and every `/dashboard` fallback repoints (`auth:token` CLI, custom-token + OAuth handlers, hero-demo redirect, app-sidebar brand href, dev-palette link → `/admin/dashboard`). The dashboard BLUEPRINT survives for brands that scaffold their own. Backend: new `GET /admin/users/list` — the paginated user directory with the Firebase Auth JOIN client SDKs can never read (providers, disabled flag, email verification, last sign-in), email/uid prefix search (U+F8FF range scan), and cursor pagination; schema twin + `list_users` MCP tool (admin role, read-only annotated) so Claude can answer "who signed up this week" directly. Route proven against the real emulator (3-test suite: join fields, prefix search, cursor never repeats; the project-mismatch guard correctly refused to hijack the playground emulator mid-run). Web 152/0.
- **cp172 — consistency sweep: every bento header gets its icon, every alert goes quiet.** Ian's "some have icons next to the headers, some dont": only the default-type bento tile carried an icon — all five now wear the chip (sliders/half-stroke/terminal/palette/shield). Bigger catch: classy had ZERO alert theming — nine pages rendered raw Bootstrap tinted slabs; new `_alerts.scss` turns every alert into a quiet hairline note (surface bg, 3px level edge, level-colored icon, ink text) in one move. Web 152/0.

- **cp169 — REIMAGINE: the 404 typographic moment, the status ops console, the updates timeline.** /404 sheds the card-in-the-void for a full-bleed moment on the living dotfield: giant serif 404 with the accent-italic zero, "Lost, elegantly.", the real-URL chip (JS contract kept), ink+ghost actions, quiet trail links, full chrome. /status makes the state the headline — serif "Every system, accounted for" + a quiet live strip (glyph + serif state + refresh pulse; the JS's bg-* class contract preserved), ONE ops card holding every service's 90-day bars, incidents/maintenance as a duo with dot-and-sentence empty states, a split subscribe band, and a build manifest card. /updates becomes a version timeline: hairline spine, accent latest-node, mono v-pills, serif titles, highlight chips, hover-lift entry cards. - **cp168 — legal trio reimagined: the editorial document treatment.** /terms, /privacy, /cookies shed the bare masthead-plus-prose shell for a real document system: sober serif header with a legal-hub switcher (all three policies one hop away, current one inked), a sticky "On this page" rail built from the document's own h2/h3 sections (stable slug anchors, nested subsections, reading-position highlight via IntersectionObserver), the text at a 46rem measure with serif section headings, an effective-date meta band (was an ugly h3 polluting the ToC), a questions-email + print rail footer, and print styles that strip the chrome. De-ITW while in there: the blueprints hardcoded "ITW Creative Works" as the parent company — new optional `brand.company` schema field ("legal entity; falls back to brand.name"), templated into the docs + the document footer; playground config sets it (true for playground). Config 111/0, web 152/0, browser-verified.
- **cp167 — REIMAGINE arc opens: /download + /extension rebuilt from zero (manus-grade).** Ian's mandate (2026-07-16): "I SAID REIMAGINE THEM" — these are original compositions, not re-dressed UJM skeletons. **/download**: the page picks YOUR button — detection now populates a hero CTA ("Download for macOS" + platform mark) instead of auto-selecting a tab; the tab rail + reveal-dance panes are GONE, replaced by three always-visible platform cards (mark chip, requirements, stacked install buttons) and a split mobile band (store badges via the real `apple-app-store`/`google-play-store` brandmarks when configured; the notify-me email flow when not); the install-walkthrough modal survives verbatim. **/extension**: same family DNA — detected-browser hero ("Add to Chrome" + real mark), six browser cards wearing their REAL brandmarks (chrome/firefox/edge/opera/safari/brave), "Coming soon" as a designed status (not a warning banner), and a 3-step pin ritual band. Mechanics: cards carry `data-download-card` (the bare `[data-platform]` also lives on `<html>`/forms/modal panes — the first hero cut famously read "Download for DOES NOT" off the IE alert); both page JS modules rewired to the card contract; `.classy-dl-*` is a shared marketing partial; playground config gains real `download.{mac,windows,linux}` release-hub URLs (ios/android absent on purpose to demo notify-me). Copy rewritten page-wide. Web 152/0; both pages browser-verified dark+light.

Five new sample posts (boring deploys, naming, pricing-page-as-product, transactional email, docs-with-the-diff — all with topical heroes) take the set to 11 at pagination size 6, so /blog/page/2 exists and the pager renders; the two deliberate image-less posts are the oldest and now prove the no-media panel on page 2. Two-part fix: (1) `omega dev` now overrides `site.url` to the resolved local origin (`http://localhost:<port>`, legacy `_config_dev.yml` parity) so EVERY absolute-URL surface — canonicals, og:url, uj_external, absolute_url, redirect pages — resolves against the server actually running (web +1 test, live-proven: zero live-origin hrefs on every page). Modernizations while in there: the Google button's ITW-CDN logo `<img>` → inlined FA brand icon; consent-group inline styles → `.classy-auth__consent` classes; reset button gains the shared hover-nudge; dead `wakeupServer` dropped. - **cp161 — the stub-icon purge, loud missing icons, and the ghost hover fix.** Root cause of Ian's scattered missing-icon reports: `core/icons/solid/` shipped TWELVE placeholder SVGs (`M1 1 L256 256Z`…, Phase-B1 skeleton leftovers) that sat FIRST in the icon chain and silently shadowed the real Font Awesome set — `users` (Explore dropdown), `eye` (the password toggle "append icon"), heart/rocket/bolt/star/globe/shield/clock/music/tv/chart-line, everywhere, in both the build-time inliner and the runtime `/assets/fa/` emit. Deleted; the npm floor serves the real icons. Missing icons are now LOUD: the build-time fallback stamps `data-omega-icon-missing="<slug>"` (uj_logo too, which also gains the once-per-name build warning) and a dev-only browser audit console.errors every stamped miss (pinned in template-kit). Also fixed the dark-mode button disease: the shared adaptive layer loads after the theme and re-litigates the ghost's vars at equal specificity — outline buttons inverted to paper on hover while the theme's unconditional `color` froze the text light (unreadable). The classy ghost is now double-scoped (`.btn.btn-outline-adaptive` + dark-scoped twin), unconditional color/background dropped — hover is ink-on-surface-2 in both modes, proven painted.

Card + buttons share the stops token.

The blog card is ONE include (`frontend/components/post-card.html`) shared by the index, related-posts, category, and tag grids — four hand-copied card blocks deleted.

- **cp145 — Draft-1 feedback round: signin fixed, legacy URL shape restored, the service worker is BACK, real webfonts.** Ian's first review of Draft 1 surfaced four systemic gaps; all four are closed:
  - **Signin/signup/reset actually work now**: `data-page-path` carries Eleventy's trailing slash (`/signin/`) but the auth lib compared strictly against `/signin` — the form initializer matched NOTHING, `formManager` stayed null, and every auth page sat dead in "initializing" (the real cause of the parked "spinners until Firebase resolves" note). Path matching is segment-based (locale-prefixed `/es/signin` included) with a warn-and-bail guard.
  - **Legacy URL shape restored framework-wide (Ian directive)**: pages write flat `.html` files (`signin.html`) and `page.url` is slash-free (`/signin`) — exactly legacy UJM/Jekyll. The bake-off port's "pretty URLs" branch was the drift. Real-extension whitelist (dotted slugs `/updates/v1.0.0` get their `.html`), `addUrlTransform` keeps `page.url` extensionless, blog pagination/taxonomy spell their shapes explicitly, `devCleanUrls` middleware serves `/signin` and stray `/signin/` from the flat file (legacy serve.js contract; GitHub Pages does the same natively), and `permalinkOf` normalizes to the slash-free canonical. Latent slash-free comparisons (`page.url == "/contact"` JSON-LD, urlmatches nav) now match again.
  - **The service worker is ported (Ian: "we need it in dev — things USE it, like firebase messaging")**: every build — dev included — emits `/service-worker.js` (esbuild iife of the consumer's scaffolded `src/service-worker.js` or the packaged default) + `/build.js`/`/build.json` config transport (brand, environment, version, `timestamp` for the client version check, main-bundle URLs, firebase config). The worker is the master-service-worker successor: FCM background messages + notification clicks (push's `getToken` rides this registration), the `update-cache` command, `skipWaiting`+`claim`. **One localhost port, many projects, no ghosts**: same-scope registration replaces the previous project's worker (verified live — the squatting Somiibo worker was replaced and its caches evicted by the brand+build cache-name rule).
  - **Classy wears real type (D5)**: Inter (UI grotesk) + Newsreader (marketing serif; the italic rotator finally has real italics) — variable woff2, latin+latin-ext, OFL — vendored in `themes/classy/fonts/` via a new layered `fonts/` → `/assets/fonts` convention. On Chrome the old `ui-serif` stack fell all the way to Georgia (the "ugly" verdict was correct). Fixed a latent cascade bug on the way: core main.scss `@forward`'d the theme BEFORE the token sheet, so theme-level token overrides could never win.
  - **Icon fix**: `fa-2xs` (real FA size) + legacy house sizes `fa-md`/`fa-3xl` now parse as modifiers in the shared icon-core — they were parsed as icon NAMES, 404ing and wiping build-time-inlined icons (nav toggler, footer hand-wave, pricing checks) off the live page.

Names stayed contract-stable; shell geometry → 264/68/60.

  - **The reusable motion library (Ian's ask)**: `@omega.js/client/modules/motion.js` (icon-renderer-pattern engine — reveals + stagger, count-ups, word rotators, marquee duplication, scroll watchers) + `core/css/motion/_index.scss` (hover lift/raise/dim, pressable, nudge, float, caret, marquee, rotator styles). No-JS pages render fully visible (inline `data-omega-motion` gate), `prefers-reduced-motion` gets static final states, and the PurgeCSS safelist widened to the whole `omega-` namespace. Client suite +7 tests (103).
  - **classy gut renovation**: new `_config.scss` (full `!default` consumer surface forwarded into Bootstrap), Bootstrap-CSS-var token bridge (`css/base/_root.scss` — dark mode, brand ramps, and consumer token overrides restyle every Bootstrap component with zero recompiles), ink-primary buttons (`.btn-adaptive` from ink/paper neutrals), hairline ghosts, `.btn-cmd` mono command CTA, hairline cards/forms/badges/dropdowns, glassy scroll-nav on the shared `[data-omega-scroll-watch]` mechanic, footer reskin. All gradient-era partials and dead JS (navbar-scroll, infinite-scroll) deleted; legacy gradient class names neutralized to flat token paint pending each page's remake; dead `core/css/bundles/` removed.

Signin recut as the `.classy-auth` card (all auth hooks preserved).

Backend sections get core JSON defaults; admin JSON gains the selector demo. Rail collapse, mobile drawer + scrim, and iframe-embed hiding all verified in-browser.

- **cp143 — classy v2 DIRECTION LOCKED (Ian GO 2026-07-16): the skin arc opens.** Ian's theme notes (2026-07-15) + two interactive comp rounds (artifacts, browser-verified light+dark) converged the flagship direction: classy keeps its NAME and gets a full gut renovation — ink-on-paper neutrals (warm paper light / de-blued charcoal dark, ElevenLabs reference), **ZERO gradients** (monotone chart fades only), ink primary buttons, restrained single-hue accent driven by `brand.color`, Manus-split typography (serif marketing display / sans app UI) with consumer presets, gray-canvas sidebar + one big rounded content card, nested sidebar groups, topbar ⌘K search, merged hairline stat card, sidebar ad slot. Spec + both self-contained comps vendored at `plans/classy-v2/` (DIRECTION.md = the build contract; comps carry a live view/theme/brand/type/density dock). Consumer contract pinned: tier-1 customization from the app's own main.scss (`brand.color` ramp, `!default` Sass knobs, `--omega-*` token overrides) vs tier-2 full theme via `_template`; stable-API line = token names + config vars + shell markup. Chart series palette passed the CVD validator in both modes. Draft 1's violet/cyan-gradient palette REJECTED by Ian ("too AI-like") and kept only for the record.
- **cp142 — FULL-DEPLOYMENT REHEARSAL: every playground target shipped for real, and the rehearsal caught five real bugs (Ian 2026-07-15: "lets do #1 and 2").** **Web LIVE**: `omega deploy --direct` → gh-pages; playground.omegajs.dev now serves everything cp138–141 built — all nine meta-files 200, responsive image variants 200, minified HTML (homepage renders with 15 newlines), `generator: OMEGA`. The cp140 purge fired and failed USEFULLY: Cloudflare 10000 on `purge_cache` from a token that could read the zone = missing **Cache Purge** permission (DNS-scoped token); the designed warn-never-fail path held, and the error now carries a guided hint naming the permission + dashboard URL (pinned). **Backend LIVE**: `omega deploy` → omegajs-playground — every function updated + hosting released; api.playground.omegajs.dev answering (router error page for unknown routes = end-to-end proof). Caught: BOTH backend apps' `file:` spec carried an extra `../` (resolving OUTSIDE the repo — workspaces resolve by name so installs never noticed; the deploy staging trusts the manifest and refused with an exact message). Fixed playground + sandbox. **THE BIG CATCH — `hasOmegaConfig` was app-dir-only** while every framework gate (desktop main+build, extension build, backend manager) rides it to fail-soft: in a brand-monorepo app whose OPTIONAL app-layer file is absent (cp121c), desktop hard-failed its audit ("brand.id is required") and the extension SILENTLY built a hollow artifact — its `build.js` config had NO brand, NO urls, and nothing warned. Fixed at the SSOT: the probe is brand-aware exactly like loadConfig (`resolveConfigPath || findBrandConfigPath`), pinned so the gate and the loader can never disagree, re-vendored into all three framework dists. Extension REBUILT and verified: the zip's build.js carries the full playground brand config. **Desktop: SIGNED + NOTARIZED, beyond the plan** — three more catches on the way: (1) the manager scaffold never wrote `version`/`author` into app manifests and electron-builder hard-requires version — scaffold now writes both (playground manifest fixed); (2) electron-builder refuses semver ranges and can't see workspace-hoisted electron — `build-config` now pins `electronVersion` from the framework-resolved installed electron; (3) the scaffolded `postinstall` used a literal `node_modules/@omega.js/desktop/...` path (breaks hoisted; breaks electron-builder's prod-only install) — now resolution-based and never-fatal (`node -e "try{require('@omega.js/desktop/scripts/sync-nvmrc')}catch(e){}"`, new exports entry), and the publish owner resolves config-first (`releases.owner` → `github.org` → git discovery — a nested app has no git remote; electron-builder's update-info step crashes on a null publish config). Result: universal DMG + zip, signed with the Keychain's `Developer ID Application: Ian Wiedenman (9S9QEYN7C6)` (auto-discovered — no .p12 needed locally), NOTARIZED via the ASC API key cp135 filed, `spctl: accepted, source=Notarized Developer ID`. The Apple key COPY (cp131) is no longer blocking local signed builds — it remains the path to portable/CI signing (.p12 export) + the installer cert. **Extension**: chromium + firefox store zips + source.zip minted (store publish stays gated; CI-dispatch legs stay paused per standing gates). Stray brand-root package-lock.json (electron-builder's internal npm run) removed — the monorepo lock governs; it reappears untracked on future packages, harmless. Suites all green: desktop 775 / extension 102 / backend smoke 11 / config 110 (+1 brand-aware probe pin) / manager 681 / web 141 (+1 purge-hint pin). Bycatch: disposable-domains list refresh (prepare-time, 4 new domains).
- **cp141 — dev live-reload, narrowed (the LAST gap-audit parity item).** `omega dev` no longer needs a hand refresh: the dev server chokidars the built asset trees (new `devServerOptions()` — the image-fallback middleware + `watch` globs on `dist/assets/{css,js}`), so asset rebuilds push to the browser. NARROWED like the queue item says: the watcher classifies the changed set — css-only edits rebuild JUST the stylesheets (`buildAssets({ only: 'css' })` writes zero js files, so the dev server **hot-swaps the css without reloading the page**), js edits rebuild js (full reload — scripts need one), mixed/unknown sets rebuild everything. Dev's stable asset names make the partial manifests harmless. BROWSER-PROVEN live on the playground (chrome-devtools): a `window.__proof` marker survived a core-scss edit while the injected style landed (hot swap, no reload), then a core-js edit reloaded the page (marker gone) — both with zero hand refreshes. Suites: web 141/0 (+2: `devServerOptions` shape, only-half rebuild pins — css writes no js and vice versa).
- **cp140b rider — re-key CONVERGENCE PROVEN + the generator meta tells the truth.** The one-time translation re-key from cp140 is complete and stable on the playground: after the cache refresh, a fresh build reports `0 new, 3713 cached` (`/account` — whose 61 strings a killed verification build had orphaned mid-write — fully cached), so there is NO per-build flapping; the completed es cache lands here. Bycatch from the build-diff: every page still shipped `<meta name="generator" content="Ultimate Jekyll">` — now `OMEGA`. Web 139/0.
- **cp140 — production HTML minification + Cloudflare purge (gap-audit closure, second half).** **minifyHtml as a transform**: new `packages/web/src/minify-html.js` — the Rust minifier (`@minify-html/node`, UJM's proven option set) mounted by the engine as an Eleventy transform for `environment: 'production'` ONLY (.html outputs only — the cp139 meta-files ship exactly as rendered; dev and the test harness stay readable). The legacy extraction dance is preserved: JSON-LD scripts minify as JSON (parse→stringify; unparseable verbatim), inline scripts extract and minify via **esbuild** (already a dependency — replaces legacy's terser; minify-html's buggy JS pass stays off), IE conditional comments survive `keep_comments: false` verbatim (head.html still ships one). A page that fails to minify ships unminified — never a failed build. Playground proof: index.html renders with **11 newlines** (was hundreds), all 4 JSON-LD blocks parse, the IE conditional intact, robots/sitemap untouched. **Cloudflare purge, de-ITW'd**: legacy `uj cloudflare-purge` proxied through the hardcoded ITW wrapper API; new `src/purge.js` hits the Cloudflare API DIRECTLY with the brand's own `CLOUDFLARE_TOKEN` from the .env cascade — zone from config `cloudflare.zone` (the migrated UJM key) or looked up by the brand URL's apex. Wired three ways: `omega purge` command (`-cf`/`cloudflare-purge` legacy aliases, `--dry-run` plans), automatic after `omega deploy --direct` (content just changed → clear the edge; purge failure is a warning, never a failed deploy — the site is already live), and a guarded CI step in the scaffolded build workflow (runs only when the repo has the `CLOUDFLARE_TOKEN` secret). No token → clean skip. Playground proof: `omega purge --dry-run` resolved the REAL `omegajs.dev` zone via the token in the brand .env (read-only lookup) and planned without purging. **blogify/optimize PARKED** (survey verdict): blogify is a fake-post test-content generator (the monorepo corpus generator covers that need properly) and optimize is a GPT-based content-rewrite authoring tool — neither is a build-pipeline gap; revisit post-launch if wanted. Suites: web 139/0 (+9: extraction-dance pins incl. broken-block verbatim, production-vs-dev engine integration with meta-files untouched, purge unit set over an injected fetch recorder — direct zone, apex lookup, skip ladder, dry-run, API-error detail). **Translation re-key (one-time, self-healing)**: minified text nodes lose their surrounding whitespace, so space-padded source strings hash to NEW cache keys on a brand's first cp140 build — the default live-translate build refills them automatically (the playground's refreshed es cache rides this checkpoint's commit; strings inside the preserved IE conditional kept their keys), while `--cached-only` deploys skip those pages with the standard cold warning until one live build/`omega translate` runs. `omega translate` collects from the dist that `omega build` rendered, so build and translate always see the same (minified) HTML — no key flapping.
- **cp139 — the site meta-files: every UJM machine file has a successor, and the dangling feed link is dead (gap-audit closure, first half).** Nine new default pages in `@omega.js/web` — sitemap.xml, `/feeds/posts.xml` (RSS 2.0) + `/feeds/posts.json` (JSON Feed 1), robots.txt, ads.txt, humans.txt, opensearch.xml, the pages.json search index, and `/.well-known/security.txt` — riding the default-pages channel, so consumer files at the same URL suppress them like any other default page, and every brand gets all nine with zero setup. The `/feeds/posts.xml` link that core head.html has stamped on EVERY page since the port now resolves (Ghostii `feedUrl` too). Semantics preserved from the UJM originals (sitemap include/changefreq/priority frontmatter keys, `/test` + redirect + draft exclusions, ca-prefix-stripped AdSense line, Wayback rules, year+1 security.txt expiry) with deliberate upgrades: JSON outputs are VALID BY CONSTRUCTION (`uj_json_escape` is a real JSON escaper + first-emitted-comma pattern — legacy's trailing-comma bug when the last item was skipped is impossible, pinned by JSON.parse in tests), the machine files exclude each other (legacy pages.json listed robots.txt as a search hit), `/admin/` is excluded from sitemap + search index (matching the robots block), ads.txt renders an honest comment instead of a broken record when no AdSense client is configured, and post titles beat cascaded dir-data meta in feeds/index. New `site.uj.date.iso` engine global (legacy `site.time`) stamps sitemap lastmod + feed build dates. Playground proof: all nine in dist with real brand values — 26 sitemap locs on playground.omegajs.dev, both JSON files parse with ZERO posts (the empty-brand edge), security.txt expires 2027, ads.txt honest comment. Suites: web 130/0 (+8: per-file pins incl. exclusion sets, RSS item/guid/content shape, JSON.parse validity both files, ads.txt both states, socials-conditional humans.txt).
- **cp138 — the imagemin successor: the responsive image matrix is BACK, cached outside the repo (Ian 2026-07-14: "we NEED the imagemin task").** NEW `packages/web/src/imagemin.js` — sharp-based (no gulp), a `buildSite` phase right after `static`: every jpg/jpeg/png under `dist/assets/images` becomes the legacy UJM matrix — widths 320/640/1024 + the original size × source-format + webp (8 outputs per image), quality 80 (progressive mozjpeg / png palette), metadata stripped, UPSCALING allowed so every variant name ALWAYS exists (the `@srcset` no-404 contract the core lazy-loader consumes). The original NAME survives re-encoded (URL contracts hold — the playground sample ships 43% smaller under its own name); svg/gif/webp pass through verbatim; the minted `favicon/` dir is exempt (exact-name mint contract); an undecodable file warns and ships verbatim — never a failed build; uppercase extensions ship lowercased with a case-insensitive-filesystem-safe order of operations. **`web.imagemin` is LIVE** (INERT since the migration mapping): schema-known on the web target, `enabled: false` opts out (build logs it, images ship verbatim). **Cache outside the repo**: content-addressed entries (bytes + settings salt) at the brand's `.omega/cache/imagemin` — machine-owned, never committed (reproducible binaries), stale entries prune every run, warm outputs byte-identical — and the scaffolded build workflow now restores it via an `actions/cache` step keyed on `hashFiles('src/assets/images/**')` (the `cache-uj-imagemin` branch successor; exact hit = zero processing in CI). **Dev never processes**: `omega dev` mounts a dev-server middleware (`devImageFallback`) that rewrites missing `-NNNpx`/`.webp` variant URLs to the verbatim original — legacy serve.js fallback semantics, traversal-guarded, LIVE-PROVEN with curl (variant URLs 200 with the original's bytes, junk 404s). Playground proof: a real 1600×900 sample jpg minted into the website app; cold build processed 3 images → 24 outputs (brandmark.png upscale-proven to 1024px), warm rerun `0 processed, 3 from cache` at a 0.03s phase; `omega test`/deploy ride the same build command, so the matrix ships everywhere automatically. Suites: web 122 (+10: matrix dims/formats, upscale, uppercase, verbatim+exempt pins, warm-cache byte-identity, prune, corrupt-file resilience, missing-dir no-op, full buildSite integration, dev fallback incl. traversal + query strings), config 109 (imagemin schema entry).
- **cp137 — canonical .env ordering, the omega.json5 treatment for env files (Ian 2026-07-14: "reorder our env files like the config files? so we have a consistent order? moving comments too?").** NEW `lib/env-order.js` — CANONICAL_ENV_GROUPS is the ONE list of known keys, grouping, order, and group comments (scaffold's ENV_GROUPS absorbed + the previously ungrouped keys homed: the Omega identity keys lead, Font Awesome Pro and the auto-generated ACCOUNT_PASSWORD_SEED/CSC_KEY_PASSWORD close). The scaffold stub renders FROM it and every reorder renders FROM it, so a fresh brand's .env and a years-old converged one have the same shape. Reorder semantics: machine comments (boxed `# ── … ──` headers, `# KEY=` placeholders, SSOT note lines, the legacy trailer) REGENERATE — stale text self-heals; hand comments travel verbatim with the key below them; the leading comment block stays as the header; unknown keys keep relative order in an Other section; duplicates collapse to the LAST occurrence (dotenv.parse's winner — the effective value can never change) with hand comments merged onto the keeper. Loss-proof: structural pre-check (only comment/blank/single-line KEY=value — multi-line values and export forms decline untouched), kept lines carried VERBATIM, and a last-wins value-equality post-check refuses any render that would change an effective value. Wired at both write paths: `writeEnvValue` applies the order on every writeback (the writeConfigValues pattern; declines fall back to the plain write), and the workspace service gains an `env-order` ensure op converging the brand .env AND the company .env when company-managed (dry-run plans, unparseable files are a dim note never a failure, missing files skip). LIVE on the playground: 3 duplicate lines collapsed (the doubled Google OAuth pair + ACCOUNT_PASSWORD_SEED, last values kept), every key regrouped, the stale pre-cp134 group comments self-healed ("firebase" → "cloud", "sendgrid + beehiiv" → "campaigns + newsletter"), SENTRY_AUTH_TOKEN landed in its monitoring group, missing keys documented as placeholders (META/TIKTOK pixels, PayPal/Chargebee, MRLOGO) — rerun converged ("current"), env still loads (monitoring 4× Exists). Manager 681 (9 new: render shape, organic regroup, idempotency, empty-last dup, decline, default-header rule, writeEnvValue integration, workspace op both-files + dry-run/missing; the old byte-exact writeEnvValue pin respelled to value semantics).
- **cp136b — monitoring MINTED LIVE for the playground; the desktop platform slug was invalid (Ian's first live run, 2026-07-14).** Ian's mint 400'd on `omega-playground-desktop`: Sentry validates project platforms against its integration-docs index (`Project.is_valid_platform` → `integration_doc_exists`), where Electron is the TOP-LEVEL id `electron` — `javascript-electron` (what cp132 used) is only a frontend-UI alias and does not exist there. His scopes were never the problem (the team create succeeded on `org:write`). Two fixes: `TARGET_PLATFORMS.desktop` → `electron` (comment now states the validation source), and `SentryAPI.makeRequest` serializes field-keyed DRF validation bodies into the error when `detail`/`message` are absent — the failure now reads `{"platform":["Invalid platform"]}` instead of a bare `Bad Request` (the same raw-error class cp129 fixed for Apple). Stale org-token wording in the API lib header + README platform list corrected. Now `GOOGLE_SCOPES` (the manager-wide union, google-auth SSOT) + ONE shared store (`.omega/auth/google-tokens.json`) serve firebase/search-console/analytics/adsense: the operator consents ONCE, and the store records its granted scopes so a cached-but-underscoped token (legacy stores, future scope additions) re-consents once instead of 403ing forever. GA's data-collection acknowledgement stopped being a warn-and-rerun: interactive runs pressEnterToOpen the settings page and retry-poll (`(enter)=check now, (s)=skip`) until the secret mints — ONE analytics run finishes the job (acknowledgement is per-property, so the first stream's prompt clears all four). Bonus fix from Ian's log: analytics' firebase-link op read only `firebase.projectId` and skipped playground — now derives via `cloud.config.projectId` like the firebase service (cp100). Run records now serialize skip `reason` + `missingEnv` (machine-readable WHY — previously console-only). Companion fix: headless Google consent **fails fast** — google-auth refuses to open a browser or camp on its 5-min callback wait without a human (was: background runs popped a consent tab on Ian's desktop and hung 5 minutes; now: instant error carrying the exact scopes + seeding instruction). Deliberately NOT in `npm test`/CI — real APIs, run sparingly. Tests: 7 pipeline (engine matrix + fail-fast timing) + devkit env-override case; manager 630 (+7), devkit 196 (+1). Found along the way: the framework ships NO default homepage by design (the homepage IS the brand's marketing page = skin scope) — the playground app gains a 3-line `src/pages/index.md` on `blueprint/index` so `/` serves; title/copy stay placeholder until the skin. Service wave (each live, once): workspace ✓, disperse ✓ (backend functions `.env` composed, 8 keys), recaptcha ✓ (shared secret validated; the key's domain list has no read API — console note printed), account ✓ (`support@playground.omegajs.dev` created through the LIVE backend — admin role, plan, signup completed; Google-provider link is interactive-only, expected ✗ on create), testing ✓⚠ (api health 200, homepage 200, Pages deployment success; the ⚠ was this checkpoint's then-uncommitted files), assets ⊘ (no brandmark source yet), adsense ⊘ (no accountId). Wave part 2 (same day, Ian: "services should be runnable non-interactively — pass the answers in"): sendgrid RAN fully autonomously (domain auth created + its 3 DNS records via Cloudflare, sender, list — `marketing.campaigns.listId` written back into omega.json5 by the config writeback — 17 fields verified; stopped at a SendGrid 429 on segments, any rerun converges); beehiiv RAN and reported its one true manual step (Beehiiv has NO create-publication API — exact console recipe printed; fields/segments/webhook auto-provision after); search-console failed honestly after its 5-min window (needs a ONE-TIME OAuth scope grant: webmasters + siteverification — after that it's non-interactive forever); analytics skip names its answer channel (`analytics.providers.google.propertyId`). - **cp115 — proxy eligibility is plan-aware (2026-07-12, Ian: "not all users will be on the same plans — if there's a way to tell, that would be nice").** There is a way to tell, and the hosting op now uses it: cp114b's static free-plan rule becomes the fast path, and deeper api domains ask the zone what TLS coverage it ACTUALLY has — `GET /acm/total_tls` (Total TLS mints a certificate for every proxied hostname) and `GET /ssl/certificate_packs` (active packs matched with real wildcard semantics via NEW `certHostMatches` — `*.play.zone` covers `api.play.zone`, `*.zone` doesn't). Covered deep names ride the proxy (dim note says what covers them), uncovered ones stay DNS-only, and lookup failures fail safe to DNS-only (works on every plan; wrongly-proxied never does). The never-un-proxy exception keys off the same live answer, so a paid zone's working proxied record is never flipped down. Bonus fix: hosting with NO Cloudflare client deref'd `zone.name` on null since cp114b — now guarded (+ regression test). firebase 36 (+4: Total-TLS PATCH-up, pending-vs-active cert pack, lookup-failure fallback, null-client); manager 623.
- **cp114b — deep api domains stay DNS-only (2026-07-12, live find).** Minutes after the playground's DNS converged, `api.playground.omegajs.dev` resolved but failed TLS: Cloudflare Universal SSL covers only `*.zone` (one label), so a proxied two-label CNAME can never handshake. The firebase hosting op now computes proxy eligibility per domain (`universalSslCovers`) — apex/one-label domains proxy as before, deeper ones (subdomain projects) keep a DNS-only CNAME and Firebase serves its own certificate; the never-un-proxy guard gains exactly one exception for never-coverable names, so the playground's pre-fix proxied record converges down on the next firebase run. firebase 32 (+1).
- **cp114 — missing secrets ask for themselves (2026-07-12, Ian: "run the manager with no .env and check that it asks for them").** NEW `ensureEnvSecrets` gate (manager lib) — the ONE way a service declares the env vars its APIs need: all present → proceed; missing + interactive → each value is asked for (NEW masked `password` prompt in devkit), persisted to the brand `.env` (writeEnvValue), exported for the running process, and the run continues — a fresh brand configures itself mid-run; missing + non-interactive → skip with a machine-readable `missingEnv` list that the run summary aggregates into a NEW 🔑 section (exact keys per service + the interactive retry command, sibling of the ⚑ needs-interactive section). Adopted in cloudflare (CLOUDFLARE_TOKEN), domain (CLOUDFLARE_TOKEN + NAMECHEAP_USERNAME/API_KEY), sendgrid, beehiiv, recaptcha (both keys) — each with a mint-one URL; firebase/github keep their bespoke flows. Proof: an env-less scratch brand run prints the skip + the 🔑 aggregate naming CLOUDFLARE_TOKEN and the retry command. Tests: 6 env-secrets cases (proceed / skip shape / dry-run-never-asks / ask→writeback→export / empty-paste / 🔑 rendering) + recaptcha reason updates; manager 618 (+6), devkit prompt 18.
- **cp112b — extension icons wired: all three targets now SYNONYMOUS (2026-07-12, Ian: "we need icon support SYNONYMOUS IN ALL including bxm").** The build-side chain + emission move to their right shared home — NEW `@omega.js/devkit/icons` (`resolveFontAwesomeRoots` + `emitIcons`, ONE implementation; web's `src/fontawesome-roots.js`/`src/icons.js` deleted, engine/build respelled; devkit gains the client dep for icon-core's PACKAGES order; tests moved to devkit). BXM wiring: `@fortawesome/fontawesome-free` becomes an extension dependency (the floor, same as web/desktop), new gulp `fontawesome` task emits the merged set to `dist/assets/fa` every brand build (packaged = offline), all four page surfaces (popup/options/sidepanel/page) get the shared watcher via one self-starting `src/lib/icons.js` import with a `chrome.runtime.getURL` fetch resolver, and `assets/fa/*` joins `web_accessible_resources` (defaults template + playground manifest) so content-script-injected UI can fetch icons too — content scripts deliberately don't auto-watch host DOMs (FA-using sites would collide); they `scan()` their own containers. Proof: playground extension build exit 0 with **5,681 icons emitted including Pro-only `acorn`** — Ian's Pro set flowed brand `.env` → BXM build untouched-by-hand. Suites: devkit 195, web 103 (6 tests moved out), extension 102.
- **cp112 — dynamic icons everywhere: ONE browser auto-render (2026-07-12, Ian: "fa-{icon} via js MUST work" + "make it part of one single WEB library").** The old stack's icon headache (three inconsistent import styles + the prerendered-sprite system) is fully dead: authoring is plain Font Awesome markup on every surface — `<i class="fa-solid fa-rocket">` static, `el.className = 'fa-sharp fa-light fa-play'` set or CHANGED via JS at any time — rendered by ONE shared module, NEW `@omega.js/client/modules/icon-renderer.js` (scan + MutationObserver for insertions AND class changes, re-render in place, clear on class removal, caching, `data-omega-fa` marker); FA's family × weight class parsing moves into icon-core as `parseIconClasses` (SSOT). Targets supply only transport: desktop = IPC to main's icon server (renderer.js's ~90-line local watcher deleted — it also gains attr-change re-render, which the cp111b version lacked), web = `fetch` from the site's OWN emitted set — every build now ships the merged chain (brand Pro + free + curated core, best source per file) to `assets/fa/<style>/<name>.svg` (new build phase `icons` via `src/icons.js`; browsers fetch only icons actually used, hosting deploys diff by hash), wired in `runtime/boot.js` for every page. Extension: recipe documented, wired when its first icon consumer lands. NEW repo doc hub docs/icons.md (authoring, chain, Pro supply — desktop doc trimmed to a pointer); CLAUDE.md Icons section. Suites: client 96 (+1: class-model matrix), web 109 (+2: emission merge + real-chain), desktop 767 (+1: live re-class → swap → clear proof).
- **cp111 — Font Awesome Pro brand supply (2026-07-12, Ian: "i want to use my pro icons").** The channel cp108 deferred: every icon surface now resolves through a best-first root chain — `OMEGA_FONTAWESOME_ROOT` (a fontawesome.com "Pro for Web" download dir, no npm token needed) → the brand's own `@fortawesome/fontawesome-pro` npm install → the `@fortawesome/fontawesome-free` floor, which always stays in the chain so a partial brand set never loses icons (or alias metadata) the free set has. Pro remains NO omega package's dependency — license — each brand authenticates the `@fortawesome` scope with its own FA token or supplies a download dir. icon-core owns the preference order (`PACKAGES`) and style validation switches from the free-set whitelist to path-safe shape (`STYLE_REGEX`), so Pro families (light/thin/duotone/sharp-*, future sets) work with zero framework changes while the IPC no-traversal guarantee holds; web gains `src/fontawesome-roots.js` (engine feeds `[core, brand?, free]` + richest alias file), desktop's `lib/fontawesome.js` walks the same chain (env override logged, Pro logged, free fallthrough), and desktop's renderer auto-render learns FA's family × weight class model — `fa-regular`/`fa-light`/`fa-thin` weights plus `fa-sharp`/`fa-duotone`/`fa-sharp-duotone` family prefixes compose (`fa-sharp fa-light` → `sharp-light`; previously everything but `fa-brands` silently rendered solid, and Pro-style markup without a Pro set now stays empty instead of injecting the wrong style). Tests are Pro-presence-proof (green before and after Ian installs Pro). Suites: client 95 (+1), web 107 (+4), template-kit 42, desktop 766 (+2) — incl. a live main-process proof that an env-root glyph resolves while `play`/`search` still come from the free floor, and a renderer proof that `fa-light fa-play` never falls back to the solid glyph. Docs: desktop docs/fontawesome.md "Supplying Font Awesome Pro", docs/template-kit.md.
