- **scripts/copy-legacy-env.js — Ian-run secret mover (2026-07-12).** Copies selected keys from the legacy omega-manager `.env` into a brand's `.env` (default `apps/omega-playground`) without ever displaying a value: curated groups (`core` = cloudflare/namecheap/sendgrid/beehiiv/recaptcha; `signing` + `stores` opt-in via `--include`; payment keys never — live-mode payment stays gated), verbatim raw-line copy (quoted multi-line values intact), idempotent (existing keys kept unless `--force`, replace-in-place, dated marker comment), `--dry-run`/`--only`/`--brand`/`--source`, dest written 0600. 8 node:test cases + a real dry-run proof (all 7 core keys present in legacy, absent in playground).

### Changed
- **cp228 — every package is 1.0.0 (Ian 2026-07-18): the version foundation of the local-era fork.** All seven non-1.0.0 packages reset in one sweep — backend 6.2.0, client 5.0.0, desktop 2.2.0, extension 2.2.0, devkit 1.2.0, web 0.4.0, manager 0.1.0 → **1.0.0** (account/config/template-kit were already there) — and the one real registry range normalized with them: `@omega.js/client` is `^1.0.0` in BOTH consumers now (extension pinned a stale `^5.0.0`, desktop carried `*`; the published-runtime dep of both frameworks needs a real range for the eventual publish flip). Nothing has ever published under the new names, so the renumber is conflict-free; legacy-numbered releases keep shipping from the legacy repos. The full battery proves the reset inert: every package suite green, corpus 1242 + shapes 7/7, cross-stack e2e PASSED, wizard journey PASSED, goldens **byte-zero on both themes** (the web build stamps the CONSUMER's version into build meta/SW — never the framework's). Lockfile resynced under npu. Tooling scar for the record: `npm pkg set 'dependencies.@omega.js/client=…'` parses the package name's dot as a key-path separator (writes a garbage `"@omega": { "js/client": … }` block and leaves the real spec untouched, plus side-effect resorts/range rewrites) — the edits landed as exact-match string replacements instead.
- **cp110 — per-app Node: the split-brain dies (2026-07-12, friction #15, Ian: "just make it work").** One brand spans Node majors (web pins 24, backend functions pin 22, desktop pins Electron's 24) and used to demand nvm switches + rerun-to-pass setups. Now the manager's `runCommand` — the single spawn seam for update/build/test fan-outs — resolves the app dir's `.nvmrc` (root, then `functions/`, legacy-update semantics ported to `src/lib/node-version.js`) against nvm's installed versions and prepends that Node's bin to the child's PATH: node/npm/npx all resolve per-app, a pinned-but-missing major fails fast naming the `nvm install`, and a command that rewrites its own pin and then fails (EM setup when Electron's bundled Node moves) is re-resolved and retried once — one run self-heals. Setups stop halting mid-run: BEM's nvmrc fix writes the pin and continues (the throw is gone), BEM's node-version check warns into the summary instead of hard-halting, EM's ensureNodeVersion warns instead of throwing (its pin is already written first). Live proof: one parent process on v24 drove playground website→v24.15.0, backend→v22.22.1 (`↪ Node v22.22.1 (.nvmrc v22/*)`), desktop→v24.15.0 — zero nvm switches. Suites: manager 608 (+6: spec parsing, highest-patch matching, functions/ lookup, shim-proven PATH override, fail-fast, rewrite-retry), backend boot 11, desktop 764+5skip.
- **cp109 — theme-once: the classy triplication is dead (2026-07-12, C4, Ian's go).** `@omega.js/web/themes/` is now the ONE theme tree for web, desktop, and extension. Desktop + extension declare it in `omega.vendorAssets` (the cp104 channel) — every prepare copies the resolved web package's `themes/` into their `dist/assets/themes/`, exactly where both targets' sass loadPaths and the extension's `__theme__`/`__main_assets__` webpack aliases already pointed; their hand-copied theme trees are DELETED. The drift audit showed rot in BOTH directions — desktop's classy fork was missing web's `has-validation` input-group fix, while web's was missing desktop's `safeRedirect` zero-trust gate in hero-demo-form.js — so the shared classy is the UNION: upstreamed into web = desktop's safeRedirect, its `$min-contrast-ratio` knob, its `.em-titlebar` component, plus the extension fork's six generic modules (soft-colors, spacing, avatars, links, logo-scroll, spinners); web wins all divergent shared files. Desktop's prebuilt `bootstrap.bundle.js` moved from themes/ to `assets/js/`. A separate `@omega.js/themes` package was considered and rejected — the vendor channel gives one-source semantics without a new publishable surface. Proofs: both dists carry has-validation + safeRedirect + titlebar; omega-playground desktop AND extension consumer builds exit 0 with the union in their compiled css (the desktop css now has fixes its old fork lacked; extension pages get web's real `_theme.js` init instead of a commented-out stub). Suites: web 103, extension 102, desktop 764+5skip. Docs: desktop themes/tooltips + extension themes/css docs rewritten. The skin pass now ships everywhere at once by construction.
- **cp108 — ONE FontAwesome mechanism, and the Pro-redistribution license bug dies (2026-07-12, C4, Ian's go).** Icon semantics now live in a single module — NEW `@omega.js/client/modules/icon-core.js` (pure CJS, the analytics-core pattern): valid names/styles, candidate lookup order (requested style → brands fallback), the injected SVG root attributes (merged best-of-both: web's only-when-absent injection + desktop's full set including the FA-7 `overflow="visible"` clip fix, so web icons gain `aria-hidden`/`focusable`/`overflow` too), and alias mapping from fontawesome-free's metadata (`search` → `magnifying-glass`, 514 aliases). The default asset source is the **`@fortawesome/fontawesome-free` npm dependency** (web + desktop), resolved through node_modules and never vendored: desktop's 21MB / 4,799-icon vendored Font Awesome **Pro** set is DELETED — a published @omega.js/desktop shipping Pro SVGs would have been license-violating redistribution — and web's silent icon gap closes (core carried only 12 curated SVGs, so most of the 591 `uj_icon` template uses rendered the default warning triangle; the engine now passes the chain `[core/icons, fontawesome-free/svgs]` + alias metadata, and true misses warn once per name). template-kit's `uj_icon` and desktop's `lib/fontawesome.js` both consume icon-core (template-kit gains the client dep; desktop's IPC + renderer auto-render contract unchanged — brands/regular/solid only now, Pro styles return null). Extension asset supply + brand-level Pro override land with the theme-once work (cp109+). Proof: live chain render — curated-first `rocket`, free-only `circle-user` (was the triangle), alias `search`, brands-fallback `github`. Suites: client 94 (+4), template-kit 42 (+1), web 103, desktop 764+5skip. Docs: desktop docs/fontawesome.md rewritten, docs/template-kit.md updated.
- **cp102b — run-mode gates standardized (2026-07-12, Ian's go on the survey).** NEW `packages/manager/src/lib/run-gates.js` — the three patterns every ensure-handler was hand-rolling now have ONE home: `canPrompt(options)` replaces the TTY+dry-run compound that had drifted into three spellings across 21 sites (a handler checking only one half is how prompt-during-dry-run bugs start); `dryRunPlan(message, result?)` owns the canonical `⊘ Dry run — would <message>` line (~60 sites swept; 8 drifted "… skipped" phrasings respelled into the canonical form; single-line planned-returns folded into one-statement gates); `needsInteractiveSkip(key, action, note?)` builds the #32 warned step-aside (billing + cloud-messaging converted; authentication keeps its accumulator style for multi-step merges). 46 files touched, zero behavior changes — output format and gate semantics are byte-compatible. Manager 602 (+3: canPrompt TTY/dry-run matrix, dryRunPlan format + pass-through, needsInteractiveSkip shape).
- **cp107 — FormManager is a shared client module (2026-07-12, C4).** The 1,471-line form state machine moved verbatim from `packages/web/core/js/libs/` into `@omega.js/client/modules/form-manager.js` — it always built exclusively on client primitives (now imported relatively: `./dom.js`, `../index.js`; zero self-name refs). All 23 web import sites plus web classy's dynamic import respelled to `@omega.js/client/modules/form-manager.js`; web's copy deleted. Desktop's classy `hero-demo-form.js` had a DEAD `__main_assets__` alias import (unresolvable in desktop webpack since the theme was hand-copied) — it now points at the client module, which desktop already carries as a runtime dependency (cp106b); the extension gains the module the same way. One implementation, every surface. Suites: client 90 (+5 — real import under the test DOM shim, constructor-throw behavior, source pins), web 103, desktop 764+5skip, extension 102.
- **cp100e — deploys ship the RESOLVED config (2026-07-12, friction #31).** The config cascade (company ← brand ← app) is a local walk-up and the upload boundary cut it — deployed functions served framework defaults (`GET /omega/brand` → "My Brand"). NEW `composeTargetConfig(projectDir, target)` in @omega.js/config: freezes the target's full interleave (brand shared ← brand target ← app shared ← app target) into ONE self-contained config file — presence-only `targets` map (a raw merged targets map would let a brand-target value beat an app-shared one, flipping the chain) and NO baked framework defaults (the shipped runtime applies its own, so defaults evolve with the package). The backend deploy gains `stage-resolved-config` (the config twin of stage-local-packages): compose → write into the upload with a do-not-commit banner → deploy → restore original bytes in `finally`; apps without a brand layer are self-contained no-ops. Proofs: config 99 (equivalence theorem: composed-standalone resolution === local walk-up, same errors + enabled; the interleave corner pinned), backend boot 11 (upload-boundary simulation resolves "Acme Corp" not "My Brand"; byte-identical restore), and a live staging pass on the real playground tree ("OMEGA Playground" + billing/org composed; git clean after restore). **LIVE-VERIFIED same night**: playground redeploy (both stagings fired in the real path) → production `GET /omega/brand` serves `"name":"OMEGA Playground"` + real url/contact — the "My Brand" placeholder era is over.
- **cp102 — provisioning tri-state standard + needs-interactive aggregate (2026-07-12, Ian's #33 + #32).** ONE contract for provisioning config keys, live in the manager's config-flow engine: **missing/null = ASK** in an interactive run (the answer lands in omega.json5), **`false` = opted OUT** (silent skip, never prompts or warns again — honored at the key or any ancestor section), **value = use it**. Every ask can land the opt-out: the gate's Disable is always offered (disablePath defaults to the value's own path) and selection flows can carry an inline opt-out choice. First consumers: `firebase.organizationId` — project creation now ASKS when unset (pick from `organizations:search` or "No organization — create it standalone"), so org-less downloaders onboard cleanly; `firebase.billingAccount` — the billing step asks when unset (pick from the billing-accounts list, create one in the console, or "Stay on the Spark plan"), and `false` is a clean Spark success with zero nagging; `firebase.supportEmail` already conformed (null auto-derives the authorizing user's email). The run summary gains a **`⚑ Skipped — needs an interactive run` aggregate** (#32) fed by `needsInteractive` output markers (VAPID paste, OAuth redirect-URI confirm, billing ask) with per-service `npm start -- --service=<name>` hints; the retry command is the blessed `npm start -- …` form now too (npx gets rerouted by stdio-piping shell wrappers on some machines — the form that burned Ian live). New `listBillingAccounts` + `listOrganizations` on the Firebase API client (existing scopes already cover both; org search failure returns `[]` so it can never block creation). Docs: tri-state section in docs/config.md. Manager 599 (+13: tri-state engine ×4, aggregate ×4, billing pick/opt-out/marker ×3, org ask/standalone ×2).
- **cp106b — ONE analytics engine, literally (2026-07-12).** GA4 Measurement Protocol semantics now live in a single module: NEW `@omega.js/client/modules/analytics-core.js` (pure CJS — identity math: deriveNamespace/deriveClientId/deriveUserId; GA4 event-name rules; user_properties wrapping; collect URL + payload shape). The browser engine consumes it, and @omega.js/desktop's main-process lib now requires the SAME module (client added as a desktop runtime dependency; its own uuidv5/payload/URL/normalize code deleted — queue, IPC bridge, env secret, and user-properties enrichment stay desktop-local). Identity and payload shape can never drift between surfaces again. Suites: client 85, desktop 764+5skip (analytics + bridge + real-Electron boot canary), web 103 (esbuild CJS-interop proven in the bundle path). C4 cp106 CLOSED — next cp107 forms.
- **Analytics speaks ONE shape with ONE identity — cp106a complete (2/2, 2026-07-12; Ian: "unify it and have it in one place").** The canonical `analytics.providers.google.{id,secret}` shape now flows end to end: @omega.js/client's flat `analytics.google`/`googleSecret` handoff is dead (init reads the providers shape + passes the brand's projectId), web foot.html's flatten bridge is dead (`resolved.analytics` rides into the page config verbatim — ids only, secrets never reach omega.json5), and the extension's build snapshot emits nested `providers.google.{id,secret}` (secret still baked from `GOOGLE_ANALYTICS_SECRET` at build). The client engine ported desktop's cross-surface uuidv5 identity: namespace = uuidv5(projectId), client_id = uuidv5(deviceId, ns) from a persisted `_omega_device_id`, user_id = uuidv5(firebaseUid, ns) auto-wired to the Firebase auth listener — the same human resolves to the same GA4 identity on web/desktop/extension/backend, and raw uids never leave the device. `setUserProperties` implemented ({value}-wrapped, merged into every payload). Dead `itwcw-package-analytics` dep dropped from client; `uuid` added. Suites: client 85, web 103 (flat-bridge pin flipped to canonical), extension 102. Next: cp106b — desktop's lib collapses onto this engine.
- **Pricing marketing chrome restored — Ian's scope correction on cp97 (checkpoint 98b, 2026-07-11).** Only PLAN fiction stays dead; the marketing chrome is deliberate product behavior and ships ON by default again across all three themes: the promo banner (15% OFF! / WELCOME15 — `pricing.promo` overrides badge/text/code, `pricing.promo: false` kills it), the annual savings badge (real computed % when prices allow, "up to 20% off" default otherwise), guarantee lines (`pricing.guarantee` / `pricing.card_guarantee` override), the Enterprise/group-access card (`pricing.enterprise: false` kills), social proof, testimonials (classy), and FAQ defaults — now layout-frontmatter defaults the consumer overrides per-page. Blueprint pricing's SoftwareApplication schema `features` list restored; the aggregateRating JSON-LD is DECIDED-KEEP (friction #21 closed as Ian's call, not a defect). Pricing plans remain 100% `payment.products` (unchanged from cp97). Live re-proof on omega-brand: banner + social proof + testimonials + FAQs + enterprise + schema present, plans still exactly premium/launch-kit/enterprise-contact. Web suite 81 (chrome-default assertions replace the removal pins).

### Fixed
- **cp227 — parked-findings sweep: deterministic meta-files, extension cache flagged off, dead newsletter alerts culled.** Three ledger items closed. (1) `sitemap.xml` + `pages.json` now emit in URL byte order — Eleventy's `collections.all` order varies run-to-run (render/discovery concurrency), so two builds of the SAME tree listed URLs differently (cp204 golden-control find); the meta templates iterate the new `allByUrl` collection (byte-order compare, immune to locale/ICU drift across machines) and the meta-files suite pins the sorted order. (2) The extension background service worker's page cache gets the web SW's verdict (cp200 find): it was write-only — one `caches.open` call, zero reads anywhere in the extension, and no context even sends `update-cache` — so `updateCache` is disabled behind the same single `CACHE_WARMING_ENABLED = false` re-enable flag, machinery kept, pinned by a build-layer source test. (3) The newsletter band's static success/error alert divs die (cp209 find, deferred to "cull or wire" at pass B): FormManager presents success/error as toasts and the baked `d-none` divs were never toggled — culled from both theme markups along with the now-orphaned `form.success_message`/`error_message` args (four layout call-sites + both showcase demos; `form.placeholder` stays). Goldens: zero unexplained on both themes — the alert cull is the ONE declared lane (21 pages each), and the meta-file reorder is invisible because the golden normalizer has always sorted those two files. web 248/248 · extension 105/105 · corpus 1242 · e2e + journey PASSED.
- **cp225 — page-wins parity repair: Eleventy's cascade merge no longer corrupts `resolved`.** Found by pass A's about draft — the first consumer page to override a layout's array defaults. Eleventy's data-cascade merge CONCATS a page array onto a layout-default array (the draft's four timeline moments rendered as layout's-four-plus-theirs) and lets a layout-default object beat a page scalar (the documented `gallery: false` kill switch could never fire — no consumer had ever exercised it). No Eleventy hook sees page data apart from the cascade (preprocessors receive the merged tree), so the engine re-parses the page's own frontmatter from source and re-applies it over the cascade with the shared `deepMerge` inside the `resolved` computed: arrays replace, scalars beat objects, partial object overrides keep layout siblings, virtual templates (blueprints/sample content) keep pure cascade behavior. Goldens: exactly ONE explained diff per theme — the mini fixture's own alternative doc authored two faqs that had been concatenating with the layout's five templated defaults (both the rendered accordion and its FAQPage JSON-LD deduplicate; question-set-verified in cp225-explain.js); every other page byte-zero. Pinned in `test/resolved-page-wins.test.js`. web 247/247 · corpus 1242 · e2e + journey PASSED.
- **Walkthrough UX restored + upgraded — press-Enter-to-open everywhere, auto-open auth (checkpoint 101d, Ian's calls 2026-07-12).** The omega-manager "press enter to automatically open" convention (dropped in the port) is back as a devkit primitive: `pressEnterToOpen(url, label)` (interactive: Enter → browser; non-TTY: prints the URL and returns false) + dependency-free `openInBrowser` (darwin `open`/win `start`/`xdg-open`), adopted at the VAPID console, the sign-in-providers page, and the OAuth-client settings steps. The manage OAuth URL now AUTO-OPENS the browser in interactive terminals with the wait bumped 2→5 minutes — the friction #25 dead-link race (human reads a URL after its listener expired) is largely gone. Queued as standards from Ian's same message: the provisioning tri-state (#33: null=ask / false=opted-out / value=use — org-less and billing-less users must onboard cleanly) and a needs-interactive aggregate in the run summary (#32). devkit 188, manager 586.
- **OAuth consent screen creates itself — supportEmail must be OWNED by the caller (checkpoint 101c, closes friction #29; plus `omega build --translate`).** The consent ensure-step defaulted supportEmail to `support@{domain}`, which Google's IAP brand API rejects outright ("Request contains an invalid argument") — it only accepts an identity the AUTHORIZING user owns; the step's own header documented the constraint its default violated. Fixed end to end: the OAuth scope list gains `userinfo.email` (tokens cached before it re-consent once in the browser), the API client gains `getAuthenticatedEmail()`, and the step resolves config `firebase.supportEmail` (for an owned Google Group) → the authed user's own email → warn-with-guidance; a doomed value is never sent, and dry-run stays zero-network. Manager 586 (+2).
- **Build translation settles: complete by default, `--cached-only` to opt out (checkpoints 101b+101d, friction #24 — Ian's final call).** The engine gained a `cachedOnly` mode (a page-language pair with ANY cold string skips WHOLE — no mixed-language copies, hreflang stays honest because only produced copies get alternates — with a warning listing the skips, capped at 10 + count; stats gain `skippedCold`). After trying cached-only as the build default, Ian's final call: **`omega build` translates everything by default** — warm strings from the committed cache (instant, zero provider calls once warm), cold strings live through the provider, so a build always ships the complete translated site; `omega build --cached-only` gives the provider-free build. `omega translate` unchanged as the standalone live pass. Web suite 103 covers both modes. docs/translation.md documents the lifecycle.
- **Functions deploy works with local-first packages — staging step + first LIVE deploy (checkpoint 100d, Ian's go 2026-07-12).** The first real `omega deploy --only functions` against omegajs-playground failed ALL 10 functions in Cloud Build (friction #30): the functions dir's `"@omega.js/backend": "file:../../../../../packages/backend"` points outside the uploaded folder (Cloud Build got a 37 KB source with no backend), and GCF's buildpack runs `npm ci`, which hard-requires a matching package-lock.json. The deploy command even had a BEM-era guard ("Please remove local packages before deploying!") — reading the WRONG package.json (@omega.js/backend's own, never the consumer's) and embodying the obsolete publish-first worldview. New `stage-local-packages` util: packs each outside-the-folder `file:` dep into `functions/omega_modules/*.tgz` (`npm pack` runs the package's prepare, so the tarball always reflects current source — the cp104 vendor design makes the backend self-contained, local deps are devDependencies folded into dist), respells package.json to the tarball, regenerates the lockfile for the staged shape, then restores everything verbatim after deploy (originals never committed differently). Deploy runs it automatically when `--only` includes functions; boot-layer tests exercise it against REAL npm (stage + lockfile-resolves + restore, and the no-op path). `deploy.log` added to the functions ignore (template + playground) — the first upload shipped its own 18 KB log. Second deploy: 1.38 MB staged upload, all 10 `omega_*` functions built and live; third deploy exit-0 end to end (policy pre-step → staging → deploy → public-invoker fix set `allUsers` on omega_api → restore verbatim), and the live probe answered — `GET /omega/brand` 200 via the hosting rewrite, our router speaking from production. That same probe surfaced the NEXT finding (friction #31, own slice queued): the deployed backend serves PLACEHOLDER brand values ("my-app"/"My Brand") because the config cascade's walk-up stops at the upload boundary — the brand-layer omega.json5 never ships; the staging step should compose the RESOLVED config into the upload the same way it stages packages. Also closed **friction #27b with a corrected diagnosis**: the org-parented create-project flow WAS ported all along (project-flow.js + `createProject`, tested end-to-end) — the real gaps were the config VALUE nobody set (legacy hardcoded ITW's org id; now `firebase.organizationId: "1076309192968"` in the playground omega.json5, with the billing account) and omegajs-playground being hand-created outside the flow entirely. Friction #30b logged: the assistant's "wrong service account" warning compares SA project_id to the brand id instead of `cloud.config.projectId`.
- **The playground brand is EXPLICIT — rename + domain scoping + org placement (checkpoint 100c, Ian's directives 2026-07-11 late).** Ian's calls, all landed: `apps/omega-brand` → **`apps/omega-playground`** (git mv; gitignored .env/token-cache/secrets rode along), identity `omega-playground` / **"OMEGA Playground"** / `playground.omegajs.dev` — so every DERIVED surface scopes off the real domain (`api.playground.omegajs.dev` hosting claim, `authDomain: playground.omegajs.dev` healed by the writeback, canonicals, extension name, desktop `dev.omegajs.playground` via the cp95c derivation — the stale explicit override died). The real `omegajs.dev` belongs to the future standalone OMEGA brand, full stop (CLAUDE.md topology updated). Ian also **moved the GCP project into `itw-creative-works-org`** (it was org-less: cp96b provisioned it via the Firebase CLI's personal login — friction #27b logged: the ported firebase service lacks legacy's org-parented `createProject` step, the actual root cause) and the manage rerun stamped the console name **"OMEGA Playground"** live. Proofs: website build 65 pages with the new identity rendered ×11 + playground canonicals (translation cache re-translated exactly the 15 changed strings); manage rerun 1 passed/1 warned with cached-token zero-click auth. Leftovers on Ian: delete the stale `api.omegajs.dev` pending claim (console, 10s), consent screen now fails "invalid argument" post-org-propagation (friction #29 — the step must pass an authorizing-user-owned supportEmail).
- **Manage-cycle LIVE acceptance MET — the firebase service completed against omegajs-playground, ITW-authorized (checkpoint 100b, 2026-07-11 night).** Ian pasted the OAuth client creds, the account topology got settled mid-flight (the playground was personal-account-owned from cp96b's CLI provisioning; Ian added `itw.creative.works@gmail.com` as owner — his migration direction — and authorized as ITW), and the run delivered: **Spark → Blaze via the ported billing ensure-step** (Main Billing Account attached), 8 APIs enabled + compute deploy roles, project renamed OMEGA, service-account key minted into `.omega/secrets/` + `functions/`, Realtime Database + storage bucket created, Identity Platform configured (email/password, email privacy, anon auto-delete, password policy, authorized domains), FCM enabled, and the sdk-config step healing `omega.json5` live via the comment-preserving writeback (`authDomain` → brand-domain form, `databaseURL` added). 1 passed / 1 warned, 2m36s. Warnings, each honest: OAuth consent screen needs an org (gmail-owned project — manual link), Google sign-in provider + VAPID keys are one-time console/interactive steps, hosting domain claim + firestore PITR hit long-op handling bugs. **Friction #25–#27 logged from the live evidence** — headline #25: interactive OAuth mints a fresh ~2-min loopback URL PER ATTEMPT (run 1 burned 3 URLs and timed out while the only human-visible link was already dead; needs one-auth-session-per-run). The functions deploy proof is unlocked by Blaze and awaits Ian naming it (auto-mode classifier gates deploys he didn't ask for — correctly).
- **Manage-cycle dogfood — first real `omega-manager manage` run against omega-brand (checkpoint 100, 2026-07-11).** The dry-run walks all 25 services in dependency order with honest per-service skip reasons (creds/config gates) — the wiring map for the manage half of the stack. Two finds fixed: the testing service's working-tree check clipped the first character of the first reported path (whole-stdout trim before the fixed-offset porcelain slice; parser extracted as `parseWorkingTree` + regression test), and the firebase service now derives `projectId` from `cloud.config.projectId` when `firebase.projectId` is absent — framework-first brands enter the manage cycle without hand-editing. omega-brand configures `firebase.billingAccount` (ITW Main Billing Account; private repo, ID unusable without IAM) so the ported billing ensure-step performs the Spark→Blaze upgrade the moment credentials land; the LIVE firebase run waits on Ian (GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID/SECRET into the brand .env + the first-run OAuth consent click). Manager suite 584 (+2).

### Added
- **Advertising is role-keyed config — the last ITW ad-server hardcodes die (checkpoint 105, C4).** New `advertising` shared section (schema-known, in `SHARED_SECTIONS`): `advertising.providers.google-adsense.{client, display-slot, in-article-slot, in-feed-slot, multiplex-slot}` + `advertising.providers.inhouse.serverUrl` — presence-driven like analytics; the section was previously consumed by templates but completely unschema'd. Every consumer respelled to the providers path (adsense include, head gate, foot chrome comment, blog post.js). **vert.js de-ITW'd**: the hardcoded `promo-server.itwcreativeworks.com` fallback URL, its message-origin allowlist entry, and the `brand.id === 'promo-server'` dev special-case are GONE — the in-house ad server comes from `advertising.providers.inhouse.serverUrl` (unset → in-house ads honestly disabled), message origins derive from the configured URL, and the generalized dev rule is "the brand that HOSTS the ad server serves its own /verts locally" (serverUrl host == brand.url host). `omega migrate`'s config-convert now lifts legacy flat `advertising.<provider>` into the providers shape (the ports-site recipe fixture models the migrated form). The arc's "ad TYPE param" item was already live (`data-ad-type`: display/in-article/in-feed/multiplex/custom — cp63-era port). Tests: config 95 (+1 schema pin), web 102 (+4: vert/template source guards, config→rendered-unit engine proof, no-config → no-markup); manager 584 green; omega-brand build unchanged (65 pages, 0 deprecations).
- **C4 opens: cross-target design tokens — ONE `--omega-*` sheet for all three UI targets (checkpoint 104; survey + slicing in [plans/c4-cross-target.md](plans/c4-cross-target.md)).** The devkit vendor tool gains a **declared-assets channel**: package.json `omega.vendorAssets` (`[{ package, from, to }]`) copies files/dirs from a sibling package into the host's dist on every prepare — the non-JS twin of module vendoring (fails loud on missing sources; dist-escape guarded; runs even when no dist JS references @omega.js). Desktop + extension declare the web package's token sheet (`core/css/tokens/_index.scss` → `dist/assets/css/tokens/`), their scss entries emit it BEFORE the theme (mirroring web's main.scss order), and both ship a first real consumer: `body { accent-color: var(--omega-accent) }`. **Vendor scan hardened along the way**: a dist reference to a publishable @omega.js package that isn't a runtime dependency now fails loud with guidance (`VENDORABLE_PACKAGES` allowlist: devkit/config/account) — previously the tool silently attempted to fold the ENTIRE package into the host and exploded on its transitive deps mid-rewrite, leaving a part-rewritten dist behind prepare-package's non-blocking after-hook (the first cp104 prepare produced exactly that; found because the hook's one-line failure was chased, not shrugged off). Proofs: plain `npm install` vendors both sheets byte-identical; omega-brand's extension AND desktop app builds carry `--omega-ground` + the accent rule in their built bundles. Suites: devkit 186 (+4), desktop 764+5skip (+3: sheet contract, entry order, full entry compile), extension 102 (+3). Framework READMEs gain the tokens section.
- **App-shell mechanics — the skin-independent backend/admin shell (checkpoint 103, C3 systems).** New [packages/web/core/css/shell/_index.scss](packages/web/core/css/shell/_index.scss): structural regions (`.omega-shell__sidebar/topbar/main/scrim`, topbar start/end slots) and states — desktop rail collapse (`data-shell-collapsed`; `.omega-shell__label` text hides in the rail), mobile drawer below 1200px with scrim (`data-shell-open`; matches classy's xl cutover), and a `.omega-shell--locked` variant whose main never scrolls (calendar/studio pages). Geometry ONLY: every painted value is a `--omega-*` token, dimensions are `--omega-shell-*` custom properties, motion respects `prefers-reduced-motion`, and it's ONE markup tree — classy's Bootstrap shell duplicates the entire nav for its mobile offcanvas. New `core/js/core/app-shell.js` (wired into main.js): declarative controls (`[data-shell-toggle="collapse|drawer"]`, `[data-shell-dismiss]`), persisted collapse (`shell.collapsed`), Escape closes the drawer, `aria-expanded` synced, `omega.uj().appShell` API, inert on shell-less pages. **PurgeCSS gains its first safelist** (`/omega-shell/`) — the state attributes are stamped client-side, so the content scan can never see them; without it the mechanics silently vanish from every production bundle (caught in-checkpoint: the first omega-brand proof build shipped only the `:root` vars). Classy untouched; the D10 skin's layouts adopt the contract (documented at the top of the sheet + README "App shell (C3)"). Proofs: toy theme expresses the shell through the real engine (new `/app` fixture page; classy fall-through pinned), purge keeps the mechanics while still stripping dead rules, omega-brand live build 65 pages / 0 deprecations with ALL shell selectors surviving purge. Web suite 98 (+7).
- **Two-tier theming mechanics — consumers restyle OR fully replace the theme without forking (checkpoint 102, C3 systems).** New `resolveThemeLayers` in `packages/web/src/layers.js` is the ONE theme-chain composer (build/engine/dev had three copies): `<consumer>/themes/<id>` now beats the packaged theme for the same id, so a brand ships a FULL theme in its own repo (tier 2) — layouts it doesn't cover fall through to the classy base, `omega dev` watches consumer theme dirs, and the legacy `themes/<id>/…` layout spellings alias for consumer-local ids too (the alias table previously only knew packaged ids). `buildAssets` takes explicit `themeRoots` (a consumer theme dir can't be recognized by its parent directory). Tier 1 — the documented-but-untested consumer `main.scss` path — is now pinned: the entry wins the layered lookup, `@use 'omega:main' with (…)` pulls AND configures the chain below (core's `@forward` carries theme variables), consumer rules land last, warning-free; `layeredFileImporter` exported for the test seam. Proofs: toy consumer-local theme renders `/pricing` through the engine while uncovered pages fall through to classy; omega-brand's stock build byte-identical behavior (65 pages, 0 deprecations, ramp intact). Web suite 91 (+3). README gains the two-tier guide.
- **C3 systems track opens: the core design-token layer (checkpoint 101, D2/D6/D10 plumbing).** New [packages/web/core/css/tokens/_index.scss](packages/web/core/css/tokens/_index.scss): the `--omega-*` custom-property contract every theme consumes (color/radius/motion slots; values are neutral scaffolding until Ian's cp99 board notes land) — light-first, token-level dark redefinition under `prefers-color-scheme`, and `data-bs-theme` attribute overrides beating the OS in both directions (the appearance.js stamp; renames when classy retires). Emitted before the theme in main.scss so themes override at equal specificity. **`brand.color` now drives the accent family everywhere**: the engine derives a ramp via new `src/brand-tokens.js` (dependency-free hex→HSL math; hover/active lightness shifts that brighten near-black accents and deepen the rest; WCAG-luminance-picked on-accent ink; subtle/ring alphas) and head.html emits it inline AFTER the css bundles so the brand wins the cascade; invalid/missing color → null → the placeholder stands (values regenerate from parsed channels — config strings never reach the style block verbatim). **Friction #16 fixed for real**: the ×10 build spam was Dart Sass warning that silencing `mixed-decls` is now obsolete (the deprecation graduated) — dropped from the silence list, and the token sheet is test-pinned to ZERO deprecations (the bar for all new core css). Shared mini-fixture builder extracted to `test/lib/build.js` (pricing tests adopt it; per-file output namespaces for concurrent `node --test`). Proofs: omega-brand build exits 0 with 0 deprecation lines (was 10+), built head carries `--omega-accent:#3b5bdb` → `--omega-accent-hover:#2647cd` + white ink from the new `brand.color` placeholder, bundle ships all 4 token blocks. Web suite 88 (+7). Also logged friction #24: translation-enabled builds go live-LLM on cold-cache pages (a routine build hung 13 min in the claude provider; `OMEGA_TRANSLATE_ONLY` is the dev workaround) — build-vs-translate default is a queued design call.
- **Deliberate deploys — commits never auto-publish; ONE executor for every surface (checkpoint 98, D13 + D9 addendum; docs/deploys.md).** New `@omega.js/devkit/deploy`: a GitHub REST `workflow_dispatch` client on native fetch (no gh-CLI dependency — Cloud Functions and laptops share the code path; token = GH_TOKEN → GITHUB_TOKEN → `gh auth token`), with `buildDispatch` producing the PLAN (`--dry-run` prints exactly that, sends nothing) and everything injectable (7 unit tests). **Scaffolded workflows lose their push triggers** — web `build.yml` + extension `publish.yml` now trigger on `workflow_dispatch` + `repository_dispatch: [omega-deploy]` only (desktop was already manual-only — the reference shape); both files are framework-owned overwrites, so existing consumers converge on next `omega setup`. **Extension artifacts → GitHub releases** (D9 addendum 2): the publish workflow attaches `packaged/extension.zip` to an `extension-v<version>` release instead of a 7-day-purged workflow artifact — same durable channel desktop distributes from, and where CMS download links will point. **`omega deploy` is the uniform explicit verb**: web = sync (push triggers nothing) → dispatch `build.yml` (`--dry-run`/`--local`/`--no-sync`; the `file:` guard now applies to real deploys only); extension = same against `publish.yml` (new command); desktop = `--dry-run` prints the dispatch, otherwise delegates to the existing `release` flow (new thin verb); backend = direct `firebase deploy` + NEW `--only` pass-through (`--only hosting` deploys on Spark where functions would demand Blaze). **Content-publish implies deploy**: `POST`/`PUT /admin/post` dispatch the website repo's build on its default branch after committing (shared `dispatch-deploy.js`; `deploy: false` opts out; non-fatal — outcome surfaces as `deployDispatched`). Proofs: omega-brand's committed workflows regenerated trigger-free; `--dry-run` prints the exact POST for all three workflow-backed targets; **LIVE deliberate deploy: `omega deploy --only hosting` → https://omegajs-playground.web.app serves 200** (functions stay Blaze-gated; live Actions runs stay Ian-gated). Suites: devkit 182 (+7; the 94a IPC flake reappeared file-level on e2e-harness — isolation 8/8, still parked), corpus 1225/44/0, desktop 761+5skip, extension 99, web 80, backend self-test 7.
- **Pricing renders from config ALONE — `payment.products` is the only source; the dispersal-era marker convention is dead (checkpoint 97, C2; friction #6/#7).** New engine composer (`packages/web/src/pricing.js`): `site.pricing` derives from the catalog — subscription plans in config order, `type: 'one-time'` products in their OWN section (single price, "Buy now", no billing cadence — #7), billing toggle only when both cadences exist, an HONEST savings badge computed from real prices (omega-brand: "up to 17% off"), and a feature-comparison matrix where tiers inherit earlier plans' features. Surfaces as `resolved.pricing`; consumer page frontmatter still overrides presentation (hero, `faqs`, `social_proof`, `pricing.enterprise`/`promo`/`guarantee`/`price_per_unit`). Optional product presentation fields: `tagline`, `popular`, `url`, `features [{ id, name, icon, definition, value }]` (value falls back to `limits[id]`, `-1` → Unlimited). **All three theme pricing layouts rewritten with ZERO fictional defaults** — the fake Basic/Plus/Pro/Max catalog, "5M customers" social proof, fake testimonials, generic FAQs, always-on WELCOME15 promo banner, and unconditional Enterprise card are GONE (#6); empty catalog → explicit `#pricing-empty` state + an `omega build` warning; free plan (no prices) anchors to `/signup`. Checkout JS drives frequency off `data-plan-type` (subscription vs one-time) instead of hardcoded plan ids. `core/pricing/resolve-plan.html` retired (the composer owns the math); a slim `price-per-unit.html` include remains. **Marker sweep: 167 packaged files** (defaults/pages, core blueprints + module, all three themes) stripped of `### ALL PAGES ###`-family section markers (inert YAML comments since the Eleventy port — dead protocol, live syntax), plus the three emitters that still wrote them (manager devlog `renderPostFile`, backend admin post templates ×2; backend dist re-vendored); a contract guard test pins the convention dead in packaged content (the ports-site fixture keeps its markers deliberately — consumers' legacy files stay tolerated). Blueprint slim: `blueprint/pricing.html` drops its fictional SoftwareApplication `features` list. Proofs: omega-brand live build (exactly 2 purchase buttons — premium subscription + launch-kit one-time, zero fiction strings, computed 17% badge, trial CTA) and sandbox-brand's real 4-product catalog through the engine (its website app is a client harness with no Eleventy pages). NEW friction #21 logged: foot.html's SoftwareApplication schema fabricates aggregateRating (hash-derived 4.8/4.9 + fake 200k–1M count) — C3/SEO territory, untouched here. Suites: web 80 (14 new: composer unit + 3-theme render/empty-state + marker guard), config 94, manager 582.
- **omega-brand goes live on a REAL Firebase project — omegajs-playground (checkpoint 96b, Ian's go 2026-07-11).** Provisioned on Ian's CLI auth: project `omegajs-playground` (omega-playground was taken globally), web app registered, Firestore created (nam5), real Admin-SDK service-account key minted straight into `functions/service-account.json` (gitignored — verified invisible to git). omega-brand's `cloud.config` now carries the real console values (public by design; demo-omega remains a comment-documented offline fallback). **Live setup proof: 39/39, demo gates stepping aside for real** — the real SA accepted, `.firebaserc` rewritten from config (demo-omega → omegajs-playground), firestore indexes SYNCED against the live API (the old 403-poisoner path, now legitimate), and 5 marketing campaigns seeded into and READ BACK from real Firestore; rerun idempotent. **One real bug caught by the swap**: `self.projectId` is snapshotted from `.firebaserc` at setup boot, so the consistency fix's rewrite left every later check's `isDemoProject` gate on demo semantics — check 39 printed ✓ while silently skipping live seeding. The fix now refreshes the shared context (`self.projectId`/`projectUrl`/`firebaseRC`) after rewriting; new self-test group `project-id-refresh` pins the contract (BEM self-test 7/7). Functions DEPLOY still needs Blaze (Ian-gated, matters at D13); hosting/firestore/auth run on Spark.
- **Translation overhaul — config-driven, provider-selectable, committed cache (checkpoint 96, Ian directive 2026-07-11; docs/translation.md).** New shared `translation` config section (schema-known, in `SHARED_SECTIONS`): `{ enabled, default, languages, provider, model, exclude }` — languages gate the feature, codes validate against the new language SSOT. **One engine for every framework** (`@omega.js/devkit/translate`): JSON-array batch protocol (25/batch) with an alignment sentinel appended to EVERY batch, validation retries, whitespace preservation, and two providers — `claude` (default; rides the LOCAL Claude Code install via the Agent SDK, zero API keys) and `chatgpt` (OpenAI Responses API, `OPENAI_API_KEY` via the .env cascade). Claude sessions run hermetic (`settingSources: []`, custom system prompt, no maxTurns cap) — a canary caught the user's stop-hook text leaking into output and `maxTurns: 1` erroring AFTER the reply arrived. **The cache is part of the repo** (`<app>/translations/{lang}/<ns>.json`, `sha256(source)[:12]` → translation): one source edit = one re-translation, warm clones/CI build fully-translated output with NO credentials (kills the cp64 parked finding — BXM fresh clones burning live calls), and a hand-edited translation VALUE sticks until its source changes. Replaces BOTH legacy caches (UJM's `cache-uj-translation` GitHub-branch flow + BXM's gitignored whole-file-hash `.cache/`). **Web port (stub dies)**: `omega translate` is real, `omega build` auto-translates when enabled — `dist/{lang}/` copies with localized `<html lang/dir>` (RTL-aware), canonical/og:url/og:locale, internal links rewritten to `/{lang}/`, hreflang + og:locale:alternate stitched into copies AND originals for exactly the pages translated (hreflang never lies), system excludes (auth/checkout/legal/404/socials/admin/…) + `translation.exclude`, `data-omega-no-translate` opt-out, `OMEGA_TRANSLATE_ONLY` page filter; collector fixes vs UJM (aria-describedby/labelledby ID refs no longer translated; `value` only on button-type inputs). **Extension refresh**: per-KEY incremental messages (unique strings only), `dist/_locales` COMPOSED from EN source + cache (always complete), CWS limits ride the prompt + violations warn, description per-language as committed markdown with an `<!-- omega:source hash -->` marker (package task ships store assets from it), hardcoded 16-language list deleted. Wizard seeds a commented `translation` block; `# OPENAI_API_KEY=` placeholders in web/extension env templates. Devkit vendor tool learned directory modules (`translate/index.js`). Tests: devkit 176 (+12), config 94 (+1, enum + SHARED_SECTIONS pins respelled), web 65 (+4 full-pipeline fixture), extension 99 (+3), manager 582. Live canary on omega-brand via local Claude: `/about` → Spanish (102 strings, rerun = 0 calls) + extension messages/description → Spanish (brand name preserved, emoji intact), both idempotent, artifacts committed.
- **C1 first-run polish, slice 3 — cross-framework consistency + de-ITW (checkpoint 95c, closes the C1 arc slice).** Six friction findings die; the log stands at 18/22 fixed (open: #6/#7 → C2/cp96, #15 Node story, #16 sass → C3). **Gulpfile hoisting bug (#14, the cross-brand 🔴)**: EM + BXM consumer scripts hardcoded `./node_modules/@omega.js/<fw>/dist/gulp/main.js` — broken in EVERY brand monorepo (workspace hoisting moves the framework up). Both frameworks now scaffold a consumer `gulpfile.js` shim (`module.exports = require('@omega.js/<fw>/gulp')` — new `./gulp` package export; resolution-safe via the require climb) and sync a plain `"gulp": "gulp"` script; existing consumers converge on next setup (script sync overwrites, defaults copy-if-missing adds the shim). Proven on omega-brand's extension app — the exact cp93 "No gulpfile found" case now builds through `npm run build`. **Dep home rule (#12)**: EM/BXM's updateManager/checkLocality/migrate accept the framework in `dependencies` OR `devDependencies` (5 sites), matching web/backend. **Honest 405 (#17)**: the backend middleware checks route-file existence BEFORE require (a route module's own broken import can no longer masquerade as "route missing"), and a route directory with no file for the request's method answers `405 Method not allowed` instead of 500 "Unable to load route"; corpus gains `method-mismatch-is-405` (GET on the POST-only uuid route). **ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE scrub (#17c)**: every EM Electron spawn site deletes the var from its child env (serve task, electron + boot test runners — matching launch.js), so paths that bypass the CLI/gulp boundary strips can't boot Electron as plain node (the `app.setName` crash from editor-spawned shells). **EM identity de-ITW (#18)**: `app.appId` derives from the BRAND — reverse-domain of `brand.url` (`https://www.somiibo.com` → `com.somiibo`), else `app.<brand.id>` — and copyright defaults to `© {YEAR}, <brand.name>`; build-config's `com.itwcreativeworks.app` / ITW-copyright literals are gone (test pins updated to the new derivation). **BXM branding (#19)**: the extension defaults' `config/messages.json` carries `[ site.brand.name ]` tokens resolved by the scaffold's site-token transform from the RESOLVED config (brand root included) — a fresh brand's built extension is named after the BRAND, not "Ultimate Browser Extension"; omega-brand's committed copy converged to OMEGA. Gates: backend parse-audit 416 + self-test, corpus (now 1225 with the 405 test), desktop suite (pins respelled), extension suite; dists re-vendored ×3.
- **C1 first-run polish, slice 2 — demo-* backend setup completes, start to finish (checkpoint 95b).** Kills friction #8–#11 (and most of parked 1.4b): a fresh emulator-only brand's `omega setup` now runs **39/39 checks green and exits 0**, and the emulator boots first try. **demo-* awareness (#8, three heads)**: `BaseTest` gains `isDemoProject` (`projectId` starts with `demo-`); the `firestore-indexes-synced` check skips live sync outright (its live pull shell-redirected the 403 error text INTO firestore.indexes.json), the indexes-FILE check's fix seeds the template's empty shape instead of pulling live — and now also DETECTS an already-poisoned (unparseable) file and rewrites it (the class self-heals; the scratch proof healed one live) — and the `marketing-campaigns-seeded` check (the very last one) no longer opens PRODUCTION Firestore on demo projects, where its first `.get()` threw gRPC `16 UNAUTHENTICATED` and halted setup after 38 green checks. **`database.rules.json` scaffolds (#9)**: `scaffoldConfigs` copies the template (firebase.json references the file; `omega emulator` died ENOENT without it). **Fake service accounts for demo (#10)**: the service-account check's fix generates one (real throwaway RSA key via `node:crypto`, `project_id` = the demo id, admin-SDK-parseable shape) — the runtime only reads the file on its production branch, so emulator-only projects never needed a real credential, just the file. **Precedence flip (#11)**: the resolved config's `cloud.config.projectId` is the SOURCE OF TRUTH everywhere — `project-id-consistency` now rewrites `.firebaserc` FROM config (the old fixer wrote a stale `.firebaserc` value INTO the config, comment-stripping rewrite included; it now uses the comment-preserving `writeConfigValues` for its one remaining config-write case, config-carries-no-id), regenerates the fake SA on demo mismatches, `resolveProjectId()` consults the resolved config first, and `scaffoldConfigs` seeds the app config BEFORE deriving `.firebaserc` — a fresh brand app stamps `demo-<brand id>`, never `demo-project` (95a's live observation, closed). Proofs: backend parse-audit 416 + self-test 6/6, corpus 1224/44/0. **Live acceptance (scratch demo brand, real wizard, deleted after)**: phase 1 scaffolds `.firebaserc (project: demo-scratch-95b)` + database.rules.json; the two-phase nvmrc rerun (friction #15, still open) then lands **39 passed / 0 failed, exit 0** — with the fake SA generated, project-ids consistent, live index sync skipped — a rerun converges (**39/39, zero fixes, zero writes**), and `omega emulator` boots to "All emulators ready" + the post-seed "Emulator ready" marker (53 personas seeded) with a clean teardown. (Two session-shell caveats bypassed for the boot, neither a product issue: an interactive `npx`→npu alias wraps children in Socket Firewall's proxy, whose HTML answer breaks firebase-tools' local REST call to the firestore emulator — invoke the bin directly; and the boot shell must carry the functions `.nvmrc` Node or workers refuse — friction #15's family.) The dispatcher-era caveat that BEM setup "can't complete on emulator-only demo-* projects" is dead; 1.4b's residue (serve's hosting-upstream 403 on demo-*, two-phase nvmrc, emulator-orphan grandchildren) stays parked with C1's #15/#17 family.
- **C1 first-run polish, slice 1 — the brand speaks for itself (checkpoint 95a).** Burns 7 of the friction log's 22 findings, headlined by the systemic one: **layer-aware consumer seeding (#1)**. New `@omega.js/config` seed module (`resolveSeedMode` — thin wrapper over `findBrandRoot` — and `renderBrandAppSeed`, the ONE targets-only seed with the brand-root pointer comment): all four framework setups now branch on it — inside a brand monorepo the app's `config/omega.json5` seeds TARGETS-ONLY and the full-template merge rules turn OFF (web's setup-merge and the extension's every-build merge would otherwise keep re-adding placeholder keys under the slim file); standalone consumers keep the full template. The extension's template also **loses its REAL ITW values** (`ultimate-jekyll`, itwcreativeworks.com images, a live universal-auth Firebase block → `my-brand` placeholders + empty client-config shape). **Wizard seeds (#2/#3/#4)**: scaffolded app package.jsons declare their framework (`devDependencies: { '@omega.js/<fw>': '*' }` — workspace link in-monorepo, `mgr i local` pre-publish, npm post-publish; a failed install of unpublished `@omega.js/*` prints that guidance from the update service), the backend app gains a scaffolded **`functions/package.json`** carrying `@omega.js/backend` in `dependencies` (a Cloud Functions runtime dep — and without it a fresh backend app couldn't even DISPATCH `omega setup` to its framework), backend brands seed `cloud: { provider: 'firebase', config: { projectId: 'demo-<id>' } }` (emulator-bootable out of the box, swap-at-launch comment), and the brand config ships a commented `payment.products` starter catalog (subscription + one-time). **Schema is the required-key oracle (#5)**: the backend's `omega-config` setup check now loads the RESOLVED config (app ← brand root) and validates it against the shared schema instead of demanding every leaf of the fully-populated template (which made `brand.address`/`brand.images` — schema-optional — a guaranteed first-run failure). **The `.env` empty-shadowing class dies (#20), three prongs**: the env cascade skips EMPTY file-layer values (`KEY=`/`KEY=""` = "documented, supplied elsewhere" — only the shell can blank a key), all four framework `_.env` templates ship `# KEY=` commented placeholders instead of `KEY=""` (24 of which had shadowed the brand root's real keys on omega-brand), and devkit's merge protocol understands placeholders (an existing SET value keeps its line in Default — Custom values promote up; empties converge to the placeholder instead of migrating into the user-owned Custom section; idempotent), with omega-manager's disperse uncommenting a placeholder in place when it composes a real value (no duplicate lines). **Extension scaffolds at setup (#13)**: `omega setup` now runs the same defaults-engine pass the build task performs (it previously did nothing — consumers got files at first BUILD). Proofs: config 93 (4 new: seed render/merge-under-brand/mode resolution + cascade empty rules), devkit merge tests 14 (3 new placeholder pins), manager 582 (scaffold/onboard pins updated + cp95a content assertions; disperse pins respelled to placeholders + uncomment-in-place pin; the nudge/handoff manage tests stub framework resolution — pre-publish installs must never hit the registry), web 61 (+brand-app seed test), extension 95 (+brand-mode build-task test), desktop 766 (+brand-mode copyDefaults test), backend parse-audit 416 + self-test 6/6 (re-merge pin respelled to placeholder form). **Live proof (scratch brand, real wizard, in-tree, deleted after)**: onboard → 10 files incl. functions/package.json; web `omega setup` seeds targets-only + `omega build` renders **"My Brand" ×0, "Scratch 95a" on 64/65 pages** — cp93's headline bug dead at the root; backend setup from functions/ reaches **14/15 with check 14 "using proper config/omega.json5" ✓ on the slim seed** (the one failure = service-account, cp95b's #10); extension setup scaffolds the full interior + slim seed live. Corpus fallout, fixed in the same slice: the sandbox's committed `functions/.env` carried 21 `KEY=""` empties and the email-preferences suite had been signing with `''` AS the HMAC key (the cascade's empty rule surfaced it) — `UNSUBSCRIBE_HMAC_KEY` now carries a real sandbox value and the remaining empties converged to placeholders; **corpus 1224/44/0 exact parity** (an intermediate run's two cron-wait timeouts traced to TWO generations of orphaned demo-sandbox-brand emulator jars squatting classic ports with `--functions_emulator 5001` cross-talk — killed; more evidence for parked 1.4b, not a regression). Also observed for cp95b: `.firebaserc` scaffolds `demo-project` instead of reading the brand's `cloud.config.projectId` (the #11 precedence class). Remaining slices: cp95b (BEM demo-* warts #8–#11), cp95c (cross-framework consistency + de-ITW #12/#14/#17/#17c/#18/#19).
- **C5 CLOSED — brand-ROOT test dispatch (checkpoint 94b).** The other half of Ian's grammar: `npx omega test` AT A BRAND ROOT fans out over the brand's apps instead of falling to an arbitrary hoist-winner framework CLI. **omega-bin learns brand roots**: the dispatcher's walk now returns the nearest CONTEXT — an app's framework (unchanged, nearest wins; a standalone consumer with its own config still dispatches as an app) or a brand root (`config/omega.json5` that isn't itself an app-of-brand — the stdlib twin of `@omega.js/config`'s `resolveBrandRoot`, commented as such because the vendored dispatcher must stay dependency-free) — and a brand root resolves `@omega.js/manager/cli` from where it stands and hands over (new `cli-run.js` surface, shared by `bin/omega-manager` which shrank to two lines; bare `omega` = manage and `omega onboard` now also reach the manager at a brand root, strictly better than the old "no app context — running <hoist-winner>" fallback there). **The manager gains the `test` command**: discovers target-mapped apps, partitions targets per C5 — universal targets (bare paths, `framework:`/`omega:`/`mgr:`, `full:`, `project:`/`brand:`) forward to EVERY app verbatim, per-framework ids route ONLY to the owning app, unknown prefixes warn + drop with bare-everywhere fallback when nothing valid remains (scope.js parity) — resolves each app's OWN framework `omega` bin via the node_modules climb (exports-restricted packages break `require.resolve`) and spawns sequentially with cwd = the app dir, streaming output, per-app summary, aggregate exit 1. Flags deliberately don't fan out (documented — flagged runs are app-level invocations). **`FRAMEWORK_IDS` becomes the id-alias SSOT** in `@omega.js/devkit/test/scope`: the four framework runners now read their own `frameworkAliases` entry from it (desktop + extension runner configs, backend runner, web test command; dists re-vendored ×3) and the manager's routing reads the same map — the grammar can't drift. The update service's spawn helper hoisted to shared `manager/src/lib/run-command.js` (targets.js imports it). **Friction 17b closed**: `em:` from the brand root runs the desktop framework suite (751 passing / 5 skipped from consumer context — the boot layer stays self-test-only by design, per docs/testing.md). **NEW friction #20, found by the first live fan-out**: the composed backend `functions/.env` ships every key as `KEY=""` — 24 empty values, each SHADOWING the brand-root `.env` in the cascade (app layer wins), so the run died "Missing backend manager key" with the wizard-minted key one layer up; the `.env` flavor of systemic finding #1, queued for C1 (omega-brand's copy hand-fixed to commented placeholders). Docs: [docs/testing.md](docs/testing.md) "Brand root" section, manager README (module map + tests paragraph). Tests: devkit 161 (7 new omega-bin brand-context cases incl. run() dispatching to a fake installed manager + the FRAMEWORK_IDS↔FRAMEWORKS consistency pin), manager 582 (9 new fan-out tests over a staged fixture brand whose fake framework bins record every spawn). Live proofs at real brand roots: omega-brand bare = **4/4 apps, project-only** (65-page web build+smoke, backend emulator boot with ZERO corpus tests dragged in, desktop/extension 0-test clean, exit 0 — via the backend-bin hoist winner, proving cross-package handoff); `em:` = **desktop app ONLY** (751 passing); sandbox `backend:routes/general` = **backend app ONLY, exactly the 5 general-route tests**; monorepo root stays null-context → host fallback. Gates: web 60, extension 94, desktop 760, backend self-test, sandbox corpus 1224/44/0.
- **C5 test-scoping DX, per-app layer (checkpoint 94a)** — ONE scoping grammar for `omega test` across every framework, per Ian's 2026-07-11 syntax: **bare runs are PROJECT-ONLY** (a brand app's `npx omega test` never drags the framework suite in), the framework suite is explicit (`framework:` / `omega:` / `mgr:` / per-framework ids incl. legacy `em:`/`bxm:`/`ujm:`), `full:` = both sources, `brand:`/`project:` = explicit project aliases, paths after any prefix scope within that source, unknown prefixes warn instead of silently matching nothing. The parser is NEW `@omega.js/devkit/test/scope` (10 tests) with ONE self-test exception: inside a framework package itself, bare still means "my suite" — so every package's `npm test` is unchanged. Adopted by the devkit runner-core (desktop + extension in one edit), the backend runner (whose per-source filter also simplified — prefixes now stripped centrally, closing parked 1.2a's mismatch class), and web's test command (which gains `framework:` access to @omega.js/web's own suite from any consumer). The sandbox corpus scripts now SAY what they mean: `npx omega test framework:` — and prove parity (1224 passing / 44 skipped / 0). Canonical doc: [docs/testing.md](docs/testing.md); all four framework READMEs updated. Gates: devkit 153, web 60, extension 94, desktop 760 (exit 0), backend self-test, corpus — all green; live acceptance on omega-brand: bare = build+smoke only, `framework:` = web 60/60 from the consumer app. Remaining C5 half = cp94b: brand-ROOT dispatch (omega-bin brand-root detection → manager `test` fan-out over apps).
- **The OMEGA brand is born (checkpoint 93)** — the dogfood arc's opener: [apps/omega-brand](apps/omega-brand) scaffolded by the REAL onboarding wizard (one non-interactive shot: 9 files, four targets, config validates), app interiors filled by each framework's own setup, and **all four targets proven live**: web (65 virtual default pages in 1.75s, dev server on the N7-allocated :4000, pricing rendering the config catalog the moment `payment.products` existed), backend (demo-omega emulator: "All emulators ready", 10 `omega_*` functions loaded, router + validation answering with precise semantic errors), extension (MV3 strict-JSON packaged for chromium/firefox/opera from one build), desktop (webpack main+preload bundles; real Electron boot — window-manager built the main window). The checkpoint's REAL deliverable is [plans/dogfood-friction-log.md](plans/dogfood-friction-log.md) — **21 findings**, headlined by the systemic one: **every framework's consumer config seed duplicates shared sections at the app layer and SHADOWS the brand config** (the first build rendered "My Brand" 22× and zero "OMEGA"; the extension seed even carries real ITW `ultimate-jekyll` values — de-ITW miss). Also caught: a real cross-brand bug (EM/BXM consumer scripts hardcode `./node_modules/...` gulpfile paths that break under workspace hoisting — i.e. in every brand monorepo), BEM demo-* warts in full color (live index-sync 403 text WRITTEN INTO firestore.indexes.json; scaffolded firebase.json references a database.rules.json that setup never creates; the config auto-fixer trusts stale .firebaserc over brand config and strips comments), the wizard/BEM required-key mismatch (address/images demanded but never collected), and hardcoded fake Basic/Premium pricing when products are absent (C2's case in one sentence). Brand configs in the dogfood app are hand-slimmed to targets-only with pointers to the findings; placeholders awaiting Ian: domain `omegajs.dev`, address, product catalog, desktop appId. — [plans/dogfood-arc.md](plans/dogfood-arc.md): read-only survey of every arc input + the checkpoint slicing (cp93 scaffold the OMEGA brand in-monorepo on current classy → cp94 C5 test-scoping DX → cp95 C1 first-run polish → cp96 C2 pricing/blueprints → cp97 D13 deliberate deploys → cp98+ C3 cross-target redesign fronted by a design brief for Ian → C4 sharing → arc close cuts the template repo). Survey deltas worth knowing: **C2 is further along than the spec assumed** (`payment.products` schema + `site.web_manager.payment.products` composition + classy pricing loop already live; what remains is frontmatter slimming and retiring the dispersal-era `### ALL PAGES ###` marker convention); **D13's target is confirmed alive** (scaffolded build.yml deploys gh-pages on push; `omega deploy` = build + `npu sync`); sandbox brand covers 2 targets, so the four-target omega-brand is new coverage. Calls made (flagged in the plan): omega-brand lives in-monorepo for the arc, C5 jumps ahead of C1, D13's live workflow proof stays Ian-gated with the other runner-minute spends.
- **Brand-account provisioning ownership (checkpoint 91) — the auto-created admin accounts are fully owner-defined, and the framework gains its first OWNER HOOK.** Closes the Ian-2026-07-10 interjection ([plans/brand-account-provisioning.md](plans/brand-account-provisioning.md)): omega-manager created 4 hardcoded company accounts with a personal password formula compiled into source; now every piece is the owner's. **(a) `support@{domain}` stays the built-in default** (the manager DEFAULTS entry — already true since the cp52 port, now schema-known: the shared config schema gains the `account` section). **(b) Personal/company admin accounts are company config**: `account.admins` in the COMPANY omega.json5 rides the existing company→brand layer and REPLACES the default whole (array merge semantics) — ITW's three personal accounts become 3 lines in Ian's company config, zero framework code. **(c) NEW owner-hooks system in `@omega.js/config`** ([src/hooks.js](packages/config/src/hooks.js)): `config/hooks/<call-site-path>.js`, **nested to mirror the invoking structure** (Ian's directive — `account/password`, never flat name-mangling), resolved brand root → company root via the company stamp; home is `config/` — VERSIONED by default alongside the other owner-authored inputs (cp91b, Ian's catch: the first cut used gitignored machine-owned `.omega/`, where a hook lost on a fresh clone would have silently rotated passwords onto the seed channel); plain CJS `module.exports = fn`; absent → null (caller defaults), broken (unloadable / non-function / bad return) → **throws, never silent fallback**. First hook point: **`account/password`** — `({ email, domain, apex, brand }) => password`, so Ian's keyboard-column formula moves into HIS company tree and out of framework source forever. **Password resolution is now per-account, strongest first**: `OMEGA_ACCOUNT_PASSWORD__<EMAIL>` env pin (email uppercased, non-alphanumerics → `_`; rides the D15 .env cascade so brand or company .env can pin it) → the owner hook → the existing HMAC `ACCOUNT_PASSWORD_SEED` derivation, with the seed now **lazy**: generated + persisted only when an account actually falls through to it (an env/hook-covered or marketing-only brand never grows a seed in its .env — previously every setup wrote one). Non-default channels are called out in the run log (`· via env OMEGA_…` / `· via hook <file>`), passwords themselves never printed. **Onboarding gains the accounts step**: the wizard shows the list the brand inherits (company config, else the built-in default) — keep it and NOTHING is written (the source layer keeps owning it, SSOT), or customize and the entries land as `account.admins` in the brand's omega.json5. Docs: [docs/config.md](docs/config.md) "Owner hooks", manager README account row + onboard line, config schema description. Proofs: config 89/89 (6 new hooks tests: nested resolution, brand-over-company precedence, absent-null, non-function throw, broken-load throw, malformed-path rejection), manager 573/573 (7 new channel tests incl. REAL hook files — brand hook formula over email/domain/apex/brand, company hook through the stamp, env-beats-hook, invalid-hook-return fails the service naming the file, env/hook/marketing-only runs never write a seed; onboard: inherit-writes-nothing + customize-writes-admins driven through the real interactive wizard).
- **N7 COMPLETE — port auto-allocation, desktop + extension + stragglers (checkpoint 90): every fixed dev port in the stack now allocates or follows the resolved map.** **Desktop URL getters read the env channel**, mirroring @omega.js/backend's exactly: `getFunctionsUrl` (`OMEGA_FUNCTIONS_PORT`), `getApiUrl` (`OMEGA_HTTPS_PORT` → `https://localhost:<port>` for `mgr serve`'s mkcert proxy, else `OMEGA_HOSTING_PORT` → plain http — reconciling the long-standing desktop-vs-backend/client scheme divergence), `getWebsiteUrl` (`OMEGA_WEBSITE_PORT`) — and **`getWebsiteUrl` now says `http://localhost:4000` in BOTH backend and desktop**: the `https://` form was a browsersync-mkcert-era assumption that no current dev server speaks (`omega dev` is plain http), so dev signin flows built from it would TLS-fail. **Livereload allocates per target**: desktop and extension `serve` each resolve `livereload` through the allocator (classic 35729, bump when taken — two targets of one brand land on distinct ports instead of colliding; config `ports.livereload` pins), publishing via `OMEGA_LIVERELOAD_PORT` BEFORE any build bakes it; the extension's `package.js` hardcoded-`35729` fallback now rides the getter, so a bumped server and the baked client config always agree. **CDP allocates when requested** (`OMEGA_CDP_PORT` set → probe/bump instead of the old "try `OMEGA_CDP_PORT=9223` manually" advice; a pre-probed-free port that's dead after boot now correctly reads as "the app didn't enable remote debugging"). **`mgr serve` joins the allocator**: public + internal ports resolve (`--port`/positional pins with a hard busy error; the `checkAndKillBlockingProcesses` kill-the-incumbent pre-check is DELETED), and serve **publishes `{ https, hosting }` in the ports file** — so `omega dev` composes even a bumped serve into the page chrome and @omega.js/client's `getApiUrl` follows; `OMEGA_HOSTING_PORT` rides into the functions env; the file clears on child close. **Stripe webhook forwarding** (dormant — it short-circuits "coming soon") now takes an explicit plain-http target from each caller (serve: the internal port under https / public otherwise; `mgr emulator`: the RESOLVED hosting port, passed after boot instead of before; `mgr stripe`: live ports file → firebase.json → classic) — replacing a firebase.json re-derivation that aimed plain http at the TLS proxy's port. **The manager's Google-OAuth loopback binds `listen(0)`** (RFC 8252 — Google's desktop-app client accepts any localhost port) instead of pinning 9876. The extension manifest's dev-website origin (`http://localhost:4000`) is documented inline as build-time-baked — packaged artifacts can't probe (the stated N7 non-goal). Docs: [docs/config.md](docs/config.md) cp90 bullet, backend README ports paragraph, [plans/n7-port-allocation.md](plans/n7-port-allocation.md) final status. Proofs: desktop 758 (new env-branch getter tests; renderer pins updated in real Electron), extension 94, manager 565, backend parse-audit 416 + self-test 6/6, corpus 1224/44/0, e2e 18/18, live `mgr serve` ports-file publish/retract proof.
- **N7 port auto-allocation, web + client + sandbox (checkpoint 89) — the BROWSER now follows the resolved map; `omega dev` moves to the website convention 4000.** `omega dev` allocates its port through the shared allocator: classic **4000** (pre-N7 the Eleventy default was 8080 — colliding with the SAME brand's firestore emulator), `--port` flag or config `ports.website` PINS (busy = hard error naming the pin), otherwise bump-if-taken; it publishes its own `.temp/ports.json` (scaffold gitignore gains `/.temp`) and reads live sibling apps' ports files (brand-root walk; dead-pid leftovers ignored) so a running backend's resolved — possibly bumped — emulator map rides into the page: the engine's `jekyll` global gains `dev` (`null` outside dev) and foot.html bakes `dev: {...}` into the Configuration chrome. **`@omega.js/client` resolves every dev port through the map** with precedence `window.__OMEGA_DEV_PORTS__` (NEW runtime channel — for drivers that learn the map only after the page was built) → `config.dev.ports` (the chrome) → classic fallbacks: emulator connects (`auth`/`firestore`), `getFunctionsUrl` (`functions`), and `getApiUrl` — whose dev scheme is now truthful: mapped `hosting` → `http://127.0.0.1:<port>` (the hosting emulator speaks plain http; pre-cp89 dev-against-emulator was broken because the getter always assumed the https proxy), mapped `https` → `mgr serve`'s mkcert proxy, no map → the classic `https://localhost:5002` serve assumption (unchanged). **Devkit e2e harness**: the pre-flight "default ports free" throw is DELETED (the stack self-allocates), the site port resolves through the allocator, the backend's published map is read after the post-seed ready marker, and new **`preparePage(page)`** wires console capture + injects the map per page (`evaluateOnNewDocument` — survives reloads); the sandbox fixture's `__omega.api` now rides `manager.getApiUrl()`, so the e2e covers the client's map branch in a real browser (e2e = 18 steps now, the vestigial check step is gone). **Live proof**: sandbox e2e **18/18 with auth 9099 + firestore 8080 + hosting 5002 + site 4600 ALL squatted** — the emulator bumped (hosting :5003), the browser connected to `auth :9100` / `firestore :8081` via the injected map, the whole subscribe→cancel→refund lifecycle flowed through the bumped hosting, and shutdown retracted every artifact (plus a clean-classics run and a site-only-squat run, both 18/18).
