Proofs: devkit 142 (2 new), web 60 (6 new), client 81 (4 new), backend self-test, the three e2e runs.
- **N7 port auto-allocation, foundation + backend (checkpoint 88) — two brands' dev stacks run concurrently; the second BUMPS instead of killing the first.** New `@omega.js/config` ports module ([src/ports.js](packages/config/src/ports.js)): `CLASSIC_PORTS` (the historical defaults — functions 5001, hosting 5002, firestore 8080, auth 9099, …), `resolvePorts({ wanted, pins, claimed })` (bind-probe on 127.0.0.1; free → keep, taken → +1 until free; a shared claimed-set stops two names landing on one port; explicit config-`ports`-section pins NEVER bump — busy pin throws naming it), ports-file helpers (`.temp/ports.json`, pid-stamped, dead-pid leftovers ignored, cleared on clean shutdown) and the `OMEGA_<NAME>_PORT` env channel (`portsToEnv`/`envPort`). Schema gains the optional `ports` section. **Backend adopted end-to-end**: the THREE duplicate `loadEmulatorPorts` copies (emulator.js / test.js / firebase-init.js) collapsed into `setup-tests/emulator-config.js`; `startEmulators()` allocates at boot — defaults-free runs spawn byte-identically to before, bumped runs materialize a gitignored `firebase.resolved.json` (full firebase.json with only emulator ports patched, same dir so relative paths hold) and boot firebase-tools with `--config`; the resolved map publishes via the ports file + env (functions workers inherit; `getFunctionsUrl`/`getApiUrl`/`getWebsiteUrl` read `OMEGA_*_PORT` → classic); `omega test` prefers a RUNNING emulator's published map and the auto-start path rebuilds the test command AFTER boot (a pre-built one would bake stale ports); the old `checkAndKillBlockingProcesses` call in the emulator boot is GONE (bump replaces kill), and the post-shutdown orphan sweep now takes THIS run's resolved map — sweeping firebase.json defaults after a bumped run would kill the OTHER brand's live emulator (shared hub/storage ports swept only on defaults runs). **Live proof**: sandbox brand + the backend fixture project booted side by side — B bumped `functions 5001→5003` (5002 was A's hosting; the +1 walk skips it), `hosting 5002→5004` (5003 just claimed by B's own functions — claimed-set), booted via firebase.resolved.json, **both hostings HTTP 200 simultaneously, A alive throughout**, clean shutdown cleared both ports files + the resolved config; bonus: A itself had silently bumped firestore/database/pubsub around stale e2e orphans — crash leftovers get absorbed, not fatal. `mgr serve`'s mkcert proxy + stripe-forward stay classic-port until cp89/90 (client's hardcoded 5002 getter moves in cp89). Docs: [docs/config.md](docs/config.md) "Port auto-allocation", backend README, [plans/n7-port-allocation.md](plans/n7-port-allocation.md). Proofs: config 83/83 (12 new), backend parse-audit 416 + self-test 6/6, corpus 1224/44/0, e2e 19/19, the live concurrency proof.
- **Zod route schemas, engine + first cohort (checkpoint 76) — route schema modules can export zod, with the powertools contract preserved to the byte.** New `@omega.js/backend` `src/manager/helpers/schema-zod.js`: `fields` builders (`f.object/string/number/boolean/array/passthrough/any/multi/field` — one-to-one with the declarative node options: `default`/`value`/`min`/`max`/`required`/`clean`/`sanitize`, unknown options throw) whose per-field `z.preprocess` pipes replicate the powertools resolution semantics exactly — coerce-never-reject (single-type mismatches run `powertools.force`: unparseable string→`1`, `"a,b"`→array, null→type-zero), multi-type replace-with-default, min/max clamp/truncate incl. the `min||0` negative-clamp quirk, undefined-only `required` (bool or `(assistant, settings, options)` fn) with the identical 400 message, unknown-key strip, absent/invalid parents resolving nested leaf defaults. `Settings.resolve` detects a zod export (duck-typed `safeParse` + `_zod`, consumer-copy safe) and routes to `resolveZodSchema`; raw-zod exports opt into zod-native reject-with-400 semantics (documented). Two deliberate fixes over powertools, asserted in the parity suite: non-empty object/array defaults come back **clean** (powertools injects `types`/`min`/`max` junk keys into them on the wire — live today on `admin/notification`) and **cloned** (powertools returns the schema's default by reference). Proof: `test/helpers/schema-zod.js` — a 30-case differential battery + the frozen pre-conversion `test/schema` declarative twin, BOTH engines through the real `Settings.resolve`, byte-diffed. **First cohort converted** (all of `schemas/test/*` + `general/*`, 10 modules incl. the every-option reference `test/schema/post.js`); the existing 30-assertion HTTP suite (`test/routes/test/schema.js`) passes unchanged against the zod-served route. zod ^4.4.3 becomes a direct backend dep. **Second cohort (checkpoint 77): all 16 user/* schemas + the stragglers (brand, content/post, handler/post, special/electron-client, restart) converted** — includes the tricky ones: signup's nested consent groups (resolved shape pinned: omitted consent → `granted: false / text: ''`, exactly what `buildConsentRecord`'s never-downgrade logic consumes; the declarative engine only terminated on those no-`default` nodes because `powertools.defaults` mutates the schema in place before `iterateSchema` walks it — two bugs canceling), api-keys' `array|string` multi-type (strings deliberately NOT comma-split), oauth2's decorative `enum` (now an accepted-but-non-enforcing builder option, pinned by a parity test). **Final cohort (checkpoint 78): all 30 admin/payments/marketing schemas converted** (admin/email's `sanitize: false` html + 3-type recipients, admin/database's 5-type `document`, admin/notification's non-empty object defaults — handler verified to read named props only, so the clean-defaults fix is wire-invisible; payments/cancel + refund's required-no-default `confirmed`, campaign/put's default-undefined sparse-update fields, email-preferences' required+default footgun preserved byte-for-byte). **Every framework route schema now runs on the zod engine**; the 4 provider webhooks (`payments/webhook`, `payments/dispute-alert`, `marketing/webhook`, `marketing/webhook/forward`) deliberately stay declarative-empty (raw provider body, query-param auth), and consumer projects' declarative schemas keep working unchanged (the powertools engine remains for them); post-parity tightenings (enum enforcement, the dead `Manager.Settings().schema` sanitize surface, pollution fix ripple) go to Ian as one list — new findings recorded in [plans/zod-route-schemas-design.md](plans/zod-route-schemas-design.md). Docs: backend [docs/schemas.md](packages/backend/docs/schemas.md) "Zod schemas". Proofs: parity suite 11/11, backend parse-audit 414 + self-test exit 0, sandbox corpus green.
- **D15 shipped: the .env cascade (checkpoint 73a) — secrets mirror the config hierarchy.** New `@omega.js/config` env module: `loadEnv(startDir)` resolves + loads `company .env ← brand .env ← app .env` with the shell above all (dotenv's no-override semantics — files load strongest-first; same walk as the config cascade: `findBrandRoot` in load.js is now the ONE hierarchy definition, and the `.omega/company.json` marker read lives in config so manager + frameworks share it). A brand-wide `GH_TOKEN` is defined once in the brand `.env` and resolved everywhere at runtime/build. Adopted at every boot surface: web/desktop/extension CLIs + both gulp pipelines, the @omega.js/backend CLI (test/setup/mcp/firebase-init/stripe-forwarding) AND runtime (from the functions dir — in the cloud the walk finds no brand/company and behavior is identical to plain dotenv), desktop setup/push-secrets, and the manager's manage + devlog (via `loadEnvChain`; staleness handling unchanged — company.test.js's shell>brand>company proof now runs through the shared module). **Disperse is now a composer, not a copier**: brand-wide values are no longer duplicated into app .envs — only files that must physically exist still get written: backend's `functions/.env` (FULL curated pass-through — it ships with the Firebase deploy artifact), per-app GA4 stream secrets, and exists-gated signing-cert paths; web left ENV_MAP entirely. dotenv is a dependency of @omega.js/config alone now (web/manager dropped their direct deps; vendoring hosts already declare it — the devkit vendor dep-guard caught its own fixture needing the same). 8 new config env tests + reworked disperse pins (brand values NOT copied; backend full pass-through pinned; multi-line escaping moved to a unit pin). Docs: [docs/config.md](docs/config.md) "The .env cascade". Proofs: config 66, manager 565, devkit 130, web 53, extension 94, desktop 763, backend full emulator suite, sandbox corpus + cross-stack e2e — all green.

### Changed
- **N6 third slice (checkpoint 86) — the global lifecycle flows run as browser e2e.** The sandbox cross-stack e2e grew 11 → **19 steps**, all driven through a real Chromium against real routes + real webhook triggers (nothing mocked, `OMEGA_TEST_MODE` only skips emails/analytics — the `payments-webhooks` on-write trigger's user-doc subscription write runs for real): **seeded persona signs in with the known password** (the cp85 boot-seeding DX proven in-browser: `_test.basic@… / omega-test-password` → uid `_test-basic`); **subscribe** (`POST /omega/payments/intent {processor:'test', productId:'premium', frequency:'monthly'}` → auto-webhook activates → authState polls to `status:'active'`, `payment.processor:'test'`); **cancel** (`confirmed:true, skipGuards:true` → test-processor webhook doc → `cancellation.pending:true` with status still `active`, `resolved.cancelling:true`); **refund** (`{success, refund.full:true}` → webhook → `status:'cancelled'`, `resolved.active:false`); **data-request create/status/cancel** (pending doc → status echoes it → DELETE removes it → status returns `request:null`) — **first coverage anywhere for `user/data-request`** (no backend test existed); **delete account** (legal post-refund — the paid-sub block only gates `active|suspended` — then re-signin rejects `auth/user-not-found`, proving the auth user is gone). Plumbing: the sandbox fixture's `window.__omega` gains **`api(method, route, body)`** — the signed-in user's Firebase ID token on the `Authorization: Bearer` header, POSTed to the hosting emulator's `/omega/**` rewrite, JSON-or-plain-text response surfaced (the backend's exact wire contract). Design note: no static persona holds an active cancellable test-processor sub (journey personas' seeded states are corpus-pinned), so the e2e builds its own subscription via intent — mirroring the corpus's `route-cancel-success` journey. Proofs: **cross-stack e2e 19/19** (log-verified), sandbox-only change (corpus untouched by construction).
- **N6 second slice (checkpoint 85) — personas seed on standalone emulator boot + the brand e2e harness generalizes into devkit.** (D) New shared seed module `@omega.js/backend` [src/test/seed.js](packages/backend/src/test/seed.js) — the ONE orchestration for populating an emulator with personas (wipe → meta/stats baseline → create ~53 accounts → fetch privateKeys → `test/_init.js` hooks); `runner.js setupAccounts()` now delegates to it (corpus wire-identical: 1224/44/0 unchanged), and **`npx omega emulator` seeds on boot by default** — boot the emulators and every persona is signin-able immediately with `TEST_ACCOUNT_PASSWORD` (the N6 manual-dev DX). `--no-seed` skips (yargs-declared boolean — `--no-seed` parses as `seed: false`); seeding is fully non-fatal (config/admin failures warn and the emulator keeps running; missing brand domain skips cleanly instead of firing 53 doomed createUser calls). (E) New `@omega.js/devkit/test/e2e-harness` (`E2eHarness` + `discoverTargets`): the generalized boot-the-brand-stack-and-drive-it harness — target discovery (`apps/backend`/`apps/website` by dir convention; manager-typed discovery joins when desktop/extension targets do), website build + static serve (traversal-guarded), emulator boot that waits for the CLI's **post-seed** "Emulator ready" marker (NOT firebase-tools' "All emulators ready" — proceeding on that line would race browser steps into the seed's Firestore wipe + auth bulk-clear window), step()/teardown/page-log plumbing; the sandbox brand's `e2e/run.js` is now a consumer authoring ONLY its browser steps (infra shrank out of it; puppeteer stays in the brand's devDeps). Fable-review catches folded in pre-commit: the dead `--no-seed` check (yargs negation), the non-fatal guarantee actually holding (whole body guarded), the seed-wipe race (ready-marker move), a deletion span that swallowed `runTestsInDir` (corpus caught it — restored). Docs: backend README (emulator seeding), test-framework.md Personas, sandbox README (harness-consumer shape). Proofs: devkit 140/140 (6 new harness unit tests: discovery, step accounting, site server incl. traversal guard, teardown), backend parse-audit 416 + self-test 6/6, corpus 1224/44/0, **cross-stack e2e 11/11 through the new harness with 53 personas seeded during the boot step**.
- **min/max enforce only when declared (checkpoint 84, Ian: "min should not be enforced if not provided. Same as max.").** The engine's last powertools bounds quirk is gone: `min || 0` / `max || Infinity` silently clamped negatives to 0 on EVERY number field without a declared `min`, and made declared `min: 0`/`max: 0` vanish entirely. Now bounds apply exactly as written — no `min` → negatives pass through to handlers; no `max` → unbounded; a declared `0` is a real bound (`max: 0` truncates strings to `''`). Both engines flip together (shared pipeline). Battery rows renamed (`negative-passes-no-min`, `max-zero-enforces`) + a new literal-pin test (`min-max-enforce-only-when-declared`) covering both engines. Corpus green — no framework schema relied on the implicit floor. Also recorded: Ian's brand-account answers (support@{domain} stays a default suggestion; personal admins = ITW company-level config; hooks organized NESTED mirroring the call site, e.g. `config/hooks/account/password.js`) in [plans/brand-account-provisioning.md](plans/brand-account-provisioning.md); N6 slices renumber to cp85–87. Proofs: parity suite 16/16, parse-audit 415 + self-test, corpus 1224/44/0.
- **N6 personas, first slice (checkpoint 83) — every seeded account is now a signin-able persona.** Design + survey landed in [plans/n6-personas-lifecycle-e2e.md](plans/n6-personas-lifecycle-e2e.md) (verdict: most of N6 already exists — the ~55-persona catalog with plan states, `http.as()` privateKey auth, consumer `test/_init.js` hooks, `/user/token` minting, the web's `?authCustomToken=` signin, emulator-safe test-processor cancel/refund webhooks, app discovery ×2). Shipped this slice: **deterministic `TEST_ACCOUNT_PASSWORD`** (`'omega-test-password'`, exported) on every seeded persona — manual dev signin is now boot-emulators → open an emulator-connected dev site → sign in as any persona with email + the known password (previously each account got a throwaway `uuid.v4()` password); a first-class **`refunded` static persona** (post-refund-webhook end state: cancelled premium on the `test` processor — the refund itself lives on the order doc; "unauthed" deliberately stays `http.as('none')`/signed-out, no fake account); sandbox `window.__omega` exposes **`signInWithCustomToken`/`getIdToken`** for the lifecycle e2e driver (cp85). The survey's `_init.js` array-vs-object contract worry was a non-issue — `loadInitHooks` already normalizes both. Docs: test-framework.md "Personas" section. Proofs: parse-audit 415 + self-test, corpus 1223/44/0 (seeding exercises the password + new persona on every boot), cross-stack e2e 11/11. Next slices: cp84 seed-on-standalone-emulator-boot + generalized boot-all-targets harness, cp85 lifecycle browser flows, cp86 /account mock removal.
- **N5 shipped: emulator-first frontend dev (checkpoint 82) — dev mode means LOCAL Firebase, zero flags.** The @omega.js/client emulator auto-connect existed but was gated on `config.env.FIREBASE_EMULATOR_CONNECT` — an env flag, exactly what the decided N5 spec ruled out; plain `omega dev` against a configured brand talked to LIVE Firebase. The flag is dead: `environment === 'development'` alone connects the REAL Auth + Firestore SDKs to the local emulators (`:9099`/`:8080` CLI defaults; N7's port map owns configurability), production builds never connect, and there is deliberately NO live-Firebase opt-out for dev (build production locally if you truly need live). `omega dev` injects the development environment via the chrome (`jekyll.environment` → the Configuration block), `omega build` stamps production — verified end to end. **Bonus catch: the cross-stack e2e had been latently broken since cp74** — D12's `firebaseConfig` → `cloud.config` rename missed the sandbox website fixture (`main.js` still passed the flat legacy key, so Firebase init silently SKIPPED), and the e2e's "page boots" step passed anyway because `isReady` resolves even Firebase-less; cp74 only re-ran the corpus. Fixture now speaks the canonical `cloud: { provider, config }` shape and the boot step REQUIRES the `[Firebase] Emulators connected` console line — the hole can't reopen. Docs: client README (dev = local, zero flags), web README (`omega dev` ↔ backend `omega emulator` workflow), sandbox README. Proofs: client 77, **cross-stack e2e PASSED (all 11 steps — signup → authOnCreate → signin → persistence → subscription, real browser against real emulators, zero flags)**, extension + desktop suites green.
- **N4 parked verifies closed (checkpoint 81).** (1) **Web's site-wide defaults-style override is achievable via the native data cascade — verified with a pinned proof.** A root directory data file (`<srcDir>/<srcDirName>.11tydata.json`) sits exactly where jekyll-uj-powertools 1.8.1 put Jekyll's `_config.yml` `defaults:` layer — ABOVE layout front matter, BELOW each page's own front matter — giving consumer sites the same one-place override for layout/blueprint sample content. New slice test pins all three edges through the real classy layout (directory data replaces layout `hero.description` site-wide; the page's own `meta.title` still wins; sibling layout keys survive the deep merge) + README engine-fact 11. (2) **`devlog` + `seo` are now schema-known config sections** — @omega.js/manager reads both (`config.devlog` pipeline settings; `brandConfig.seo` + the `config/seo.json5` sidecar) but the canonical schema (which doubles as the config documentation) had no entries; added as optional objects with descriptions + a validate test (wrong types now report; validation stays non-strict). Proofs: web 54, config 71.
- **Schema tightening #1 shipped (checkpoint 80, Ian-approved — the tightenings list is now fully closed).** `enum` on a schema field (declarative node or zod builder) now REJECTS instead of decorating: values the caller sends are checked post-coercion against the list, out-of-list → `400 Invalid settings {field}: must be one of [...]` — byte-identical in both engines via the shared `enforceEnums()` in `schema-engine.js`, running at the route boundary (`Settings.resolve` / `resolveZodSchema`). Absent fields pass (enum does not imply required; schema-author defaults are presumed valid), so the only wire change is garbage-in now gets a clean 400 — live on the two `user/oauth2` `action` fields (`authorize|status` GET, `tokenize|refresh` POST), the only enum declarations in the tree. Docs: [schemas.md](packages/backend/docs/schemas.md) gains the `enum` field property. Proofs: parity suite 15/15 (`enum-enforced-post-resolution` pins exact message, post-coercion rejection, absent-passes — both engines), parse-audit 415 + self-test, sandbox corpus green.
- **Schema tightenings #2–#4 shipped (checkpoint 79, Ian-approved) — `powertools.defaults()` retired from settings resolution, `''` fails `required`, per-field `sanitize: false` actually works.** New `@omega.js/backend` `src/manager/helpers/schema-engine.js`: the in-house declarative resolver BOTH engines now share — `resolveSchema()` replaces `powertools.defaults()` at all 3 call sites (`Settings.resolve`, the `user/settings/validate` route, the legacy `user:validate-settings` action; node-powertools stays a dependency for `force()`/`getKeys()`/etc), so the two cp76 bug fixes now apply to DECLARATIVE consumer schemas too: non-empty object/array defaults come back **clean + cloned** (powertools mutated them in place — injecting `types`/`min`/`max`/`value`/`default` keys — and returned them by reference), and the schema walk terminates soundly on no-`default` nodes (powertools' walk only terminated because its own pollution added `default` to the node first — the two-bugs-canceling case from cp77's signup conversion). **#3 required tightening**: `''` now counts as missing alongside `undefined` (null/0/false still pass) — identical 400 + message in both engines; declarative required checks also moved BEFORE resolution (matching zod; the `required`+`default` footgun semantics are unchanged — still checked against the raw input). **#2 sanitize surface fixed**: middleware now holds the Settings instance it resolved with — it used to read `Manager.Settings().schema` off a FRESH instance (always `undefined`), so per-field `sanitize: false` was silently ignored on every `{ sanitize: true }` route; `Settings.resolve` exposes the per-field map for both engines (zod via the new `buildSchemaMap()` off the builder registry). **#1 (enum enforcement) awaits Ian — still decorative.** Oracle-pinned edge semantics preserved (probed live against powertools before the swap): empty-object schema nodes (`options: {}`) contribute nothing, empty schema → `{}`, input never mutated, `min||0` negative clamp kept. Docs: [schemas.md](packages/backend/docs/schemas.md) + [sanitization.md](packages/backend/docs/sanitization.md). Proofs: parity suite 15/15 (new: powertools-oracle pins, sanitize-map exposure, both-engines clean+cloned defaults; frozen-twin diffs now STRICT — the pollution-normalization shim is gone), backend parse-audit 415 + self-test, sandbox corpus **1223 passing / 44 skipped / 0 failing**.
- **Marker-block harmonization (checkpoint 75) — ONE grammar for every machine-parsed marker (Ian: "no more ALL OVER THE PLACE DIFFERENT STYLES/FLAVORS").** The family: `<comment> ========== <Label> ==========` — same words/structure everywhere, only the comment token adapts per file type (`#` for env/gitignore/CLAUDE.md, `//` for rules). The already-uniform `Default Values`/`Custom Values` section pair (devkit merge-line-files across every framework's `_.env`/`_.gitignore`/consumer `CLAUDE.md`) DEFINES the family and is unchanged; the odd flavor out — the rules managed block's FOUR sub-shapes (`///---omega---///` open, `///---version=X---///` stamp, `///--------tests--------///`/`///------resources------///` dividers, `///---------end---------///` close) — is re-cut into it: **`// ========== OMEGA Rules (vX.Y.Z) ==========` open (version folded into the marker; the separate stamp line dies) · `// ========== Tests / Resources ==========` dividers · `// ========== End OMEGA Rules ==========` close**, across `omegaAllRulesRegex`/`rulesVersionRegex`/template version substitution, both rules templates, both sandbox consumer files, error messages, and the consumer CLAUDE.md template. The `# ...` gitignore insertion placeholder stays (an insertion hint, not a block; documented). - **CI emulator-death investigation advanced (run 6)** — the leaked-process/pre-existing-memory-pressure theory is DEAD: with the preflight sweep aboard, every non-emulator suite passed on Linux (desktop under xvfb included) and the runner showed 6.9 GB available going INTO the backend suite; both emulator workloads then hit `Failed to load function.` storms (104× in the suites job — functions workers timing out requiring the heavy module on 2-core runners, storms starting <60 s in) — the suites job hung and was SIGTERM'd at ~23 min (exit 143, a LABELED failure at last), the sandbox runner died again mid-corpus at 17.5 min ("The operation was canceled"). Run-7 experiment shipped: `FUNCTIONS_DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT=120` on both emulator jobs + on-failure `free -m`/top-RSS post-mortems to catch the suspected worker-respawn memory spiral in the act. **Run-7 verdict (investigation now PARKED — CI is opt-in)**: the leash changed the failure mode — the suites job failed CLEAN at 14m35s (no 23-minute hang; every non-emulator suite green again) but the backend step was still killed externally ("The operation was canceled"), and the post-mortems never fired because cancellation ≠ `failure()` (instrumentation lesson recorded: `if: always()` next time); the sandbox job outlived run-6's 17.5-minute runner death and was canceled deliberately at 28 minutes when Ian called stop on minute burn. Next escalations if resumed: throttle the bulk-user trigger storms or split/beef the jobs.

### Removed
- **The /account `?_dev_subscription=` mock fixtures (checkpoint 87 — closes N6).** `packages/web/core/js/pages/account/test-subscriptions/` (5 fixtures: active, cancellation-requested, cancelled, suspended, trialing) deleted, along with the `@dev-only` URL-param block in `pages/account/index.js` that dynamically imported and deep-merged them over the real subscription (+ the `deepMerge` helper the removal orphaned). Dev-testing subscription states on the account page is now the real thing: boot the emulators (`npx omega emulator` seeds personas — cp85) and **sign in as a seeded persona** (`premium-active`, `premium-cancelling`, `refunded`, … with `TEST_ACCOUNT_PASSWORD`) — real user docs, real resolver, no merged fiction. Zero `_dev_subscription` references remain in source or docs. With A–G all shipped (cp83 personas/password/token surface, cp85 seed-on-boot + devkit harness, cp86 lifecycle browser flows, cp87 this removal), **N6 is complete**. Proof: web suite 54/54 (account bundle rebuilds clean without the fixtures).
- **Legacy pre-omega file-format support dropped from `mgr setup` (checkpoint 73d — Ian: "i dont care if we remove support for them since we will be migrating brands over").** The rules tests no longer match/replace the `{{ backend-manager }}` mustache placeholder (cp73c had just repaired that matcher — the fix surfaced what it was for, and Ian's answer was kill it), and the gitignore test no longer detects/strips `# BEM>>> … # <<<BEM` marker blocks. Evergreen setup now speaks exactly ONE format: the `///---omega---///` marker block. Converting older files is the (pinned) migration tooling's job — the parked finding records what it must handle: the mustache rules placeholder, BEM gitignore markers, and the middle-era `///---backend-manager---///` rules markers (which the evergreen path never matched post-cp72 anyway).

### Fixed
- **Emulator shutdown crashed on macOS `EPERM`, leaking 3 java emulators per e2e run (checkpoint 89).** `killGroup` swallowed `ESRCH` but re-threw everything else; on macOS, signaling a process group whose remaining members are zombies awaiting reap yields `EPERM`, and the un-awaited `shutdown()` in the SIGINT handlers turned that throw into an unhandled rejection that killed the CLI **before** the orphan sweep + ports-file/resolved-config cleanup ran — every sandbox e2e teardown left firestore/database/pubsub jars alive on 8080/9000/8085 (this, not the harness's 20s SIGKILL race as cp88b guessed, was the real leak). `EPERM` is now treated like `ESRCH` (group unreachable → proceed; other codes log and proceed — the port-based sweep is the safety net, and shutdown must never die before it). Verified: post-fix runs log `Cleaned up 3 orphaned emulator processes` and leave zero javas, no stale ports file, no resolved config.
- **`omega test` config-validation abort exited 0 (checkpoint 74)** — when `loadProjectConfig` failed (missing projectId/keys/brand fields) the command logged the error and bare-returned, so the process exited GREEN; any script chaining on the exit code (CI steps, `&&` markers) read a hard abort as a pass. Found live when the first D12 sandbox-corpus run "succeeded" while actually aborting on a stale pre-sweep `dist/` (the consumer CLI runs from dist — regenerated via prepare; pack-smoke re-vendors all four). Now sets `process.exitCode = 1` on that abort. (Same abort-exit-0 class as the cp67 runner fix.)
- **Three backend CLI modules were hard SyntaxErrors since cp71 — found by cp73c's repo-wide parse sweep (1,732 files), fixed and permanently guarded.** The Great-Rename sweep rewrote legacy names INSIDE regex literals: `setup-tests/firestore-rules-file.js` + `realtime-rules-file.js` (the `{{\s*?backend-manager\s*?}}` placeholder matcher became `{{\s*?@omega.js/backend\s*?}}` — the injected unescaped `/` terminates the literal) and `setup-tests/gitignore.js` (the `# BEM>>> … # <<<BEM` marker matcher). Requiring any of them threw "Invalid regular expression flags", so a real consumer `mgr setup` would crash on the rules/gitignore tests — and no suite ever loads these modules, which is why three checkpoints of green never noticed. Restored to the pre-rename literals: they match LEGACY bytes in un-migrated consumers (the mustache rules placeholder, the BEM-era gitignore markers) and must never carry the new name; the no-template error message now advises the current `///---omega---///` marker block instead of the corrupted placeholder. **Guard: new devkit `parse-audit`** (`parseAuditTree` — vm-parses every source file WITHOUT executing it; CJS `Module.wrap` first, ESM re-check via `node --check`) runs as a backend-suite pre-flight (`[parse-audit] 413 source files parse OK`); the sweep's only other hits are pre-monorepo junk under `backend/_backup/`.
- **Desktop renderer webpack `global` binding (desktop 2.0.2)** — `ProvidePlugin({ global: 'globalThis' })` resolved `'globalThis'` as a MODULE request: on macOS it silently hit the unrelated `globalthis` npm package (binding `global` to a polyfill FUNCTION — a real latent bug masked by case-insensitivity + consumers' defensive fallbacks), on Linux CI it failed the build outright. Now `DefinePlugin({ global: 'globalThis' })`; desktop suite 758 green incl. the real-Electron boot canary.
- **CI environment gaps caught by the first real run**: `firebase-tools` now installed globally in both emulator-booting jobs (backend suite + sandbox — the runner had no `firebase` CLI), and the sandbox job fetches puppeteer's Chrome via `npx puppeteer browsers install chrome` (npm 11's script-approval gating skips puppeteer's postinstall download on runners).

- **Local-linking DX (checkpoint 69) — master plan §8's dev loop, the last plumbing before the omega-brand dogfood.** New `@omegajs/devkit/local` module (devkit 1.1.0) owns every local-dev mechanic: monorepo resolution (`OMEGA_MONOREPO` env → self-location walk-up → conventional clone path), brand-root walk-up + app discovery (the Omega monorepo itself never counts as a brand), `@omegajs/*` dep detection incl. backend `functions/package.json`, **idempotent** file:-linking (already-linked deps skip; resolution walks up node_modules, so npm-workspace hoisting is handled), and the `.omega/dev-watch.lock` single-instance protocol. Root `npm start` = new `scripts/watch-all.js`: every dist-building package's `prepare:watch` (backend/client/desktop/extension — discovered, not hardcoded) runs CONCURRENTLY with per-package output prefixes; the old `npm run --workspaces` form ran them sequentially, so the first watch blocked and the rest never started. Root package.json bootstrap artifacts dropped (`main`, `preparePackage`, root `prepare`/`prepare:watch` against a nonexistent root `src/`). **`omega dev --local`** (@omegajs/web 0.2.0): links every framework the brand uses across ALL its apps from the local monorepo, starts the monorepo watch as a session-scoped child (dies with the dev server; the lock prevents doubles), then runs the normal dev loop. **`mgr i local` rewired to the shared module in all three CLIs** — desktop + extension had been broken since their renames (installing from `~/…/ITW-Creative-Works/@omegajs/desktop|extension`, paths that never existed; desktop 2.0.1, extension 2.0.1); backend (6.0.1) de-hardcodes the monorepo path and always installs where the dep is declared (`functions/package.json`) instead of the invocation cwd. New deep reference: [docs/local-dev.md](docs/local-dev.md); root CLAUDE.md publish-policy rule updated (client is a published runtime dep of desktop/extension since the client cutover, not a bundled internal).
- Checkpoint-69 proofs: devkit 120/120 (18 new local tests) · live root watch (4 concurrent watches, second-start guard, touch→rebuild, SIGTERM cascade 4→0 watchers + lock release) · scratch-brand round trip (`omega dev --local` from the website app links the SIBLING api app's backend dep brand-wide + an unlinked client, skips already-linked web, serves the default pages on :8123, SIGINT tears the watch down with zero orphans) · sandbox `mgr i local` idempotent skip with porcelain clean · suites web 52, client 77, manager 564/564, extension 93, desktop 758, backend boot green · pack-smoke ×4 from tarballs.

### Fixed
- devkit vendor: **published @omegajs runtime deps are never vendor candidates.** Desktop/extension's `@omegajs/client` dependency was being vendored into shipped dist as a pinned snapshot (duplicating the shared client singleton and freezing its version), and extension's vendor hook crashed outright on `require('@omegajs/client/package.json')` (webpack sibling resolution) — non-blocking, so it silently left raw private devkit references in extension's dist. The tool now derives the split from the host's package.json: `dependencies`/`peerDependencies`/`optionalDependencies` stay normal package requires; only private devDep workspace packages (devkit/config/account) vendor. CI's pack-smoke gate greps shipped dist for raw PRIVATE refs only and maps `@omegajs/client` to its local tarball via npm `overrides` (skipped when smoking client itself — overriding a direct dep conflicts) until the package exists on npm.

- **@omegajs/client cutover, local half (checkpoint 68) — the fourth and final Phase-3 rename; every package now carries its @omegajs name.** `packages/client` is `@omegajs/client@5.0.0` (was `web-manager@4.3.4`; 5.0.0 continues the 4.x line). Import specifiers flip monorepo-wide — `import webManager from '@omegajs/client'`, subpaths `/modules/*`, @omegajs/web's bundler alias (consumer page code now writes the new name), the extension's webpack sibling paths (`../client/src`), and the desktop/extension deps (`@omegajs/client ^5.0.0` — closing cp66's parked `^4.3.6` bump). **Parked:** the `webManager` runtime API (`window.webManager`, the singleton, every method) — only the package name moved; `omega:wm` skill refs. Desktop's bridge files renamed `web-manager-bridge.*` → `client-bridge.*`. The freeze-4.x story: web-manager 4.x is frozen at 4.3.6 in the legacy repo (old-name publish lane); all new work lands in @omegajs/client. Pre-rename point tagged `pre-client-rename`. Sweep: 561 conversions across 184 files + the acronym trio (BEM/BXM/EM → new names) inside the client package, whose folded files had reintroduced them; every touched JS file parse-checked (the cp67 regex-corruption lesson, now a standard step — zero hits).
- Upstream web-manager v4.3.5–v4.3.6 folded at the divergence check: Firebase init skips empty-value config blobs (apiKey required — fixes the auth/invalid-api-key crash on Firebase-less sites) + the `[Firebase] Skipped` console line. Three delta files copied verbatim (baseline-identical); `src/index.js` hand-merged (the monorepo copy had diverged via the @omegajs/account adoption).
- **Parked finding 1.3b resolved:** the `module: "src/index.js"` field routed exports-unaware bundlers into un-vendored source where `modules/auth.js` bare-imports the devDep `@omegajs/account` — removed; the `exports` map (vendored dist) is the only entry surface. License CC-BY-4.0 → MIT.

Pre-rename point tagged `pre-backend-rename`.

The legacy repo's one dirty file was the auto-generated disposable-domains list — churn the prepare hook regenerates, not folded.

### Fixed
- Backend test runner: **pre-flight aborts now exit non-zero** — a failed config validation, health check, or account setup returned zero-test results (`failed === 0`) and `run-tests.js` exited 0, so a dead server produced a GREEN suite. Caught red-handed when the rename's first boot run "passed" while `bm_api` was 500ing. Abort paths set `results.aborted`; proven by a dead-port run exiting 1.
- Backend `linkFixtureDeps` creates the scoped link's parent dir (`node_modules/@omegajs`) before symlinking — same class as the desktop boot-runner fix at checkpoint 66, predicted and confirmed.
- The sweep's one regex casualty — `backend-router.js`'s route-prefix stripper had its **parked** `/backend-manager` URL segment converted inside a regex literal (the injected `/` invalidated the pattern; every `bm_api` request 500'd). Caught by the boot canary, restored, and the repo-wide audit confirmed it was the only regex-literal hit.

- **@omegajs/desktop cutover, local half (checkpoint 66) — the second Phase-3 rename.** `packages/desktop` is `@omegajs/desktop@2.0.0` (was `electron-manager@1.12.0`): bins `omega-desktop` + `mgr` (`em`/`electron-manager` dropped), SCSS entry `@use 'omega-desktop'`, test prefix `desktop:`, framework IPC channels `desktop:*` (were `em:*`; `window.em` + `em:__test:*` harness channels parked like the kept `EM_*` env vars), audit IDs `DSK-xx`; all 40 docs + consumer defaults swept; sister links cross-updated in the extension package. The pre-rename point is tagged `pre-desktop-rename` (Ian can publish old-name electron-manager releases straight from the legacy repo, which now carries 1.12.1 + 1.13.0).
- Legacy EM v1.13.0 folded in during the pre-rename divergence check: FontAwesome `overflow="visible"` serve attribute + the tooltip `data-bs-original-title` observer fix (an infinite MutationObserver loop on title-only hosts) with their tests and docs — the LSUIElement/openAtLogin and classy `$min-contrast-ratio` halves were already present (shared lineage). The `web-manager ^4.3.6` bump stays behind until the client cutover (workspace still links 4.3.4). Desktop's defaults scaffold needed NO test/** copy-once port — its FILE_MAP is `overwrite: false` for everything by design.
- Manager: `TARGET_FRAMEWORKS.desktop` → `@omegajs/desktop` (564/564). Desktop suite 758 passing / 763 (boot layer = the consumer canary: the bundled fixture app builds sass via the bare `omega-desktop` entry, webpack resolves `@omegajs/desktop/main` through the scoped symlink, and the packaged bundle boots in real Electron). Pack-smoke green: 2.0.0 tarball scratch-installs via npu, entrypoints load, both bins link.

### Fixed
- devkit vendor: a host's OWN package name is never a vendor candidate — boot harnesses and consumer fixtures reference the host by name and resolve via the consumer's node_modules at runtime; collecting it folded the host into itself (the rename-exposed failure), and rewriting it would break that resolution. Locked by a new vendor test (devkit 102).
- Desktop boot runner: fixture dep symlinks create the link's parent dir — `node_modules/@omegajs/desktop` needs `node_modules/@omegajs` first; the old code failed silently and the boot layer died with "No gulpfile found".

- **@omegajs/extension cutover, local half (checkpoint 64) — the first Phase-3 rename is live in the monorepo.** `packages/extension` is `@omegajs/extension@2.0.0` (was `browser-extension-manager@1.7.3`): bins `omega-extension` + `mgr`, SCSS entry `@use 'omega-extension'`, test prefix `extension:`, build-config key `omega`, auth protocol `omega:*`; consumer defaults + all 25 framework docs speak the new name (`BXM_*` env vars parked as a cross-framework harmonization; `omega:bxm` skill refs stay until the Phase-5 skills rewrite). Publishing waits on Ian's @omegajs org claim — everything installs locally (workspace + `file:`).
- Legacy-1.7.4 divergence folded in during the pre-rename check: `test/**` defaults are copy-once (consumer `test/_init.js` survives setup reruns; the missing-config `getConfig` half was already covered) — desktop + backend have the same latent gap, queued for their flips.
- Manager: `TARGET_FRAMEWORKS.extension` → `@omegajs/extension`; the manager's companion extension (`packages/manager/extension`, now `omega-manager-extension`) consumes the renamed framework via `file:../../extension` — the full consumer canary (`mgr clean && mgr setup && gulp build`) passes: MV3 manifest, 9 icon sizes, chromium/firefox/opera zips.

### Fixed
- devkit vendor tool ×2 (exposed by the rename): `dist/defaults` is consumer-template content and is no longer scanned/rewritten/vendored (a framework's own defaults reference the framework itself — it tried to vendor the host into itself); the dep-guard strips comments before specifier-scanning (JSDoc prose in config's edit.js — "'a b' from 'a b'" — reported a phantom host dep). Locked by 2 new vendor tests (devkit 99 → 101).
- Extension icons task corrupted binaries under gulp 5 (`src()` defaults to UTF-8 decoding; sharp got mangled buffers) — now `encoding: false` both ways, matching distribute.js. Found by the consumer canary; pack-smoke of the 2.0.0 tarball (scratch install, entrypoints, bins) also green.

### Added
- **Upstream sync sweep + devlog port (checkpoint 65).** Another agent pulled/merged the legacy framework repos (fresh commits landed in EM/BEM/powertools the same night); the sweep pinned every delta against the monorepo baselines. Verdicts — already covered: BXM head 1.7.4 = exactly what checkpoint 64 folded; every dirty omega-manager service behavior (shared-Firebase skips across migrations/payment webhooks/api-check, the cloudflare zone deferral + `zone_not_active` skip, the Namecheap not-purchased warn, the seo repo-existence guard, `domain.provider: null`) was already in the port. Parity by construction: @omegajs/web's token page is byte-identical to UJM's post-fix version, its scaffold is copy-once everywhere (`'**/*': { overwrite: false }`), and Eleventy directory-data files occupy exactly the layer powertools 1.8.1 added (above layout front matter, below page front matter) — no boot-runner/theme-contract-consumer/sass-bundles analogues exist to patch. Ride-alongs annotated onto their queued cutovers: EM 1.12.1+1.13.0 (+2 dirty) → desktop, BEM 5.12.0 (+1 dirty) → backend, WM 4.3.5–4.3.6 → client.
- **The devlog subsystem ported** — the one real gap (upstream omega-manager grew it after the port drain): `omega-manager devlog` (standalone command, not a service; stateless — nothing lands in state.json) collects commits via `gh` across `devlog.orgs` listings + every brand repo derived from the brand configs (one monorepo repo per brand, `github.org` + `github.repo || brand id` — the per-target/subdomain fan-out collapsed), has Ghostii write the first-person article (BEM-mirrored client on global fetch; brief assembled task > excludeTopics blocklist > voice), and publishes into the brand's website app — `apps/{web}/src/_posts/{year}/{postPath}/{date}-{slug}.md`, blueprint front matter, local-date stamp, post-file-only commit + push in the brand monorepo — or previews to `.omega/devlog/` on `--dry-run`. Context-sensitive: from a brand root that brand publishes (company siblings still feed the backlink map via the stamp); from a company root `--brand` or the single `devlog.enabled` brand picks. New `devlog` DEFAULTS block in config.js; secrets ride the standard chain (`gh` CLI + `BACKEND_MANAGER_KEY` in brand/company `.env`).
- Tests: manager 549 → 564 (+15 devlog: project-map derivation, collect filtering incl. the `--paginate` concat repair and the 30-cap, digest/brief/links + blocksToPost via an injected writer, renderPostFile pins, publish against a real temp git repo + local bare remote proving the post-file-only commit with unrelated dirty files untouched, and runDevlog resolution + dry-run preview — zero live GitHub/Ghostii calls).

- **Extension port + assets PSD templates + AI brandmark (checkpoint 63) — omega-manager is now FULLY ported; nothing remains parked.** The companion Chrome extension (MV3 — bookmark filing + trusted-CDP browser automation via `chrome.debugger`, plus its MCP bridge) copied to `packages/manager/extension/` as a standalone BXM consumer project (excluded from the npm tarball via `.npmignore`); the manager speaks its WebSocket protocol through `src/lib/automation-client.js` (env-overridable port, correlation-id envelopes, injectable timeouts).
- The `bookmark` service (between `migrations` and `testing`): brand console/dashboard bookmarks pushed to the extension and filed under `Ω / {Brand} / {Category}` — links reshaped to new-world config (the ONE monorepo repo + Actions instead of per-target repos; unslugged Stripe dashboard links instead of the ITW platform-account deep links; per-app backend API link). Interactive-only: headless runs skip cleanly, dry runs print the planned groups without a server or the gcloud function-log read.
- The Beehiiv segment automation is live: interactive runs with missing segments offer Automate-via-extension (the battle-tested DOM driving, ported verbatim to `beehiiv/lib/segment-automation.js`) or manual dashboard creation, then **re-verify against the API** — success means Beehiiv reports every segment (omega-manager trusted the automation's own belief). Non-interactive/dry runs keep the warn-with-conditions and never mutate.
- Assets `templates` op (between `process` and `icons`): brand PSDs seeded from the company root's `assets/templates/` (omega-manager baked ITW's binaries into its own `src/defaults/`), logo layers re-rastered from the brandmark (black variant for the tray), config-driven text layers with overflow font-shrink, composited PNG exports — mtime-diffed like every assets op, manual Photoshop edits persist. omega-manager's social-images/store-images ops were **dead code** (template-name lists never matched TEMPLATE_CONFIG keys — they could never generate anything) and are not ported; the templates op produces their intended outputs.
- The AI brandmark generation is live in the assets setup: a missing brandmark offers the logo-API flow — `assets.brandmark` config (`apiUrl` + `providerBrand`/`adminEmail`, de-ITW'd from the hardcoded MrLogo constants), token from `LOGO_API_ID_TOKEN` or minted through the provider brand in the company workspace (marker → discoverBrands → its state web key + service account → the shared auth-admin custom-token chain), creative-direction prompt, SVG landed as committed `assets/logo/brandmark.svg`.
- Tests: manager 523 → 549 (+7 automation-client protocol against a real ws stand-in, +8 bookmark incl. the sync ack/refusal/timeout, +3 beehiiv automation end-to-end over fake TTY + fake extension with the wire commands pinned, +5 PSD templates against ag-psd-built fixture PSDs, +3 brandmark against a local logo API). New manager deps: `ws`, `ag-psd`, `canvas`.

- **Disperse remnants (checkpoint 62).** The `disperse` service — what survived of omega-manager's disperse after the config hierarchy dissolved file dispersal — runs between `certificates` and `update`. **certs**: signing artifacts copied from the brand-local `.omega/certificates/apple/` (was the company's shared `.output/_shared/` tree) into desktop `config/certs/` and mobile `build/certs/`, byte-compared for converged no-ops, required-miss warns vs optional-miss skips, and a self-protecting `.gitignore` (`*`) in every certs dir so signing material can never be committed. **env**: each app's gitignored `.env` composed — brand-env pass-throughs (the `.brands/{id}/.env` override layer dissolved into manage's shell > brand > company chain), per-surface `GOOGLE_ANALYTICS_SECRET` from analytics state, and signing paths (`CSC_LINK`, `APPLE_API_KEY`) stamped only when the cert file exists; keys update in place (every duplicate — dotenv lets the last win), new keys land in the Default Values section (`push-secrets` compatibility), Custom sections survive verbatim, multi-line blobs serialize as `\n` escapes dotenv expands back.
- The meta/tiktok pixel-token paste-in is live too — the one flow parked on this port: interactive runs save the entered token to the brand `.env` (`writeEnvValue` + `process.env`, the payment-secrets pattern) and disperse carries it to the backend; empty entry, non-interactive, and dry runs keep the warned guidance.
- Tests: manager 503 → 523 (+18 disperse over temp brand monorepos with the real desktop/backend framework `.env` templates pinning the cross-package key contract, mtime-proven converged reruns, dry-run zero-write for both operations; +2 pixel paste-in over fake TTYs).

- **Verification-poll adoptions (checkpoint 61).** Every wait-and-verify site now rides the devkit flow primitives in interactive runs: the pending Cloudflare zone opens the manual registrar's nameserver page (registrar knowledge extracted to `domain/lib/registrars.js` — API registrars still defer to the domain service) and polls for activation; email-routing retries the rule write once the destination address verifies (counted created/updated instead of unverified); Firebase hosting polls domain verification, writing records Firebase demands mid-poll (the ACME challenge appears once ownership passes) and proxying the CNAME after; AdSense opens the add-site page and polls until the domain appears; reCAPTCHA confirms the key's domain list once and stamps state (`domainsConfirmed` — a changed brand domain re-prompts); SendGrid domain-auth and Search Console verification poll out DNS propagation before the property add.
- Non-interactive and dry runs keep their exact one-shot + warned behavior — every poll is gated on `isInteractive() && !dryRun`, so a headless run never sits in a poll loop.
- Tests: manager 494 → 503 (+9 interactive polls end-to-end over fake TTYs with the browser opener stubbed — incl. the API-registrar deferral that never polls, the mid-poll ACME write + post-verify CNAME proxy, and the reCAPTCHA stamp never re-prompting).

- **Onboarding-flows port (checkpoint 60).** `@omegajs/devkit/flows` — TTY-safe browser-open + wait/poll primitives (`openBrowser` with a stub seam, `withSpinner`, `pollWithSpinner` with ENTER/S keyboard controls, `openBrowserAndPoll`), riding the prompt-stream seam and degrading to printed guidance without a TTY (a timer leak in omega-manager's wait loop fixed in the port). Manager side: `src/lib/config-flow.js` (`resolveConfigValue`) — the schema-prompter engine reshaped: uniform Yes/Skip/Disable gate, brand-match-sorted selections (cursor never lands on create-new by accident), create-new via API handler or browser + refreshed re-list, paste-back entry; every value lands in omega.json5 via the comment-preserving writeback and patches the in-memory config for the rest of the run.
- Missing required config now sets itself up in interactive runs: chatsy/replyify/slapform ids (Disable writes `<section>: false`), GA account + property (incl. Admin-API property creation with config `timeZone`/`currency`), AdSense account, Firebase project (incl. quota-aware creation inside `firebase.organizationId` — `listProjects`/`createProject` added to the API client), the three payment processors' keys (public halves → omega.json5, secrets → brand `.env` + `process.env` so the same run proceeds), and Beehiiv's create-publication browser poll. Non-interactive and dry runs keep their clean skips.
- Tests: devkit 99 (+14 flow units over fake TTY streams; shared harness extracted to `@omegajs/devkit/test/prompt-streams`), manager 494 (+25: config-flow engine + per-service flows, all real keystrokes, opener stubbed). Proof: scripted-TTY run through the REAL manage loop on a hand-commented scratch brand — agent id landed as a pure insertion with every comment intact, service then skipped cleanly with zero network, rerun prompted nothing and left the file byte-identical.

- **Config-writeback port (checkpoint 59).** `@omegajs/config` gained the comment-preserving omega.json5 editor omega-manager never had (its serializer re-stringified the whole file and dropped comments): `applyConfigEdits`/`writeConfigValues` make surgical span edits — existing leaves replaced, missing branches inserted, `[key=value]` array matchers so product writes self-locate by id, already-equal skips (reruns byte-identical), and a verification gate that re-parses after every edit so a corrupted config can never reach disk.
- Services now land resolved IDs in config/omega.json5 directly, with state as a mirror/resolution cache: sendgrid `marketing.campaigns.listId`, beehiiv `marketing.newsletter.publicationId`, stripe/paypal `payment.products[id=…].{stripe,paypal}.productId`, and firebase's `firebaseConfig` drift — previously warn + paste-block, now healed in place (dry-run keeps the paste block; state-known ids get promoted into the file; config-known ids leave it byte-identical). Manager side: `lib/config-write.js` (uniform dry-run gate + logging).
- Interactive selections (GA property, AdSense account, slapform/chatsy/replyify form/agent ids) stay parked by design — comments retargeted to the onboarding-flows port, which now has the writeback it was waiting on.
- Tests: manager 471, config 58 (+18 editor units incl. matcher, single-line/comment-only inserts, never-corrupt errors), devkit 84. Fixture proof: a CLI-onboarded scratch brand with hand-written comments took the full 8-path write set as pure insertions (diff = added lines only), reran to a byte-identical no-op, and loaded green through real manage.
- **Onboarding port, wizard slice (checkpoint 58).** `omega-manager onboard` — the brand-creation wizard: scaffolds the plan-§0 brand-monorepo skeleton (config/omega.json5 with brand identity + seeded `theme` + enabled `targets`, root package.json with `apps/*` workspaces, `.gitignore`, a fully-commented `.env` credential stub documenting every service's keys, README.md, minimal per-target app package.jsons) and proves the config loads before calling it done.
- Context-sensitive like manage: company roots place the brand under `brands.roots[0]` + stamp `.omega/company.json`; inside an existing brand it resumes (answers from the brand's own config, only missing files fill in); anywhere else the cwd becomes the brand root. Fill-missing semantics throughout — existing files are never touched, rerunning converges. Flags win, TTY prompts fill gaps with derived defaults, non-interactive derives everything from the id (`onboard --id=acme` = zero prompts); `--dry-run` plans without writing or prompting; `--manage`/`--no-manage` forces the child-manage handoff instead of asking.
- Deliberately structural: no framework deps wired into app package.jsons (each framework's setup owns its consumer internals; old-name deps in new-world brands would be a hybrid) — update records dep-less apps as skipped, and testing's "build output / firebase.json missing" errors on a fresh brand are the designed next-step nudge, pinned by test.
- Manager 453→465 tests: the wizard through real inquirer prompts (checkbox targets, declined manage confirm), never-overwrite convergence, dry-run-never-prompts, company placement + rediscovery, resume-from-inside, the workspace-green + honest-nudge manage pins, the real `--manage` child handoff. Live PTY proof (`script -q`) on a scratch brand: full wizard renders/resolves, then workspace green + a full `--dry-run` manage (2 passed, 20 clean skips, zero network, creds scrubbed). `--manage` stopped being a manage-command routing alias (bare `--manage` still lands on manage via the default) so onboard could own the flag.
- **Prompting port (checkpoint 57).** `@omegajs/devkit/prompt` — the TTY-safe `@inquirer/prompts` wrapper every OMEGA package uses instead of importing inquirer directly (`input`/`select`/`checkbox`/`confirm` + `isInteractive()`); no TTY → input/select/checkbox throw instead of hanging, `confirm` auto-returns its default unless `{ required: true }`.
- omega-manager's `setParallelMode` global is gone by construction — company-mode children are piped, so the TTY check covers parallel runs. Dry-run never prompts.
- Five parked manager flows are live interactively (non-interactive behavior unchanged): the Stripe Radar + Enhanced Dispute Protection confirms (`radarConfirmed`/`disputesConfirmed`), the Search Console GA-association confirm (`gaLinked`), the OAuth redirect-URI confirm (`authentication.oauthRedirectsConfigured` — previously had no setter), and the Firebase VAPID paste-back (length-validated, keys land in gitignored state; the browser auto-open was dropped — the console URL is printed).
- Tests drive the REAL inquirer prompts through fake TTY streams (`setPromptStreams` + `test/lib/interactive.js`); the fake output must ignore `end()` like a real TTY or the second prompt on a pair renders nothing. devkit 69→84, manager 447→453.
- Stale breadcrumb sweep: every "rides the prompting port" pointer now names its true remaining blocker (onboarding / config-writeback / extension / disperse port).
- **@omegajs/manager: company mode — many brands from one workspace (checkpoint 55, Task M3).** A **company root** is a directory whose `config/omega.json5` has a `brands` key (`brands: { roots: ['./brands'] }` — scan dirs RELATIVE to the company root, absolute paths are config errors; Ian's sibling-repos shape is `roots: ['..']`); anything under a root with its own `config/omega.json5` is a brand (the company itself and nested companies are skipped — one rung). The same `omega-manager` command, run from a company root, discovers the brands and runs **the per-brand manage as a child process per brand** (cwd = brand root): sequential by default with live output teed to `.omega/logs/{brandId}.log` at the company root (ANSI-stripped), `--parallel` for concurrent children (default concurrency = CPU count, `--concurrency=N`) with buffered per-brand output blocks + a TTY progress line, `--brand=<id[,id]>` filtering, and every other flag forwarded to children verbatim (the argv filter strips only the company flags). **Child processes are the design**: brands own their secrets, so each child builds its own env chain — shell > brand `.env` > company `.env` — and one brand's keys can never bleed into the next (omega-manager ran brands in-process with AsyncLocalStorage console patching; safe there only because its secrets weren't per-brand `.env` files); children also give crash containment and log files from plain pipe tees. Results aggregate into ONE summary from each brand's own `.omega/runs/` file; a child that dies before writing one gets a synthetic error entry so the aggregate never under-reports. **A brand behaves identically everywhere** (standalone / nested / loose sibling): company runs idempotently stamp `.omega/company.json` (`{ root }`) into each managed brand — manager bookkeeping in gitignored `.omega/`, exempt from `--dry-run` like run files — and brand-local runs read the stamp, layer the company config (minus `brands`, secret-shape-scanned like every omega.json5) between manager DEFAULTS and the brand file via the same agnostic deep-merge, load the company `.env` under the brand's, and print a `Company:` header line; a stale stamp warns and runs standalone. Disabled brands are stamped but never spawned. The company root's `.omega/` gets gitignored by the run itself (the ensure extracted to a shared lib — second consumer alongside the workspace service). Suite 429 → **447** (18 new, first-run green: discovery/detection/marker/layer/env-chain/argv-filter units plus five end-to-end runs with REAL manage children — sequential, `--brand`, `--parallel`, disabled brands, and the dies-before-run-output synthetic error). Live-proven on a scratch fixture company via the real CLI (creds scrubbed): sequential streaming with layered headers, real `--parallel` builds in both brands, `--brand` filtering, stamps + company-side logs, and a brand-local run inheriting company config + a company `.env` var through the stamp alone.
- **@omegajs/manager: testing-service live checks — omega-manager's service order is fully drained (checkpoint 54).** The testing service's target-checks operation grew from local file checks into omega-manager's full per-target health surface, grouped per app + a repo section. **New checks**: each app's **installed framework version** vs the npm latest (`TARGET_FRAMEWORKS` in config.js — names flip to @omegajs/\* at each rename cutover; the installed version resolves through the node_modules climb so file:/workspace refs read their real version, where omega-manager parsed the declared semver range; outdated → warned, unpublished/no-registry → dim note, backend deps read from `functions/`); web → **homepage fetch** on `brand.url` with 3 attempts + linear backoff, retrying network errors AND non-2xx (restoring the original website-check behavior omega-manager's consolidated target-checks had dropped — Node fetch's opaque `fetch failed` is unwrapped to the cause, e.g. `getaddrinfo ENOTFOUND`); backend → **API health** on `https://api.{host}/backend-manager/test/health` (the @omegajs/backend `getApiUrl` + route-mount convention) with the payload's deployed version compared to the npm latest, `firebase.shared` brands skipping (the owning brand deploys); repo-level → **working tree clean** (`git status --porcelain -- .` so a brand nested in a larger workspace only sees its own files; not a repo → silent) and the **latest GitHub Actions run** via `gh run list` on the ONE brand repo (`github.org`/`github.repo || brandId` — **de-ITW'd: omega-manager defaulted the org to `itw-creative-works`**;
