Operations: **zone** (created when missing; pending zones report nameservers and move on — omega-manager's interactive browser-open + poll rides the prompting port; a zone created THIS run is visible to every later operation via serviceData, fixing omega-manager's first-run wart where handlers read the setup-time null zoneId), **dns-records** (platform record set + custom records diff-synced: GitHub Pages A/AAAA, www CNAME, email-provider MX/SPF, DMARC; obsolete wrong-provider MX deleted; SPF enforcement + DMARC policy/report-ordering normalized in place), **email-routing** (catch-all + forwarding rules synced, stale rules removed, catch-all disabled when unconfigured; unverified destinations get a verification email sent + warned with the dashboard URL instead of the interactive poll), **zone-settings** (~40 settings diffed in one pass incl. the speed_brain/fonts addon reads; read-only settings skipped), **cache-rules / rules-redirect / rules-configuration** (entrypoint rulesets reconciled by rule name — create missing, update stale, remove unconfigured, POST the ruleset on first use), **rules-response-headers / rules-security** (PUT-style phases with metadata-insensitive comparison), **rules-managed-transforms**, **speed-scheduled-tests** (inactive zones skipped until nameservers propagate), **workers** (scripts + routes incl. stale deletion; the backend-manager-proxy worker ships with the service; route templating via the manager's own templateObject, dropping the node-powertools dependency). Subdomain brands (brand.url below the apex, via psl) use the parent zone and only run zone + dns-records. **Company-specific values moved from engine hardcode to config**: `dns.dmarcReports { rua, ruf }`, `dns.bimiLogo`, `dns.sendgrid { id, whitelabel }`, `dns.spfIncludes`, verification TXTs via `dns.records`, and the default CSP rule lost its hardcoded itwcreativeworks.com hosts — Ian's values land in company/brand config at migration. One real regression this surfaced and fixed: with Ahrefs-style verification TXTs becoming *custom* records, the type+name override check would have silently suppressed the apex SPF default — custom TXT records are now ADDITIVE (TXT coexists at one name) and required-TXT existence matches by record kind (spf/dmarc/bimi/exact-content), so policy records still converge by content update while verification tokens create alongside them. `--dry-run` = reads only across all 12 operations. Auth: `CLOUDFLARE_TOKEN` in the brand `.env`; no token → clean skip. DEFAULTS gained the full cloudflare section (+ the `domain` provider/email section the dns/email operations read); SERVICE_ORDER: workspace → github → cloudflare → update → testing. Suite 42 → **62**: converged-zone zero-mutation no-op across all 12 operations, DNS drift (create/update/delete incl. two-run convergence semantics), config-gated company extras, ruleset create-vs-update, zone-settings read-only skips, email-routing converged/unverified, subdomain filtering, per-operation dry-run guarantees; manage e2e now proves the no-token skip through the full loop (and scrubs any shell CLOUDFLARE_TOKEN so fixtures can never reach a real account). Live-proven on sandbox-brand with the token explicitly scrubbed: clean skip with guidance, 3 passed / 2 skipped, zero repo changes — per the standing rule, no real Cloudflare resources were touched.
- **@omegajs/manager: the github service — the first external provisioning port (checkpoint 34).** Three operations reconciled to the brand's `github: {}` config through the `gh` CLI (`gh auth login`, or `GH_TOKEN` in the brand `.env`): **org** — profile diff-then-patch (display name, `support@{domain}` + billing email, description with the 160-char org cap, blog URL; `location` only reconciled when `github.location` is configured — omega-manager hardcoded a country, config owns it now; owner-isn't-an-org is a note, not an error; `github.shared: true` filters the operation out entirely so one brand never rewrites a shared org's profile); **repo** — THE brand-monorepo repo (`github.repo`, default = brand id; new world = one repo per brand, replacing omega-manager's per-target template repos): created EMPTY when missing (the brand exists locally — you push; no template/clone, that's onboarding's job) with visibility/description/homepage, existing repos get exactly the drifted fields patched (`-F` typed booleans so `private` arrives as JSON, not the string omega-manager sent), identity recorded in `.omega/state.json` for later services; **pages** — gh-pages source + custom domain from `brand.url` for web brands (missing gh-pages branch = deploy-first guidance, not an error; old-world subdomain Pages repos don't map to one-repo brands — parked for the cloudflare port). `--dry-run` = reads only: every drift reported as `planned`, zero mutations. Manager DEFAULTS gained the `github` section (`shared: false`, `private: true`); SERVICE_ORDER = workspace → github → update → testing; no `github.org` configured → clean skip with guidance. Old-world behaviors (cloning, GitHub Desktop, per-repo setup commands) ride the onboarding/company-mode ports. Suite 27 → **42**: the github service against a recording fake API — converged brand = zero-mutation no-op, drift patches exactly the drifted fields, org-description truncation, shared-org filtering, repo-name override, deploy-first pages, dry-run zero-mutation guarantee — plus the manage e2e now proving the no-config skip through the full loop. Live-proven twice: sandbox-brand full run (github skips cleanly, 3 passed / 1 skipped, zero repo changes) and a **read-only dry-run against the real `itw-creative-works/ultimate-jekyll` repo** — real settings diffed (public vs the `private: true` default), shared-org filter active, real branch probe, zero writes. Company mode's design was settled with Ian the same day and recorded as PROGRESS Task M3 (self-similar company → `brands/` → brand → `apps/` → app hierarchy; `brands.roots` discovery; the `.omega/company.json` stamp) — build rides later ports.
- **@omegajs/manager (`packages/manager`) — omega-manager's orchestration engine lands in the monorepo, pointed at brand monorepos (checkpoint 33; migrator work paused per Ian's directive).** `npx omega-manager` resolves the brand root from any cwd inside it (same walk-up rule as @omegajs/config — a brand nested in a parent workspace's `apps/` still resolves), loads `config/omega.json5` as THE brand config (no `.brands/` mirror), and walks SERVICE_ORDER running each service's operations idempotently. The three-bucket principle relocates into the brand repo: omega.json5 = user choices, `.omega/state.json` = durable derived IDs, `.omega/runs/{ts}.json` = per-run transients (the manager gitignores `.omega/` itself). Ported core: the service-runner with the strict `{ state, output, status, error }` handler contract (handler loading is now existence-checked, so a broken handler file throws loudly instead of being silently skipped), the run summary with update/testing drill-downs and a retry command, and `{ domain }` config templating. First services: **workspace** (root `apps/*` workspaces; an app per enabled target — app→target via declared `targets` in the app's own omega.json5 including BEM's `functions/config/` layout, else `website*`/`backend*`/… dir conventions; brand-config health as a hard gate; `.omega/` gitignore), **update** (ONE resolution-gated `npm install` at the brand root — every declared dep probed through the node_modules climb, so a brand inside a bigger workspace never grows a stray install — then each app's own `npm run build`; script-less apps are recorded skipped, not failed), **testing** (web → `dist/index.html`; backend → `firebase.json` + `functions/package.json`; all → parseable package.json). CLI on the devkit router (`manage` default, `--service`, `--dry-run`, `--continue-on-error`, exit 1 on errors). 27 tests: runner contract, brand loading (incl. secret-key hard-fail and declared-beats-convention mapping), staged-fixture e2e with a real `npm run build`, idempotent rerun, and dry-run honesty (testing reports the unbuilt site). Live-proven on `apps/sandbox-brand`: both apps map, install correctly skipped, the website builds, 5/5 health checks, and a second run changes nothing. External provisioning services (github → cloudflare → domain → firebase → …) port next in omega-manager's order.
- **Phase B4 (Task 2.8): `omega migrate` + the liquid-lint scanner land in `packages/web` — the universal conversion plan is now a tool, live-proven on real consumers.** One command converts a UJM (Jekyll) consumer in place; `omega migrate --check` runs the whole pipeline in memory and prints the report without writing a byte. Config: `src/_config.yml` + `config/ultimate-jekyll-manager.json` → `config/omega.json5` with the unified spellings (flat `analytics.{google,meta,tiktok}` → `analytics.providers.<p>.id`, `web_manager.firebase.app.config` → top-level `firebaseConfig`, `web_manager.payment` → top-level `payment` with legacy `credential: false` idioms dropped, the remaining `web_manager` client blob + presentation sections + `permalink`/`pagination`/`collections`/`defaults`/`generators` + the UJM-json build settings under `targets.web`, Jekyll machinery + `webpack`/`gems` dropped with notes, secret-shaped keys stripped with move-to-.env warnings) — validated through the real `loadConfig(root, 'web')` before the report prints; the full mapping table joined `docs/config.md`. Codemod: the 8 A2/DECISION.md rule classes are now executable, ordered text transforms with per-line edit records — `page.resolved.`→`resolved.`, bracket/hardcoded theme layout prefixes, interpolated quoted tag args → `{% capture %}` hoists scoped to the tag span (semantically proven: the hoist renders byte-identical to a literal arg through the real registerLiquid path), `forloop.parentloop.*` → depth-scoped hoisted assigns via a token-aware for/endfor stack (same-line ambiguities are flagged for manual porting, never mis-hoisted), include leading-slash strips, `page.canonical.url` → `{{ site.url }}{{ page.url }}`, `page.<key>` → `<key>` with a lookbehind guard (a real somiibo post's `words-on-a-page.png` was the false positive that earned it) and no-fix flags for `page.next/previous/collection`, plus the flat-analytics spelling rewrite. Lint: known filter/tag names are derived at runtime from the same LiquidJS + template-kit registration the engine uses (no static list to drift); Jekyll-only tags (`post_url`, `highlight`, …) are errors with hints, unknown names are warnings — the first real run caught sweet-saucy's `| limit: 3` (broken upstream too; Jekyll never had a `limit` filter). Consumer asset layer: a seed-identical `src/assets/js/main.js` is deleted (the core main + ESM boot runtime replace it; customized ones get the exact port recipe as an error finding), `@use 'ultimate-jekyll-manager' with (…)` theme-variable customization is rewritten to `@use 'omega:main' with (…)` — the layered sass importer now skips the requesting file (containingUrl), so a consumer `main.scss` configures the main bundle from the layers below it and `with (…)` reaches the theme variables through core main's `@forward 'omega:theme'` — and page-css self-`@use 'pages/<key>'` lines are dropped (theme page css ships separately as `pageAssets.themeCss`; the self-use was a module loop AND a double-load). Legacy files (`Gemfile`, `Gemfile.lock`, the two configs, `.ruby-version`) are removed; reruns report already-migrated and rewrites are idempotent. The chrome normalized to the unified analytics spelling (gtag/fbq/tiktok read `providers.<p>.id`; `Configuration.analytics` bridges to the client's flat shape — the same pattern the extension framework uses) and the engine composes `firebaseConfig`/`payment` back into the `web_manager` runtime spots, so omega.json5 keeps ONE home per value with the template/client contract unchanged. Two real-data engine bugs fixed on the way: blog taxonomy terms now dedupe by slug with the most frequent spelling as the display name (somiibo ships `"Marketing"` ×673 and `"marketing"` ×11 — Jekyll silently last-write-won the duplicate permalink, Eleventy hard-errors), and the LIVE somiibo interpolated-icon bug (`class="fa text- display-4"`) was found in 12 latent copies inside the packaged classy layouts by our own rule 3 and fixed — plus fixed forward in consumer files by the codemod. Live verification: a deployment-playground copy migrates and builds **57 pages in 2.33 s** with the consumer's `$primary` in the compiled css, real gtag id, and composed firebaseConfig verified in the output; a REAL somiibo copy migrates (73 edits across 23 files, one honest manual flag for its customized main.js) and builds **2,556 pages in ~96–104 s vs the 332 s Jekyll baseline (~3.2× whole-pipeline on the real site)** with the marketing category built once and zero broken icon classes (the live site still ships them). Suite: web 52/52 (+14 migrate tests incl. semantic render proofs, false-positive guards, zero-write check mode, real-loader validation, and composition-through-chrome); corpus spot-check 18.14 s (no regression). B5's first job is recorded: somiibo's 2,556 vs Jekyll's 2,608 files is the `omega verify` URL-set diff.
- **Phase B3 (Task 2.7): the `omega` CLI, consumer scaffolding, the ESM boot runtime, and the Ruby-free CI template land in `packages/web`.** The CLI (bin `omega`, devkit's shared router — no new dispatcher): `setup` (scaffold + package.json script sync), `dev` (dev-mode assets with stable un-hashed names rebuilt in place on source changes + programmatic Eleventy watch/serve, `--port`), `build` (omega.json5 → `buildSite` → dist/), `test` (production build + smoke checks + consumer `node --test`), `deploy` (UJM parity: refuse `file:` deps → build → `npu sync`), `clean`, `version`; `translate`/`audit` ship as explicit not-ported-yet stubs. Scaffolding runs the devkit defaults engine over the new `scaffold/` tree: marker-section merges for `.gitignore`/`.env`/`CLAUDE.md`, a validated `config/omega.json5` seed (JSON5 defaults-merge, consumer values win), `.nvmrc` + the **Ruby-free CI workflow** (checkout → setup-node → sfw install → `npx omega setup && npm run build` → gh-pages from `./dist` — no Ruby, no bundler, no Gemfile) re-templated every run, and **zero page copying** (the ~60-page default set stays virtual). The B2 boot stubs are replaced by a real runtime handshake: bundles switched to **ESM + code splitting**, so web-manager and `runtime/boot.js` live in ONE shared chunk the browser evaluates once — every `import webManager from 'web-manager'` across the 48 page modules is the same initialized singleton (separate IIFE bundles would each have instantiated their own; page bundles collapsed to ~330-byte stubs). `bootMain` (initialize with `window.Configuration` → dev lib in development → global module) and `bootPage` (awaits the main boot) reproduce UJM's `{ manager, options }` page-module contract with a frontend Manager carrying mode helpers. Live-verified: a bare consumer scaffolds and builds **56 default pages in 1.65 s**, serves them on `omega dev`, and passes `omega test`. Gates: web 38/38 (6 new CLI/scaffold tests incl. a real-bin spawn; ESM singleton-in-one-chunk + dev-mode pins; theme contract now pins `type="module"`), template-kit 41, config 38, devkit 69, shared 4, astro 17; corpus post-ESM spot-check 1,397 files in 18.6 s (thin page stubs made the asset phase faster). Fix found by the dev command: Eleventy's programmatic `watch()` requires `init()` first (`write()` self-inits, `watch()` doesn't).

### Changed
- **`packages/extension` (browser-extension-manager) reads `config/omega.json5` — `config/browser-extension-manager.json` is no longer read anywhere** (third framework on the hard flip; the smallest — `targets.extension` is near-empty by design). `Manager.getConfig()` resolves through the vendored `@omegajs/config` (missing file → `{}`, schema findings warn once per process — BXM has no separate audit surface), which flows to every gulp task and the `BXM_BUILD_JSON` runtime snapshot. **The template's `analytics.providers.google.secret` moved to `.env` as `GOOGLE_ANALYTICS_SECRET`** (the loader hard-fails secret-shaped keys — BXM was the last framework carrying a secret in config; the build snapshot bakes it from the environment at build time, same value flow, matches BEM/EM convention). Consumers get `require('browser-extension-manager/config')` (the vendored loader). Bonus fix the flip surfaced: the `bxm setup` defaults merge started from the framework template and only walked template keys, silently DELETING consumer-only config keys (e.g. `liveReloadPort`, anything under `targets.extension`) on every setup — consumer keys now survive at every nesting level. Migration recipe in `packages/extension/CHANGELOG.md`; mapping table in `docs/config.md` marked done. Gates: BXM suite 88 passing (85 pre-flip baseline + 2 new getConfig tests + 1 auto-generated exports-map test for the new `./config` export), config suite 35, pack-smoke 9/9 (brand walk-up + overlay via the installed `Manager.getConfig`, secret hard-fail, config export, shipped template resolves clean; legacy file absent from the tarball).
- **`packages/backend` (backend-manager) reads `functions/config/omega.json5` — `backend-manager-config.json` is no longer read anywhere** (second framework on the hard flip, biggest config surface). `Manager.init()` resolves the consumer config through the vendored `@omegajs/config` with `templates/config/omega.json5` (same loader, resolved) as the framework-defaults layer — the old lodash `mergeWith` template merge, `requireJSON5` helper, `backendManagerConfigPath` option, and legacy `app.id → brand.id` fallback are all gone; boot warns on schema findings, `npx mgr setup` remains the hard audit. The loader gained the **functions-dir normalization**: BEM's runtime cwd is `functions/`, so the brand walk-up now treats the app root as one level up — `loadConfig(functionsDir, 'backend')` ≡ `loadConfig(appRoot, 'backend')` (new config test proves both entry points resolve identically). CLI surface flipped with it: setup scaffolds/loads `functions/config/omega.json5` (setup-test `bem-config` → `omega-config`, comparing RESOLVED consumer config against the RESOLVED template so brand-monorepo consumers pass), `project-id-consistency` checks the resolved projectId and fixes via an app-level override, `mgr test`/`firebase-init`/`public-files` all read through the loader. The sandbox dogfoods the FULL hierarchy: shared sections moved to `apps/sandbox-brand/config/omega.json5` (brand level), the backend app file carries only `targets.backend`. Migration recipe in `packages/backend/CHANGELOG.md`; mapping table in `docs/config.md` marked done. Gates: BEM self-test, sandbox corpus 1203 passing / 44 skipped (pre-flip baseline) THROUGH the brand walk-up, cross-stack e2e 11/11, config suite 35, pack-smoke 9/9 (walk-up + secret hard-fail + template resolution from a scratch install; legacy template file absent from the tarball).
- **`packages/desktop` (electron-manager) reads `config/omega.json5` — the legacy `config/electron-manager.json` is no longer read anywhere** (dual-read cancelled by design: implement the new way, document the migration). `Manager.getConfig()` and the dev-mode boot fallback resolve through the vendored `@omegajs/config` (shared sections top-level, `targets.desktop` overlaid, brand-monorepo walk-up); packaged apps carry the RESOLVED config in `EM_BUILD_JSON`; boot + `gulp audit` validate against the shared schema (EM's own `src/config/schema.js` + `src/utils/validate-config.js` are deleted — they were the seed). Per-OS config moved `targets.{mac,win,linux}` → `platforms.{mac,win,linux}`. Consumers get a `require('electron-manager/config')` export (the vendored loader) — the scaffolded workflow's snap/signing gates use it instead of raw JSON5 reads. Migration is a one-time file conversion (mapping table in the monorepo's `docs/config.md`); existing consumers must convert when they take this release. Gates: EM suite 751 passing / 5 designed skips (pre-flip baseline), pack-smoke 9/9 (vendored loader + export + secret hard-fail from a scratch install, no legacy file in the tarball), 17 EM docs swept.

### Added
- **`packages/web` — the REAL UJM content is IN the package (Phase B2, Task 2.6): full blueprint set, 3 themes + bootstrap, ~60 default pages, the real chrome + runtime — ported by scripted codemod (521 files copied, 127 Liquid-transformed).** Content: core layer owns `_layouts/blueprint/**` (45 page-type contracts), `_layouts/core/root.html`, `_layouts/modules/utilities/redirect.html`, the real `_includes` chrome (head/body/foot — asset blocks rewritten from fixed `main.bundle.*` paths + `uj_cachebreak` to the content-hash manifest + a new engine `pageAssets` computed; default nav/footer/account/sidebar/topbar data JSONs ship in core so a bare site has a working nav), the real UJM css tree (`main.scss` ex-`ultimate-jekyll-manager.scss`, forwarding the theme via `omega:theme`) and the real UJM js runtime (`main.js` ex-`ultimate-jekyll-manager.js` with static conditional imports — webpack expression-contexts don't exist in esbuild — plus core/libs/modules and the full per-page module tree); `themes/` = classy FULL (frontend+backend+admin layouts + includes + assets), neobrutalism + newsflash (partial, classy fills per file via layer resolution — which also FIXED neobrutalism's dangling `themes/neobrutalism/...` include reference), vendored `bootstrap/` (sibling scss/js the themes import) and `_template/`; `defaults/pages/**` = ~60 real pages at real URLs (404 at literal `/404.html`, paginated blog `size 6` + `/blog/page/N.html`, categories/tags indexes + per-term GENERATOR pages over the aggregated taxonomy — dynamic-pages.rb's replacement). Codemod applied (and recorded for B4): bracket + hardcoded `themes/<id>/` layout-prefix strips, include leading-slash strip, `page.resolved.`→`resolved.`, body-only `page.<key>`→`resolved.<key>` (83 refs; frontmatter keeps `page.*` for the COW renderer), `page.canonical.*`→`site.url`/`page.url` forms, `layout: none`→dropped. **Engine grew the real-world mechanics**: consumer `_layouts` as the top layout layer (sweet-saucy's pattern; ignored as content via a CWD-RELATIVE glob — `../`-relative input dirs miss bare `**/` ignores), core as a layout layer, the bracket-alias table generalized over the whole layout map, `resolved` seeded with the site config (inject-properties.rb parity: `site ← layout chain ← page`, collections/bulk keys excluded) with `resolved`/`page.resolved` available to frontmatter-value rendering (layout defaults template on merged data), Jekyll site emulation (`site.posts/team/updates/alternatives` as real arrays SYNCED IN PLACE when collections compute — Eleventy captures the object's key set at data-init, so getters/reassignment are invisible; `site.data._includes.*` built from `.json` files in the layered include roots; `paginator` compat over Eleventy pagination; `jekyll.environment` + `site.uj` defaults), `updates` collection + `/team/:title` `/updates/:title` permalinks with explicit-permalink-wins (and `''` counting as absent — Eleventy's computed dependency pass probes with empty-string proxies that persist). **Asset pipeline rebuilt on LAYER ROOTS** (site → active theme → classy → core; conventions `js|css/main.*` + `js|css/pages/**` + theme `_theme.scss`/`_theme.js`): entries wrapped in boot stubs (module imported, default-function export called with the web-manager singleton — the full runtime Manager handshake is B3), `__main_assets__/*`→core/themes, `__theme__/*`→active theme, `web-manager`→@omegajs/client DIRECTORY (subpath imports work), manifest v2 `{ js: { main, pages }, css: { main, pages, themePages } }` (base + theme page css BOTH load), per-page css compiled with owner-root loadPaths; new deps: chart.js, dompurify, lodash, wonderful-fetch, @popperjs/core (the admin/runtime modules' real imports). **FOUR REAL BUGS found and fixed by the port**: (1) template-kit block tags rendered their bodies EAGERLY — a falsy `{% iftruthy %}` around a re-`{% assign %}` still executed it, silently emptying every `<title>`/meta on every UJM page (upstream Jekyll behaves lazily; adapter now probes and renders the body only when the tag asks); (2) `jsonify` of an absent value emitted nothing → invalid JS in the web-manager Configuration block (Jekyll emits `null`); (3) collection docs collided at `/index.html` via the computed-proxy `''` permalink artifact; (4) corpus eleventy phase profiled 40.6 s → **16.7 s**: LiquidJS `cache: true` (the 885-line chrome was re-parsed per render) + the COW walker now skips Eleventy items via `'templateContent' in node` WITHOUT invoking the throwing getter (error construction was ~19% CPU), and site collection arrays stay out of the `resolved` seed. `uj_post` title/description now prefer `post.post.*` (UJM posts carry the real title there; Jekyll's `data.title` was filename-derived). **Suites: web 30/30** — slice rewritten against real content (real markers, neobrutalism-over-classy layering, farm parity), ports fixture owns contact/recipe as CONSUMER-LOCAL `_layouts`, assets suite on the new API, **NEW `contract.test.js` + `contract-site` fixture: every packaged theme builds the ENTIRE default set (66 pages) for a bare consumer** — key URLs, taxonomy/collection pages, markdown legal layouts, manifest injection, zero unresolved Liquid; astro 17/17 (the B1 asset pipeline FROZEN + vendored into the spike at `src/assets-pipeline.js`; shared imports unchanged); template-kit 41/41 (lazy-tag semantics, jsonify null, uj_post pins updated); corpus harness: the somiibo replica layouts moved to `bakeoff-shared/fixtures/consumer-layouts/` and the generator writes them into corpus `_layouts/` (consumer-layout layer dogfood), bench touch targets → real paths. **B2 corpus baseline: 1,397 HTML files, cold ×3 mean 20.40 s (σ 0.97)** — the real chrome now renders on every page; A2's 3.52 s record was the synthetic slice. Engine-vs-engine per page: Eleventy ~12.7 ms vs Jekyll ~68.6 ms on equivalent real content (≈5.4×); the real somiibo whole-pipeline number is the Phase-4 scale test.
- **`packages/web` — `@omegajs/web@0.1.0` EXISTS (Phase B1, Task 2.5): the engine core promoted from the winning bake-off spike** per the DECISION.md disposition. The spike's `omega-web.js` split into focused modules — `src/engine.js` (`configureOmega()`: Liquid options + template-kit registration on Eleventy's own LiquidJS, layered layouts, legacy bracket-layout aliases, frontmatter preprocessor, the `resolved` computed cascade, default pages, globals), `src/collections.js` (posts/alternatives/team + blog taxonomy with the shared deterministic sort), `src/layouts.js` (virtual-template / symlink-farm delivery) — joined by the four SSG-agnostic modules moved in from bakeoff-shared (`frontmatter-liquid.js`, `layers.js`, `consumer-scan.js`, `assets.js`), a NEW `src/build.js` (`buildSite()`: assets → Eleventy → PurgeCSS orchestration with per-phase timings — the `omega build` seed), and `src/paths.js` (packaged content locations: `configureOmega`/`buildSite` now DEFAULT `themesDir`/`coreDir`/`defaultsDir` to the package's own `themes/`, `core/`, `defaults/`, all moved in from the spike as the B2 porting seed). The full behavior-pinning suite moved WITH the package: **22/22** (13 slice + 4 asset-pipeline + 5 real-layout ports) over the relocated `test/fixtures/{mini-site,ports-site,site-assets}` — test glob narrowed to `test/*.test.js` because `node --test` treats every `.js` under `test/` as a test file (it executed the site-assets fixture as a "test"). Package is `private: true` until first publish (B3), with subpath exports per module. **Both spikes are now thin harnesses**: bakeoff-eleventy = bench harness only (corpus build via `buildSite`, `OMEGA_TIMINGS` print, dev-serve config through `@omegajs/web`, touch-targets → packaged themes, own test script removed), bakeoff-astro = retained reference importing `@omegajs/web/{frontmatter-liquid,consumer-scan,assets}` + the moved fixtures, bakeoff-shared = corpus generator + bench + records (deps slimmed to fs-jetpack). CI: the bakeoff-eleventy step became the `packages/web` suite step. Gates: web 22/22, astro 17/17 (serialized), bakeoff-shared 4/4, template-kit 41/41, config 38/38, dev config smoke-loads, and the full corpus build through the promoted package emits **1,279 HTML files with the complete 4-module asset manifest and the site-assets layer winning resolution** (~3.6–3.9 s under load vs the recorded 3.52 s bench; zero hot-path changes — pure module split + moves).
- **A2 scoring + decision memo (Task 2.4) — the bake-off is DECIDED: `@omegajs/web` builds on ELEVENTY 3** (`spikes/bakeoff-shared/DECISION.md`; weighted 4.70 vs 3.55, margin 1.15 — the <0.5-defaults-to-Eleventy rule never fired). The 20% migration-cost dimension is measured, not estimated: **three REAL files ported into BOTH candidates** (UJM classy contact 337 L, sweet-saucy recipe 412 L + the real adsense include, somiibo index 456 L + its hero-demo include; new shared `fixtures/ports-site` with the real UJM team doc + verbatim recipe doc; mirrored port suites assert the full JSON-LD Recipe schema, `uj_member` against a real team collection, and adsense `include`-param output). Result: **Eleventy = 43 single-line mechanical edits (0 hand-rewrites, all regex-able) vs Astro = ~700 lines hand-rewritten**; census across the 46 site repos (73 consumer layouts + 59 includes = 18.9 k lines, +115 UJM framework theme files = 19.7 k lines) projects Astro's bill at **~247 files / ~38.6 k lines by hand** vs an `omega migrate` codemod pass for Eleventy. The **8 extracted codemod rules are the consumer conversion-plan seed** (`page.resolved.`→`resolved.`; bracket layouts; `{{ }}`-in-tag-args hoist — which exposed a LIVE somiibo production bug, `class="fa text- display-4"`; `forloop.parentloop` hoists — a LiquidJS gap; include leading-slash; `page.<key>` forms; canonical; per-collection defaults). **Output parity: 1,279/1,279 corpus pages normalized-identical ACROSS candidates** after alignment fixes (Astro post-date zero-padding to Jekyll's `%d`; Astro auto-escaping vs Liquid's never-escapes → `set:html` audits, recorded as an XSS-policy finding; deterministic same-date post sort in both engines; taxonomy meta titles + tag `#name` headings). Engine features BOTH candidates needed (→ @omegajs/web requirements regardless of winner): team collection for `uj_member`, consumer `_includes` in Liquid roots restricted to EXISTING dirs (a nonexistent root cost **+1.05 s/corpus** in LiquidJS stat probes — caught by re-benching against a clean-worktree baseline), page-scoped frontmatter Liquid (`{{ page.recipe.title }}` in layout meta defers to a per-page copy-on-write render; the Eleventy preprocessor sees the FULL cascade and layout data objects are SHARED across pages — the shared resolver's new `renderData()` is COW and treats foreign getter-throwing objects as opaque), template-kit date filters flipped to UTC (CI-Jekyll parity: `date_to_xmlschema`/`date_to_rfc822`, test pinned exact). Re-benched after all changes: **Eleventy 3.52 s (σ 0.035), Astro 5.31 s (σ 0.176)**. Disposition: Phase B promotes the bakeoff-eleventy engine core into `packages/web`; the Astro spike stays in-tree as reference; template-kit/config/shared-pipeline/codemod rules carry forward regardless. Gates: eleventy 22/22 (17 + 5 ports), astro 17/17 (13 + 4 ports; suite serialized — parallel test files collide on the shared `.astro` store), bakeoff-shared 4/4, template-kit 41/41.
- `spikes/bakeoff-astro` (**Task 2.3b, candidate 2**) — the complete A1 slice on Astro 5.18.2 building the shared corpus. **Cold full build 5.56 s mean (σ 0.40, ×3 + warmup) vs 3.60 s Eleventy and 332 s Jekyll (~60×)** — producing 1,279 HTML files with a **URL set IDENTICAL to the Eleventy build** (find-diff verified); phase split: assets 0.15 s, Astro 4.60 s, PurgeCSS 0.36 s (wiping the persistent `.astro/` store makes no material difference). **Every load-bearing Astro bet validated (probed first)**: theme layering via an `omega:` Vite resolveId plugin (cross-layer component imports — classy's base picks up dusk's head override, zero copying) + `import.meta.glob` layout dispatch for Jekyll `layout:` names; content collections (Content Layer) over the consumer dir; `.astro` layouts exporting `defaults` consts with manual chain merge + `computeResolved()` deep-merge = the `page.resolved` equivalent; template-kit as DIRECT imports (filters as functions, tags via a thin ctx builder); default pages in two shapes (content-shaped via collection + catch-all suppression, route-shaped blog/taxonomy via an `injectRoute` integration that skips consumer-owned URLs); the SHARED asset pipeline (extracted this checkpoint) staged + copied post-build. Honest findings recorded: **layouts port from Liquid to .astro BY HAND** (~200 custom files across 49 sites — the dominant migration cost) while content stays Liquid (full liquidjs+template-kit engine ships regardless); the glob loader has no `.html` entry type (custom frontmatter loader); linked CJS workspace packages need `vite.ssr.external`; SSR chunks carry the chunk's `import.meta.url` (paths anchored at config time); NO incremental static rebuild (rebuild = full ~5.6 s; dev = on-demand — ~16.5 s boot over the corpus, then <50 ms warm renders, ~0.7–1.3 s single-page re-renders after touches); HTTPS native via Vite `server.https`. **DRY extraction into `spikes/bakeoff-shared`**: the SSG-agnostic asset pipeline (`src/assets.js`), layered resolution (`src/layers.js`), frontmatter-Liquid resolver, consumer permalink scan (`src/consumer-scan.js`), and the mini-site fixture moved out of the eleventy spike — both candidates now build the identical consumer content through the identical pipeline. Gates: astro spike 13/13 (each test build a REAL `astro build`; suite ~25 s vs Eleventy's ~1 s — itself recorded as bake-off data), eleventy 17/17 re-gated after the extraction, bakeoff-shared 4/4, template-kit 41/41, full-corpus build + spot-checks (pricing math, 1,030-post pagination ×103 pages, 6+12 taxonomy, package→solution→base 3-deep chain, real @omegajs/client in signin, icon byte-parity with the Eleventy output), CI gained the suite step. RESULTS.md Astro column + checklist filled — **both A1 candidates are now complete; next is A2 scoring**.
- `spikes/bakeoff-eleventy` (**Task 2.3, candidate 1**) — the complete A1 slice on Eleventy 3.1.6 building the shared corpus. **Cold full build 3.60 s mean (σ 0.025, ×3 + warmup) vs the 332 s Jekyll baseline — ~92×** — producing 1,279 HTML files (1,030 posts + 105 pages + 20 alternatives + 103 blog-index + 18 taxonomy + 3 default pages); phase split: esbuild+sass 0.60 s, Eleventy 2.64 s, PurgeCSS 0.42 s. Watch-mode reload ~1.9–2.0 s (caveat recorded: `--incremental` doesn't narrow under collection-paginated templates — full re-render every touch; B-phase optimization). **Every load-bearing Eleventy bet validated**: layered layouts as v3 virtual templates (zero copying, cross-layer layout chaining, extensionless Jekyll layout names resolve against virtuals) with the symlink FARM as the watchable dev mode (farm must live OUTSIDE the input dir); LiquidJS `jekyllInclude` + `root:`-array layered includes; template-kit `registerLiquid` via `amendLibrary`; the data cascade IS `page.resolved` (layout frontmatter deep-merges under page frontmatter natively — `eleventyComputed.resolved` + one mechanical `page.resolved.` → `resolved.` rewrite covers UJM's 967 refs; `layout.*` refs: zero). Slice: blueprint index/pricing (Liquid resolve-plan math), signin/signup default pages bundling the REAL `@omegajs/client` via a `web-manager` esbuild alias, blog + pagination + taxonomy from `post.categories/tags`, 404, consumer-suppressible virtual default pages, dusk/classy layered themes, 3-layer page modules (site > theme > core) with content-hashed manifest, sass layering via an `omega:` scheme importer (bare `@use` resolves file-relative BEFORE loadPaths — real finding), PurgeCSS post-pass, frontmatter `{{ site.* }}` rendering (cached, machinery-keys skip) incl. the legacy `[ site.theme.id ]` bracket-layout hack via `addLayoutAlias` (layout values resolve before preprocessors — a data transform can never catch them). HTTPS dev story DECIDED: the dev server supports `https: { key, cert }` natively (mkcert) — no BrowserSync proxy. Migration findings (timezoneOffset 0 for CI-parity dates, same-process template-cache pitfall, etc.) in the spike README; measurements + per-item checklist in `spikes/bakeoff-shared/RESULTS.md`. Gates: spike suite 17/17 (13 slice invariants vs a mini fixture through REAL Eleventy incl. farm-vs-virtual byte-parity, 4 asset-pipeline tests incl. real-client bundling and PurgeCSS strip), full-corpus build + page spot-checks green, CI gained the suite step.
- `@omegajs/template-kit` (**Task 2.2**) — the COMPLETE jekyll-uj-powertools template surface as plain, engine-neutral JS: all 13 uj_* filters (pure ports plus the context-coupled `uj_liquify`/`uj_content_format`/`uj_increment_return` as injectable factories) and all 16 tags under their exact registered names (including the unprefixed `iftruthy`/`iffalsy`/`iffile`/`urlmatches` — the usage audit found those are the heavy hitters: uj_icon 612×, iftruthy ~370× in UJM's theme). Tag renderers take an injected ctx (`lookup`/`page`/`site.{config,getCollection,getCollectionNames,fileExists}`/options) so both bake-off candidates consume the same functions; `registerLiquid(engine, options)` binds everything to a real LiquidJS instance (async liquify against the live render scope, per-context WeakMap counter, block-tag parsing, icon/logo SVG dirs injectable instead of the Ruby's hardcoded node_modules path). Data tables (184 languages, 43 flag mappings, 15 social URL patterns) machine-extracted from the Ruby source, not retyped. Jekyll-compat pack scoped by the real-usage audit — only 6 Jekyll-specific filters are used ANYWHERE across UJM+somiibo (slugify, date_to_xmlschema, jsonify, strip_html, markdownify, push) — plus relative_url/absolute_url/date_to_rfc822/number_of_words and subset where_exp/group_by_exp. Parity pinned against real ruby 4.0.0: `uj_hash` is byte-exact (full-hexdigest BigInt semantics); `uj_commaify`'s decimal grouping diverges DELIBERATELY (Ruby groups decimals too — latent bug; best-implementation-wins). PLUS `@omegajs/config` gained the deferred **`toSiteGlobal(resolvedConfig)`** (`./site-global` export): the site.* audit (site.brand 265×, site.url 161×, site.theme 101× …) showed the resolved omega.json5 shape IS the template shape, so it's an identity mapping + machinery stripped (`targets`/`enabled`) + `url` derived from brand.url + baseurl defaulted; engine-owned keys (site.posts/pages/data/time) stay the SSG's job. Deep doc: `docs/template-kit.md`. Gates: template-kit 41/41 (semantics + Ruby-parity pins + the full adapter surface against REAL liquidjs 10.27.1), config 38 (+3 site-global), BXM 92 re-run (vendored-config flow unaffected), CI gained template-kit + bakeoff-shared suite steps.

The deterministic corpus generator (`npm run generate`, seeded mulberry32, byte-reproducible) emits a content tree statistically identical to somiibo: exact per-year post distribution (1,030 across `_posts/{2017..2026,other,seo}`), word-count quartiles matching the measured min 346 / p25 875 / median 1017 / p75 1797 / max 7522, the exact page-archetype mix (59× platform-bot + 32× solution + 5× package data-frontmatter pages, 6× theme-bracket `.html`, 3× blueprint singles), 20 `_alternatives` docs, `{{ site.* }}` refs inside frontmatter values (the variable-resolver stressor), and `site-data.json` as the candidates' `site.*` source. The JSON5 defaults merge (with the checkpoint-20 consumer-key-preservation fix) and the tolerant `{{ key.path }}` renderer (GitHub's `${{ }}` survives) live in the engine and are exported for direct use. - `@omegajs/devkit` **cli-router slice**: `createCliRouter({ commandsDir, aliases, defaultCommand })` — the framework CLI dispatcher (positional command → alias table → flag-style alias → default; loads `commands/<name>.js`; errors log with the command name and rethrow to the bin), extracted from the drift-identical ×3 copies in UJM/BXM/EM. BXM + EM adopted: each `src/cli.js` is now a thin wrapper owning only its alias table + commands dir (the dotenv load and EM's bin-side `ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE` strip stay framework-side). `Main.config` exposes the resolved dispatch table, so EM's alias-integrity test introspects the installed class (now also covering the default command) instead of regex-scraping the cli.js source — the only test that needed changing. Normalization: EM's error line gains the command name (BXM's more informative variant is the shared standard). BEM's CLI is a DIFFERENT design (colon-style utility commands dispatching stateful command classes) — not duplication; it stays framework-specific. Gates: devkit suite 46 (+10 router tests), BXM 88 (unchanged), EM 751 passing / 5 designed skips (unchanged), pack-smoke 8/8 (vendored router ships in both tarballs; positional + flag-alias dispatch and clean unknown-command exit-1 from scratch installs; `Main.config` introspection from installed dists; both dists self-containment-grep clean).
- `docs/config.md` — the omega.json5 format spec (locations, shape, resolution chain, hard rules, validation) + per-framework migration mapping tables (EM's is live; BEM/UJM/omega-manager rows staged for their flips).
- `@omegajs/config` (private workspace package) — the single `omega.json5` format for every OMEGA project type: `loadConfig(projectDir, target, { defaults })` discovers `config/omega.json5` (or `functions/config/omega.json5` for standalone backends), walks up to the brand root in a brand monorepo, and resolves one agnostic deep-merge chain (`defaults ← brand shared ← brand targets[t] ← app shared ← app targets[t]` — target sections overlay the top level, so any shared key inside a target entry overrides per-surface). Key presence under `targets` = target enabled (`enabled` flag + `getEnabledTargets()`); `hasOmegaConfig()` is the frameworks' dual-read probe; `SHARED_SECTIONS` is the enumeration omega-manager's disperse will use instead of hardcoding. The validator is EM's proven schema engine ported verbatim (required/type/match/enum; findings return as errors so audit can throw while boot warns), with the shared schema seeded from EM's entries, desktop refinements from EM's real schema (per-OS `targets` renamed `platforms`), and backend refinements from the sandbox's real BEM config. Hard fails: secret-shaped keys (`/(secret|privateKey|apiSecret)$/i`) anywhere in a raw file throw before any merge, and the legacy `targets` ARRAY form throws loudly instead of mangling. 34 tests green; CI test job gained the config suite. `toSiteGlobal()` deferred to the Phase-2 A0 slice (needs the real Jekyll `site.*` usage audit); framework dual-read adoption comes next as old-name minors (EM canary is the gate).

The sandbox consumer dogfooded the real `mgr setup` gitignore merge and untracked its copies. CI's self-containment grep widened to match `require.resolve` too.
- `@omegajs/devkit` (private workspace package) — first slice of the shared build-time internals: `logger` (was identical ×4 across UJM/BXM/EM/MAM), `safe-install` (byte-identical ×4), `attach-log-file` (functionally identical ×4, header normalized to `# omega log`). 17 unit tests (node:test).
- Devkit **vendor-on-prepare** mechanism (`packages/devkit/tools/vendor.js`): frameworks require devkit modules by name as a workspace-linked devDependency; each framework's `preparePackage.hooks.after` copies devkit src into `dist/vendor/devkit/` and rewrites the requires to relative paths, so published tarballs stay self-contained while devkit stays private. Guards that hosts declare the runtime deps vendored code needs (chalk, node-powertools).
- `packages/extension` adopted devkit via 3 one-line shims (`src/lib/logger.js`, `src/lib/safe-install.js`, `src/utils/attach-log-file.js`) — suite unchanged at 85 passing; packed tarball verified self-contained.
- `packages/desktop` adopted the same 3 devkit shims (its Electron-specific `logger-lite` stays desktop-owned) — suite unchanged at 751 passing / 5 designed skips; pack-smoke verified (resolves, self-contained, vendored logger runs in a bare consumer, `scripts/sync-nvmrc.js` still ships). Backend adoption deferred until BEM gains its dist layer (harmonization step).
- CI: devkit suite added to the test job; pack-smoke gained a "no raw `@omegajs` requires in shipped dist" self-containment check (the hard gate — prepare-package `after` hooks are non-blocking by design).
- Monorepo skeleton: npm workspaces (`packages/*`, `spikes/*`, `apps/*`), Node pin, base docs (README, CLAUDE.md, PROGRESS.md).
- Changesets (`@changesets/cli`) for independent package versioning.
- Framework packages plain-copied from their (untouched, read-only) source repos: `packages/backend` ← backend-manager@5.11.7, `packages/client` ← web-manager@4.3.4, `packages/extension` ← browser-extension-manager@1.7.3, `packages/desktop` ← electron-manager@1.12.0. Legacy npm names retained until gated cutovers.

### Fixed
- Devkit vendor tool died with `ENAMETOOLONG` on backend-manager's dist: `jetpack.find` follows symlinks during traversal (matching exclusions don't prune the walk), so the host-dist scan recursed forever through the self-test fixture's circular `backend-manager → package root` symlink. The scan now uses a symlink-safe walker that also skips `node_modules` and the vendor output — fixture/dependency code inside dist is not host code and is neither scanned nor rewritten. Regression-tested with a circular-symlink + node_modules fixture (devkit suite 32 passing).
- Sandbox `test:backend` never actually worked from the brand root: `npm run test:offline --workspace=apps/backend` resolved workspaces against the MONOREPO root (sandbox-brand is itself a root workspace and workspace globs don't recurse), so the flag found nothing — the corpus had only ever been run from inside `apps/backend`. Now `cd apps/backend && npm run test:offline`; CI's sandbox job would have failed on first push.
- `packages/client` (web-manager) had **no auth-emulator support** — Firestore had a `FIREBASE_EMULATOR_CONNECT` path but auth never called `connectAuthEmulator`, so signin/signup against an emulated backend silently hit LIVE Firebase Auth. Found by the cross-stack e2e (dogfood catch).
- `packages/client` emulator-mode **account reads hit LIVE Firestore**: the emulator connect lived only in the lazy firestore module, but the auth module reads accounts via `manager.firebaseFirestore` directly — unless page code happened to touch `manager.firestore()` first, account fetches went to production. Both connects now live in `_initializeFirebase()` at instance-creation time (auth :9099, firestore :8080, one gate); the duplicate block in the firestore module was removed. Suite unchanged at 73 passing.
- `packages/backend` rules tests ignored the firestore emulator port from firebase.json — the runner never passed it to the rules client, so every rules test timed out for any consumer on a non-default port (found by the sandbox's first dogfood run). The runner now passes `emulatorPorts.firestore` through.
- `packages/backend` content/post 404 tests hit LIVE GitHub search even in normal (non-extended) test mode — the route 500s without `GH_TOKEN`, failing every token-less consumer. Both suites exercising the route (`test/routes/content/post.js` and the `--legacy`-only `test/functions/general/fetch-post.js`) now skip without a token, using the same static-skip pattern as the mailbox-key tests. Logged (not fixed): BEM doesn't support custom emulator ports at all — `Manager.getApiUrl()`/`getFunctionsUrl()` hardcode `localhost:5002`/`5001` in dev/testing; harmonization candidate.
- Root `.gitignore` comment claimed `packages/*/dist` is tracked — it never was (each package's own `.gitignore` ignores its dist, matching framework-repo convention; prepare-package regenerates dist on install/pack/publish).
- `packages/desktop` package.json `files`: added `scripts/` — the published electron-manager@1.12.0 declares `postinstall: node scripts/sync-nvmrc.js` but never shipped the script, so every FRESH consumer install fails on npm today. Found by the pack→scratch-install smoke; verified fixed (tarball ships the script; postinstall syncs consumer .nvmrc).
- `packages/desktop` test runners (`src/test/runners/electron.js`, `boot.js`): electron now resolved via `require.resolve('electron', { paths: [projectRoot] })` instead of hardcoded `<root>/node_modules/electron`, so hoisted npm-workspace installs are found. Behavior unchanged for standalone consumers. Result: desktop suite 751 passing (was 299 with 47 hoisting skips).
